# Event tracking gaps — research consolidation

**Produced by:** analytics-studio, fan-out run `wf_c84b71a1-52f`, 2026-10-11 (consolidated 2026-10-12).  
**Scope asked for:** the next tracking gaps for michi-maker + tcgscan, pickleague and doggle; the new push notification features; and how to consolidate and extend the michi/tcgscan daily email.  
**Audience:** the owner, and an agent session rooted at any of the four workspaces.

## How to read this

Nine lanes each read one surface and returned candidate gaps with file:line evidence. Every candidate was then handed to three independent skeptics:

- **already-emitted** — tried to refute it from the app code: is there a call site that already covers this?
- **ground-truth** — tried to refute it from the migrations: does a product table already answer the question, making an event the wrong fix?
- **worth-it** — tried to refute it on value and compliance: would the owner act on it; are props ids and numbers only; is every outcome paired with its impression so a zero is unambiguous?

A gap **SURVIVED** when at least two of three said it is real. The verifiers' corrections are part of each finding — several survivors were narrowed or reclassified (app gap → studio lane) on the way through.

**What did not finish, stated plainly:**

- The **michi-maker product lane never returned.** Its finder ran 3h45m and 40 tool calls without producing output and was stopped. michi-maker's own surfaces (contest, What's New, Discover, canvas editor, undo/redo, Theme Search, share links, the price change) are therefore covered only where the digest lane and the tcgscan symmetry lane touched them. **This is the largest hole in this document.**
- **80 verifier agents died inside one second** on a transient API failure. The pipeline recorded them as failed. As a result the **tcgscan**, **studio** and **digest** lanes carry finder evidence only — marked UNVERIFIED below. The second run that would have retried them was stopped at the owner's request to wrap up.

Per the house rule, an UNVERIFIED item is not a zero and not a yes: it is a lead with a citation that has not yet been checked against the code by a second reader.

## The shortlist — every verified survivor, worst first

- **[blocking]** `pickleague_product_blind_open` (pickleague) — Pickleague still emits nothing about the product; the registered fix landed in doggle only
- **[blocking]** `pickleague_push_action_blind` (pickleague) — Lock-screen action buttons emit nothing: no press, no category, no action id, no outcome, no outbox id
- **[blocking]** `pickleague_push_blind` (pickleague) — Pickleague emits nothing about push: no permission ask/result, no token registration, no opens, although every kit hook is available at its pin
- **[high]** `doggle_oauth_login_untracked` (doggle) — OAuth sign-in (Google/Apple) emits neither account.created NOR auth.login - the registered gap understates it
- **[high]** `doggle_plus_gates_blind` (doggle) — Doggle+ cap gates and the paywall emit nothing about being hit, dismissed, or converted
- **[high]** `doggle_push_dead_category_prefixes` (doggle) — Friend-request and DM pushes never get their action buttons: the dispatcher's prefix map does not match the keys the outbox writes, so the friend_accept/decline and dm_reply kinds are structural zeros
- **[high]** `doggle_push_open_unjoinable` (doggle) — push.opened carries only the action kind: the outbox id and alert kind in the payload are dropped, so an open cannot be joined to its send or to its outcome
- **[high]** `doggle_push_outbox_lane` (studio) — The studio reads no push sends, so push.opened has no denominator and sends cannot be counted by kind
- **[high]** `doggle_search_blind` (doggle) — Home and Discover search emit nothing: no query count, no result count, no no-results rate
- **[high]** `foundation_action_outcome_blind` (foundation) — runNotificationAction resolves success / queued / timed_out / failed and tells only the lock screen, so a lock-screen answer that never saved is invisible to analytics
- **[high]** `foundation_local_notice_blind` (foundation) — scheduleLocalNotification and postLocalNotice are sends that touch no outbox and emit nothing, so a local reminder has no denominator anywhere
- **[high]** `foundation_push_opened_no_join` (foundation) — The kit's onOpened hook drops outboxId, requestId and category before analytics, so an open can never be joined to the send that caused it
- **[high]** `pickleague_appstore_click_unregistered` (pickleague) — appstore.click is emitted but not in the taxonomy, has never fired, and the App Store QR is unobservable by design
- **[high]** `pickleague_checkin_blind` (pickleague) — Court check-in emits nothing about the attempt, the refusal or the dismissal; only successes land (in a table no lane reads)
- **[high]** `pickleague_match_confirm_via_blind` (pickleague) — Match confirmation from the push button and from the app write the same row; neither emits, and the push-action path cannot even reach the emitter
- **[high]** `pickleague_play_session_source_blind` (pickleague) — Play sessions record start/end but nothing says what started them or what the island was used for
- **[high]** `pickleague_push_open_blind` (pickleague) — A tapped push emits nothing and does not mark the notification read, so opens are invisible everywhere
- **[high]** `pickleague_push_optin_blind` (pickleague) — Push opt-in emits nothing: no permission ask, no permission result, no token registered
- **[high]** `pickleague_push_outbox_unread` (studio) — The studio reads nothing from push_outbox / push_deliveries / push_tokens, so every push rate above has no denominator
- **[high]** `studio_outbox_lane_missing` (studio) — No studio lane reads any outbox or token table, although the schema already answers sends, failures, expiries, retries, per-device delivery and receipts
- **[medium]** `doggle_kit_pin_drift_kind_vocab` (doggle) — Doggle at 1.25.0 hand-rolls what 1.32-1.35 hoisted, and its push.opened kind vocabulary differs from the kit's onOpened contract, so the two apps' opens will not share one shape when pickleague wires the hook
- **[medium]** `doggle_local_notice_blind` (doggle) — Local notifications emit nothing when scheduled or cancelled, and their taps are counted as push.opened indistinguishable from server pushes
- **[medium]** `doggle_notification_prefs_untracked` (doggle) — Notification preference changes, radius changes and test pushes emit nothing, and the preferences table keeps no history
- **[medium]** `doggle_onboarding_permission_asks_mislabeled` (doggle) — Onboarding's permission asks are recorded as 'settings' (push) or not at all (location); PromptSurface 'onboarding' has no caller
- **[medium]** `doggle_onboarding_steps_blind` (doggle) — The 9-step onboarding emits nothing per step; only start (account.created) and finish (profiles.onboarded) exist
- **[medium]** `doggle_ota_banner_blind` (doggle) — The OTA 'restart to update' banner emits nothing when shown, restarted or dismissed, and no OTA id is recorded anywhere
- **[medium]** `doggle_place_page_funnel_blind` (doggle) — Place pages record only last_visited_at per person; view counts, anonymous views and view-to-action per place are invisible
- **[medium]** `doggle_prompt_suppression_invisible` (doggle) — Nearby check-in prompts that the server refuses (cooldown, already checked in) or the client cannot deliver are recorded nowhere, so the prompt has no attempt count
- **[medium]** `doggle_push_surface_mislabeled` (doggle) — Onboarding's push ask is recorded with surface 'settings', so the per-surface grant rate the events exist for is blended
- **[medium]** `foundation_outbox_suppressed_as_sent` (foundation) — The kit dispatcher marks preference-suppressed rows status='sent' and never persists the resolved category, so the outbox overstates sends and cannot group by button set
- **[medium]** `pickleague_build_distribution_unread` (pickleague) — app_build and installed_at now ride on every native session row and no studio lane reads them; the update banner and hard gate emit nothing
- **[medium]** `pickleague_court_alert_permission_blind` (pickleague) — The Always-location ask behind court arrival alerts emits nothing, and the toggle itself lives only in AsyncStorage
- **[medium]** `pickleague_event_vote_via_blind` (pickleague) — Event RSVP/votes from the push buttons and from the screen are the same insert; failures and the in-app impression are not recorded
- **[medium]** `pickleague_guest_upgrade_untracked` (pickleague) — A guest-pass holder becoming a full account emits nothing; account.created only knows email signups
- **[medium]** `pickleague_local_notice_blind` (pickleague) — Local notices (check-in reminder, play-start, court arrival) have no impression anywhere, so their buttons have no denominator
- **[medium]** `pickleague_push_pref_changes_blind` (pickleague) — Category mutes emit nothing and the preference blob keeps no history, so suppression is only visible as outbox error text
- **[medium]** `pickleague_rating_prompt_blind` (pickleague) — The store-rating ask discards the only signal the OS gives back
- **[low]** `doggle_email_alert_click_blind` (doggle) — Alert emails are a sent ledger with no click signal: the web ?alert= arrival navigates and records nothing, and the link carries no outbox id
- **[low]** `doggle_events_join_lane_gap` (doggle) — Meetup RSVPs and event invites are fully in product tables the studio never reads; the app-side gap is only the view denominator
- **[low]** `doggle_geofence_prompt_blind` (doggle) — Favorite-geofence enter/exit and the local 'you are passing X' prompt emit nothing; taps on it have no shown denominator
- **[low]** `doggle_push_opened_double_count` (doggle) — Production builds can record one tap as two push.opened rows and run the action twice, because the live listener and the launch replay both feed the handler with no dedupe
- **[low]** `doggle_walk_start_sheet_denominator` (doggle) — The walk start sheet emits nothing when opened or closed without starting, so walk.start has no intent denominator and the no-dogs dead end is invisible
- **[low]** `foundation_live_session_blind` (foundation) — createLiveSession and useResumeLiveSession expose no analytics observer, so how a live session was started (island, screen, lock-screen notice) and how often a persisted one is reconciled away is invisible
- **[low]** `foundation_push_onopened_no_payload` (foundation) — The kit's onOpened analytics hook passes only a kind string, so an open cannot be attributed to its outbox row through the hook the kit offers
- **[low]** `pickleague_is_read_not_seen` (pickleague) — notifications.is_read means 'tapped in the in-app list', not 'seen', because a push tap never marks the row read
- **[low]** `pickleague_signup_claim_stage_structural` (pickleague) — The visitor funnel's 'Signed in on that visit' stage cannot be reached by an email signup

Unverified leads the owner asked about directly (digest, studio, tcgscan) are in sections 4–6 and are **not** on this list only because their verifiers failed, not because they were judged weak.

## 1. Push notifications — doggle, pickleague and the shared foundation kit

Three lanes read the two apps' push code and the expo-foundation kit they are converging on. These are the gaps with the fullest verification: every one was judged by all three lenses.

### 1. [BLOCKING] `pickleague_push_action_blind` — Lock-screen action buttons emit nothing: no press, no category, no action id, no outcome, no outbox id
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Of the match-confirm pushes sent inside the 1-hour window, how many were answered from the lock screen vs in the app vs lapsed (expire_pending_matches deletes the row), and how often does a button press end in timed_out / failed / needs_app rather than success?

**Evidence:** Surface: notificationCatalog.data.ts:13-77 (10 categories, 13 action ids); send-push attaches categoryId at functions/send-push/index.ts:94-166 and the kit stamps data.outboxId at functions/_kit/outboxDispatch.ts:156. Press path: push.ts:198-203 wireNotificationResponses({isAction, onAction: handleNotificationAction, onTap, storageKey}) with no onOpened; notificationActions.ts:189-200 handleNotificationAction -> runNotificationAction; kit actions.ts:64-78 turns success/queued/TimeoutError/mapError into one postLocalNotice and returns void. grep of mobile/src for track( finds only GetTheAppButton.tsx:63, GuestJoinScreen.tsx:114, LoginScreen.tsx:48, RegisterScreen.tsx:93. router.ts:54-61 shows the outbox id is in hand (idempotencyKey = outboxId:action) and used only for dedupe. Resulting writes have no origin column: event_slot_votes/event_declines (notificationActions.ts:113,:121), confirm_match (:132), drill_requests.responded_at (migration_push_actions_drill_tournament.sql:48-54), drill_session_rsvps (:109-111).

**Proposal:** Converge on the existing doggle name: push.opened { kind: 'open' | <action id>, category: <category id>, outbox_id: <uuid>, notification_id: <uuid> } emitted from onAction/onTap in push.ts (parsed.data carries outboxId, notification_id, type) - action ids and category ids are fixed enums from CATALOG, never title text. NEW push.action_result { action, category, outbox_id, outcome: success | queued | timed_out | failed | needs_app | stale } emitted by a wrapper around runNotificationAction in notificationActions.ts:189-200 (map NeedsApp -> needs_app, the /no longer pending|expired|not found|already/ branch of mapError :87 -> stale, TimeoutError -> timed_out; never the exception message). Denominator: push_outbox rows with status='sent' and error is null for that category, read by the studio (see pickleague_push_outbox_unread).

**Ground truth already available:** push_outbox (sends, denominator) joined to matches.team1_confirmed_by/team2_confirmed_by, drill_requests.status, drill_session_rsvps, event_slot_votes/event_declines (outcomes) - but none of those says lock-screen vs in-app, so the attribution itself is NOT in any table.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the proposal's cost: the 'push.opened' half is a one-line change because the kit already exposes the hook — pass `onOpened: (kind) => track('push.opened', { kind, ... })` in push.ts:198-203 (router.ts:39/:102 already passes the action id or 'open'); only `push.action_result` needs new code (a wrapper around runNotificationAction at notificationActions.ts:189-200, or an `onResult` option added to the kit's RunNotificationActionOptions at actions.ts:40-56 so doggle gets it symmetrically). Note that router.ts:102 fires onOpened with only `kind`; category/outbox_id/notification_id must be read from parsed.data inside the pickleague callback, and the kit's onOpened signature would need `(kind, parsed)` to carry them without re-parsing. Also register push.opened for pickleague in events.json:377-382 (currently doggle-only).
- *ground-truth:* Narrow groundTruth: the 'lapsed' leg should NOT be read from matches (expire_pending_matches deletes the row, migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:284-296) but from push_outbox.status='expired' where entity_type='match' (migration_push_outbox.sql:116-124, 235-236); likewise the denominator for all categories is push_outbox (status, sent_at, category, entity_type). Add to the proposal: (a) wire the existing kit hook onOpened in push.ts:198-203 rather than a new wrapper for push.opened; (b) push.action_result 'stale' mapping also covers confirm_match raising 'Match is no longer pending' / 'Confirmation window has expired' (migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:249-254) and respond_drill_request returning 'already' / raising 'Request is no longer pending' (migration_push_actions_drill_tournament.sql:41-42); (c) the studio lane must snapshot push_outbox daily because private.push_prune deletes sent/expired/failed rows older than 30 days (migration_push_outbox.sql:258-263). Severity stays blocking for the match_confirm category only (a missed confirm silently deletes a real match); other categories are high.
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap, severity blocking, title as stated. Replace the proposal with: CLIENT (pickleague mobile/src/lib/push.ts + notificationActions.ts): emit push.opened { kind: 'open' | <action id>, source: 'remote' | 'local', category?: <catalog category id>, outbox_id?: uuid, notification_id?: uuid } from wrappers around onTap/onAction in push.ts:198-203 (parsed.data has outboxId and notification_id; `kind` stays required so doggle's { kind } rows remain a valid subset of the same event). NEW push.action_result { action, source, category?, outbox_id?, outcome: success | queued | timed_out | failed | needs_app | signed_out | stale }. Outcome cannot be derived from a wrapper around runNotificationAction because it returns void (kit actions.ts:64-78) and TimeoutError is thrown inside its withTimeout, not inside fn: add an `onOutcome?: (o) => void` option to RunNotificationActionOptions in expo-foundation/src/platform/push/actions.ts:40-54, called at :72 (success|queued), :74 (timed_out), :75 (mapError hit -> the app's classifier; else failed); pickleague maps NeedsApp from requireUser (:70-74) -> signed_out, NeedsApp from needs() (:77-80) and /Pick a time/ (:90) -> needs_app, the /no longer pending|expired|not found|Invite already/ branch (:87) -> stale. HEADLESS FLUSH: these two events must NOT go through the pre-bootstrap buffer (analytics.ts:553-567); in the lock-screen path resolve uid via currentUserId (already done in requireUser) and call resetSessionUser({id}) or emit() directly before tracking, otherwise presses that never open the app are lost. CATEGORY ON THE WIRE: send-push must add `categoryId` to data as well as the message (functions/_kit/outboxDispatch.ts:156: data = {..., categoryId: category}), and the three local schedulers must put categoryId into spec.data too (courtCheckin.ts:86-91, courtGeofence.ts:46-47, playSession.ts:140-145); until then emit category only when present, never inferred from action id or title. DENOMINATOR (server + studio lane, not an app emitter): record the resolved button category per send - add push_outbox.action_category text (or a column on push_deliveries) written in the same update that sets status='sent' - because push_outbox.category is the pref key (migration_push_outbox.sql:34). Remote denominator = push_outbox rows status='sent' and error is null grouped by action_category; outcome truth = matches.team1/team2_confirmed_by (lapsed = outbox row whose entity_id no longer exists in matches), drill_requests.status, drill_session_rsvps, event_slot_votes/event_declines. LOCAL denominator: emit push.local_scheduled { category } at each scheduleLocalNotification/postLocalNotice call site and push.local_cancelled { category } at cancelLocalNotification, and read local presses (source='local') against scheduled minus cancelled - scheduled is not shown, state that caveat inline. Register push.opened for apps [doggle, pickleague] and the new names push.action_result, push.local_scheduled, push.local_cancelled for pickleague in config/events.json.

<details><summary>Lens verdicts</summary>

- **already-emitted** — real (high): Looked for coverage and found none. (1) Emitters: pickleague/mobile/src has exactly four track() call sites — GuestJoinScreen.tsx:114, GetTheAppButton.tsx:63, LoginScreen.tsx:48, RegisterScreen.tsx:93 — none on the push surface; mobile/src/lib/push.ts (imports at :14-33) and mobile/src/lib/notificationActions.ts (imports at :22-30) import nothing from analytics.ts, and analytics.ts itself has no push/notification reference (only `pending.push` at :559). (2) Surface exists as claimed: push.ts:193-203 setupNotificationTapHandling calls wireNotificationResponses({isAction, onAction: handleNotificationAction, onTap, storageKey}) with NO onOpened; notificationActions.ts:189-200 handleNotificatio…
- **ground-truth** — real (high): No product table records WHERE a push response was answered, and the match leg cannot even be reconstructed after the fact. Denominator exists: push_outbox (migration_push_outbox.sql:28-48) has recipient_id, kind, category, entity_type, entity_id, status in (pending/sending/sent/failed/expired), sent_at, expires_at; expires_at for 'match' = matches.confirm_deadline (:116-124) and the drain flips lapsed rows to 'expired' (:235-236), so sent-vs-lapsed per category IS answerable from push_outbox. Outcomes exist but carry no origin: matches.team1_confirmed_by/team2_confirmed_by are bare uuids with no confirmed_at and no source column (migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:23-24; confirm_match writes…
- **worth-it** — real (high): The gap stands; the evidence checks out against the code. (1) VALUE: match_confirm is a 1-hour action whose lapse DELETES the match (pickleague/supabase/migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:5,284-292; migration_match_confirm_notify_category.sql:9), so lock-screen vs in-app vs lapsed decides whether to keep the button, lengthen the deadline or resend - not vanity. (2) COMPLIANCE: proposed props are fixed enums and uuids; NoticeCopy bodies/exception messages are never emitted. Emitter absence confirmed: mobile/src/lib/push.ts:198-203 passes isAction/onAction/onTap/storageKey and NOT the kit's analytics hook onOpened (expo-foundation/src/platform/push/router.ts:38-39, invoked at :100-105); the kit…

### 2. [BLOCKING] `pickleague_push_blind` — Pickleague emits nothing about push: no permission ask/result, no token registration, no opens, although every kit hook is available at its pin
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Of the people who turned push on in Settings, how many were asked, how many granted, how many devices are actually reachable, and how many of the ~10 notification kinds ever get tapped or answered from the lock screen?

**Evidence:** Surface exists: pickleague/mobile/src/lib/push.ts:61-87 createAuthBoundPushTokens, :198-203 wireNotificationResponses, :111-130 enablePushNotifications, SettingsScreen.tsx:127-150 togglePush; 10 server categories in supabase/functions/send-push/catalog.json; notificationActions.ts:103-181 eight lock-screen handlers. No emitter: push.ts:61-87 passes no onRegistered; push.ts:40 REGISTRATION = { androidChannelId, androidChannelName } only, so no onPermissionAsked/onPermissionResult/onTokenRegistered reach registerForPushNotificationsAsync (pushNotifications.ts:65-72); push.ts:198-203 passes no onOpened (router.ts:39); analytics.ts has only track('session.start') :328 and track('page.view') :587; grep "'push\." over pickleague/mobile/src returns nothing. Kit hooks exist at the pinned version: authBound.ts:25-26 (1.35+), router.ts:38-39, pushNotifications.ts:62-72; pickleague installs 1.36.0 (mobile/package.json:15).

**Proposal:** Wire the four doggle names verbatim through the kit hooks so the studio reads one shape: push.permission_requested { surface } from registrationOptions.onPermissionAsked (surface enum: 'settings' | 'app'; pass per-call registrationOptions to pushTokens.sync/enable for the settings path); push.permission_result { result, reason } from onPermissionResult (both already fixed enums in pushNotifications.ts:67-70); push.token_registered { platform } from createAuthBoundPushTokens.onRegistered (authBound.ts:26) and after the upsert in enablePushNotifications; push.opened { kind } from wireNotificationResponses.onOpened (router.ts:39). Pairings: permission_requested is the denominator of permission_result; push_tokens row count per platform is the ground-truth denominator of token_registered; push_outbox status='sent' rows are the denominator of push.opened (see foundation_push_opened_no_join for the id that makes that a join). Add "pickleague" to the apps list of the four push.* entries in config/events.json:356-382.

**Ground truth already available:** push_tokens (migration_push_notifications.sql:24-31) answers reachable devices now; push_outbox (migration_push_outbox.sql:28-47) answers sends. Neither answers asks, denials, or opens.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowings, neither changes the verdict. (a) The evidence line "analytics.ts has only track('session.start') and track('page.view')" undercounts: pickleague also emits auth.login, account.created and appstore.click from screens/components (GuestJoinScreen.tsx:114, LoginScreen.tsx:48, RegisterScreen.tsx:93, GetTheAppButton.tsx:63) - still nothing about push. (b) The proposal's settings path should note pickleague does not call pushTokens.enable; SettingsScreen.tsx:133 -> push.ts:116 calls registerForPushNotificationsAsync(REGISTRATION) directly, so the surface:'settings' hooks go into that REGISTRATION spread at push.ts:116 (and push.ts:127-129 after the upsert for token_registered), while the auth-bound path (push.ts:61-87) gets surface:'app' plus onRegistered. Also note that doggle_product_blind (config/events.json:1226) is titled "Doggle and Pickleague" but its landed fix covers doggle only; this pickleague push gap should be registered separately rather than folded into that entry.
- *ground-truth:* Narrow the question and the proposal to what no table answers. Ground truth already covers two of the four parts and the studio should read them instead of events: (a) 'turned push on in Settings' = user_preferences where prefs->>'pushEnabled'='true' (migration_add_user_preferences.sql:12-15; current state, no toggle history); (b) 'devices reachable' = push_tokens count by platform (migration_push_notifications.sql:24-31), plus push_deliveries status 'ok'/'error' and receipt_checked_at (migration_push_outbox.sql:55-64) for delivered-vs-bounced per device, which the gap omitted. The app-instrumentation gap is asks, denials and opens: push.permission_requested { surface } and push.permission_result { result, reason } from pushNotifications.ts:65-72 via a per-call registrationOptions on the Settings path (push.ts:111-130) and the auth-bound path (push.ts:61-87), and push.opened { kind } from wireNotificationResponses.onOpened (router.ts:39,102; kind is the action string or 'open'). push.token_registered is optional (push_tokens.created_at already timestamps it); if kept, its denominator is push_tokens rows per platform. Denominator for push.opened is push_outbox status='sent' (migration_push_outbox.sql:40-41,47), joined on data.outboxId which send-push already puts on every push (index.ts:13). Also note: the lock-screen action RPCs (respond_drill_request, rsvp_drill_session, match confirm) record outcomes but not source, so push.opened { kind: <action> } is the only way to attribute an answer to the lock screen. Add "pickleague" to the apps arrays of the push.* entries at config/events.json:356-382.
- *worth-it:* Title: 'Pickleague push is blind: the pickleague half of doggle_product_blind never landed - no permission ask/result, no token registration, no opens, though every kit hook is at the pinned 1.36.0'. Register as a split of doggle_product_blind (or amend its status note: landed for doggle, open for pickleague), not as an unrelated gap. Proposal changes: (1) push.permission_requested { surface: 'settings' | 'app' } and push.permission_result { result: 'granted'|'denied'|'unavailable', reason?: 'unsupported_platform'|'no_module'|'threw'|'no_token' } via registrationOptions: static REGISTRATION gets surface 'app' (authBound sync on a new device with pushEnabled already true); the Settings path passes per-call options with surface 'settings'. Report rule: result rows = requested rows + 'unavailable' rows, because the kit never fires a result when permission was already granted (pushNotifications.ts:111-116). (2) push.token_registered { platform } from ONE emitter only: replace push.ts:114-128 (manual requestPushToken + upsertToken + void pushTokens.sync()) with `pushTokens.enable({ registrationOptions: { ...REGISTRATION, onPermissionAsked, onPermissionResult } })` (authBound.ts:51) so createAuthBoundPushTokens.onRegistered (authBound.ts:26) is the single source; do not also emit after the upsert, and do not use the kit's onTokenRegistered (pushNotifications.ts:143 fires on every sync even when unchanged). Denominator: push_tokens rows per platform (ground truth) and permission_result='granted' (attempt). The kit's onOpened receives only the action string, so emit from the onTap wrapper (push.ts:201, data in hand) and inside handleNotificationAction (parsed.data), not from onOpened. ### 3. [HIGH] `doggle_push_dead_category_prefixes` — Friend-request and DM pushes never get their action buttons: the dispatcher's prefix map does not match the keys the outbox writes, so the friend_accept/decline and dm_reply kinds are structural zeros
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Do people accept friend requests or reply to DMs from the lock screen, and does the button lift acceptance/reply rate over a plain tap?

**Evidence:** CATEGORY_BY_PREFIX maps 'friend_invite' -> doggle.friend and 'direct_message' -> doggle.dm (supabase/functions/dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:211,215); categoryForAlertKey falls through to undefined = no buttons (218-226). Outbox keys actually written: friend requests/accepts go through _enqueue_push, key 'fr:'||gen_random_uuid() (db/migrations/0105_friend_invite_alerts.sql:19; callers 0105:49-94, 0109:34-81, 0147:392-412); DMs write 'dm:'||message id (0081_direct_messages.sql:293, 0297). grep -rn "'friend_invite" db/migrations -> only home-feed jsonb keys (0105:254, 0109:253), never an outbox row. next-build counts the resulting presses as push.opened kind friend_accept|friend_decline|dm_reply (next-build PushNotificationContext.tsx:219-225) and Phase 0b/6a build DM_REPLY idempotency on them (docs/foundation/port-live-features-work-order.md Phase 0b, 6a step 5). The buttons can never be shown, so those kinds will read 0 forever with no impression behind them.

**Proposal:** Product fix first, in the dispatcher: map the prefixes the outbox writes ('fr:' -> doggle.friend only once _enqueue_push writes a kind, 'dm:' -> doggle.dm), or make _enqueue_push write '<kind>:<id>' keys (see doggle_push_outbox_lane). Analytics: the impression for an action button is the outbox row whose prefix maps to a category; the push lane should print sends-with-category by category next to push.opened by kind, and must state inline that friend/dm categories attached to zero sends until the fix deploys, so the zero reads as 'never offered' not 'never pressed'. No new event name needed.

**Ground truth already available:** public.alert_notification_outbox alert_key prefix (what category, if any, each send carried)

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow two details. (a) The dispatcher with CATEGORY_BY_PREFIX is on main too, not next-build only (main commit bedffac; docs/foundation/port-live-features-work-order.md:9 is stale on this). (b) On main the app registers only CHECKIN_NOTIFICATION_CATEGORY and CHECKIN_EXTEND_CATEGORY (app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:411,422) and its push.opened kinds are dismiss|checkin|extend|checkout|open (197-207); friend_accept/friend_decline/dm_reply exist only on next-build. So today the dead prefixes cost nothing visible; the structural zero appears the moment the next-build binary ships. Also state that the fix is two-sided: _enqueue_push is shared by friend, event_join (0231, 0233), plan join (0232), referral (0240) and points (0242) callers, so a '<kind>:<id>' key needs a kind parameter, not a blanket 'fr:' -> doggle.friend mapping (that would put Accept/Decline on referral and points pushes).
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap; tighten two points. (a) Do NOT map 'fr:' -> doggle.friend, even as a stopgap: _enqueue_push is the generic push helper and the same 'fr:' key is written for group invites (0225_group_friend_invites.sql:155), event joins (0231, 0232, 0233), referral and daily-bonus pushes (0240, 0242), so a wholesale 'fr:' mapping would put Accept/Decline buttons on a group invite or a points notice. The fix is a p_kind parameter on _enqueue_push writing '<kind>:<entity id>' ('friend_invite:'||fr.id, 'friend_accepted:'||fr.id, 'group_invite:'||id, ...) and a dispatcher map on those prefixes plus 'dm:' -> doggle.dm; drop the title's phrase 'map the prefixes the outbox writes'. (b) State the branch split inline in the lane: on main the device registers only doggle.checkin and doggle.checkin.extend (app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:411,422) and PUSH_KIND has no friend/dm entries, so those kinds are not merely zero, they are unemitted until the next-build binary ships; after the dispatcher fix the impression still requires that binary. Denominator should count outbox rows with status='sent' whose prefix maps to a category (a 'failed' row never offered a button), grouped by category id from catalog.json, printed next to push.opened by kind; the inline note reads 'friend/dm categories attached to 0 sent rows before <fix date>; zero = never offered'.

### 4. [HIGH] `doggle_push_open_unjoinable` — push.opened carries only the action kind: the outbox id and alert kind in the payload are dropped, so an open cannot be joined to its send or to its outcome
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Of the nearby/expiry prompts we sent, which kind gets tapped, which button, and how many of those taps became a check-in or an extend? Did the push that asked produce the visit?

**Evidence:** Dispatcher puts data {deepLink, outboxId, alertKey} on every push (supabase/functions/dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:248). main client never reads it: grep -rn outboxId app/src -> no matches; handleNotificationResponse emits trackPushOpened(kind) only (app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:202-212; analytics.ts:666-668 props {kind}). next-build: the foundation router builds idempotencyKey from data.outboxId (expo-foundation src/platform/push/router.ts:54,60) and then calls onOpened(action) with the id discarded (router.ts:102); doggle maps it to PUSH_KIND[kind] (next-build PushNotificationContext.tsx:339). AppState.currentState is already read for behaviour (main :125) but not recorded, so cold/background vs in-app opens are one number. Outcome side: a check-in from the CHECK_IN button goes through checkInManyOrQueue -> trackCheckin('manual', n, true) (app/src/lib/checkinQueue.ts:199) or dogPlaceSocial.ts:448/595 with source 'manual', identical to an in-app tap; extend/checkout actions emit no checkin.* at all from the notification path (PushNotificationContext.tsx:156-183, 228-237). The hardening doc names dedupe as the purpose of outboxId (docs/push-hardening.md 'Delivery is at-least-once') and nothing names attribution.

**Proposal:** Extend push.opened props (same event, no new name): {kind, outbox_id: uuid|null (from data.outboxId; null = local notice), alert_kind: enum from split_part(data.alertKey,':',1) as in doggle_push_outbox_lane, origin: remote|local, app_state: active|background|inactive|unknown (AppState.currentState at handler time)}. Foundation: change onOpened?(kind) to onOpened?(kind, parsed) so data reaches the hook (router.ts:39,102) before the kit freezes for Pickleague 7b. Outcome pairing: add optional outbox_id to checkin.create and checkin.out (trackCheckin/trackCheckout, analytics.ts:732-738) and thread it from the action handlers (PushNotificationContext.tsx:124-152 -> checkInManyOrQueue; 156-183 extendOrQueue; 228-237 checkout) so a visit joins to the push that asked; add checkin.extend {outbox_id|null, queued} because the extend action has no event today. Impression = outbox row (sent_at), denominator for both the open and the outcome.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *ground-truth:* Gap stands as an app-instrumentation gap. Two refinements: (a) the outbox IS the correct impression denominator and the studio lane reading alert_notification_outbox (recipient_id, alert_key, channel='push', status, sent_at) should be stated as the paired half of this gap rather than a separate finding; (b) an equivalent server-side fix exists - an opened_at/action column on alert_notification_outbox written by a small RPC the client calls with data.outboxId - but it needs the same client change (stop dropping outboxId in PushNotificationContext.tsx:202 and router.ts:102), so either way the client-side change is the blocking item. checkin.extend remains a genuine new-event need because no table holds one row per extension (0329:22 mutates expires_at in place).
- *worth-it:* Severity: medium (ground-truth time-join covers the remote half approximately today). Keep push.opened, extend props to {kind: open|dismiss|checkin|extend|checkout, origin: remote|local, outbox_id: uuid|null (data.outboxId; null when origin=local), alert_kind: nearby_checkin|expiry_checkout|auto_checkin|friend_invite|event_reminder|meetup_followup|planned_visit|direct_message|extend_nudge|nearby_local|other (CLIENT ALLOWLIST on the prefix before the first ':' of data.alertKey for remote; 'extend_nudge' when data.kind==='checkin-extend', 'nearby_local' for favoriteProximity's local prompt; never pass the split string through), app_state: active|background|inactive|unknown}. Pair the LOCAL impressions: add push.scheduled {kind: extend_nudge|nearby_local} at checkinReminders.ts scheduleCheckinReminder (after the past-time guard, :37) and favoriteProximity.ts scheduleLocalPrompt (:17), and push.cancelled {kind: extend_nudge} at cancelCheckinReminder (:54). Remote impression stays the outbox row (status='sent', channel='push'); the studio lane joins outbox.id = push.opened.outbox_id and reports sent / opened / outcome per alert_kind. Outcome pairing: add optional origin: notification|app and outbox_id: uuid|null to checkin.create and checkin.out (trackCheckin/trackCheckout analytics.ts:732-738), threaded from PushNotificationContext.tsx:124-152 and :228-237; fix the checkout double-emit at :233-234 so one tap yields one checkin.out. Add checkin.extend {origin: notification|app, queued: bool, result: extended|closed|queued} emitted from extendOrQueue (checkinQueue.ts:239-250) so both WalkIsland.tsx:213 and the notification path (:158) are covered; drop outbox_id from checkin.extend because the extend nudge never has an outbox row. Foundation: change onOpened?(kind) to onOpened?(kind, parsed) at router.ts:39/:102 (next-build only; main does not use the router). Studio lane (separate, no app change): time-join alert_notification_outbox nearby_checkin sends to dog_place_checkins by recipient_id + place id + created_at within 15 min as the interim push->visit ceiling.

### 5. [HIGH] `doggle_push_outbox_lane` — The studio reads no push sends, so push.opened has no denominator and sends cannot be counted by kind
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** studio

**Question it answers:** How many pushes did we send yesterday, of which kind, how many failed and why, how many devices are reachable, and what share of sends were ever tapped?

**Evidence:** Sends ledger exists: public.alert_notification_outbox db/migrations/0060_alert_notifications.sql:54-67 (+0403: status sending, claimed_at, attempts; sent_at/error per row). Dispatcher marks every row sent or failed (index.ts:340-352, 422-425). Studio: grep -rn alert_notification_outbox analytics-studio/scripts analytics-studio/config -> only the prose note at config/events.json:1232; no lane, no report. Kind is only derivable from alert_key prefix, and _enqueue_push (0105_friend_invite_alerts.sql:19, still the live definition; 0403:287 only revokes it) writes 'fr:'||gen_random_uuid() for friend requests, event/group invites, event/plan joins, referral nudges and points bonuses alike (0105:49-94, 0120:96, 0225:155, 0231:22,68, 0232:44, 0233:115,160, 0240:160,196, 0242:101), so ~8 push triggers collapse into one opaque bucket. Test pushes write 'test:' keys (0062:32-40) and must be excluded.

**Proposal:** Studio lane (scripts/push.mjs, reports/push.html), app repo change optional. Read alert_notification_outbox per Pacific day ({{DAY:created_at}}), split channel x kind where kind = split_part(alert_key, ':', 1) mapped: nearby_checkin, expiry_checkout, auto_checkin, auto_checkout, dog_news, place_comment, friend_checkin, planned_visit, event_reminder, meetup_followup, dm, fr (opaque: _enqueue_push family), test (excluded, stated inline). Status sent/failed/pending/sending plus error class enum from the error text (no_tokens | no_email | expo_ticket | resend | stuck | other). Exclusions via exclusionCte on recipient_id. Daily snapshot of count(user_push_tokens) by platform as the reachable-device line. Pair with push.opened counts from the events lane in the same report; until push.opened carries outbox_id (see doggle_push_open_unjoinable) the pairing is rate-by-day only, and the report must say so. App-side follow-up so the 'fr' bucket splits: give _enqueue_push a p_kind arg and write '<kind>:<id>' keys (friend_request, friend_accepted, event_invite, group_invite, event_join, plan_join, referral_nudge, points_bonus).

**Ground truth already available:** public.alert_notification_outbox (recipient_id, channel, alert_key, status, error, created_at, sent_at, attempts); public.user_push_tokens (profile_id, platform, created_at)

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowings. (1) The proposal's kind map is incomplete. Outbox alert_key prefixes actually written (grep "'<x>:' ||" over db/migrations): nearby_checkin, expiry_checkout, auto_checkin, auto_checkout, dog_news, place_comment, friend_checkin, planned_visit, meetup_followup, dm, fr, test PLUS gift (0101_gift_notifications.sql:50-54), vibe (0103_vibes.sql:73-76, 0288_premium_vibe_experience.sql:89-92), friend_invite, friend_accepted, meetup / event_reminder:meetup:, plan, g (via _enqueue_alert_delivery p_alert_key, live def 0081_direct_messages.sql:34-41, inserts :85 email and :98 push with caller-supplied key), and dispatcher index.ts:208-216 also expects 'direct_message'. Use split_part(alert_key,':',1) with an explicit 'other' bucket rather than a closed enum. (2) 0240_referral_growth.sql:203-206 inserts referral_nudge rows pre-marked status='sent' with sent_at as a one-time dedupe marker (comment: "already 'sent' so it is never dispatched"); the real nudge push goes through _enqueue_push as 'fr:'. The lane must exclude alert_key like 'referral_nudge:%' from sent counts or it overstates sends by one per nudged profile. (3) Pairing caveat is stronger than filed: push.opened's `kind` prop is the tap ACTION (dismiss/checkin/extend/checkout/open - PushNotificationContext.tsx:194-210 and analytics.ts:666), not the alert kind, so even a kind-by-day open rate is impossible; only total sends vs total opens per day until the client carries the outbox id or alert kind prefix.
- *ground-truth:* Proposal stands as a studio lane; three corrections to its spec. (a) Kind map is incomplete: 'vibe' (0103_vibes.sql:75, 0121:67, 0122:61, 0147:469, 0288:89) and 'gift' (0101_gift_notifications.sql:54) are written straight to the outbox with their own prefixes and must be mapped, not bucketed as other. (b) 0240_referral_growth.sql:203-209 inserts 'referral_nudge:<profile_id>' rows with status='sent', sent_at=now() as one-time dedupe markers that are never dispatched (comment at 0240:172-176); the actual nudge push goes out as an 'fr:' row (0240:196). A sent-count must exclude alert_key like 'referral_nudge:%' (or sent_at = created_at) or it overcounts sends, and 'referral_nudge' should not appear in the kind map as a real send. (c) The 'fr:' family also includes the new-referral reward push (0240:160) and the per-dog friend-checkin via 0147:392-412; the app-side p_kind follow-up should enumerate those too. Reachable-device line: user_push_tokens (0060:29-37) has platform and created_at but no deleted_at and the dispatcher hard-deletes invalid tokens (index.ts:411-415), so the lane can only snapshot count-by-platform per run, not reconstruct token churn; say so inline.
- *worth-it:* Title: "The studio reads no push sends: push.opened has no denominator, failures are invisible, and one 'fr:' bucket hides ~8 notification kinds". Proposal fixes: (1) SELECT only id, recipient_id, channel, alert_key, status, error, attempts, created_at, sent_at, claimed_at - never title/body/deep_link (user-written content). (2) Sends = rows where channel='push' AND status in ('sent','failed') AND alert_key NOT LIKE 'test:%' AND alert_key NOT LIKE 'referral_nudge:%' (the latter are never-dispatched dedupe markers written with status='sent', 0240:201-209; the real nudge goes out as an 'fr:' row). State both exclusions inline with their counts. (3) kind = split_part(alert_key, ':', 1), bucketed in SQL to a fixed enum of prefixes actually written to the outbox, with every other prefix as 'other' and the distinct-prefix count shown - do not hard-code expiry_checkout/meetup_followup/dm etc. without verifying each writes to the outbox (many grep hits are home_alerts keys). Note inline that 'place_comment' spans two preference kinds (place_news/place_fun) because _enqueue_alert_delivery writes the key, not p_alert_kind. (4) error_class derived in SQL via CASE on error text: no_tokens ('No push tokens for recipient', index.ts:398) | expo_http ('Expo push error %', index.ts:265) | expo_ticket ('Expo push ticket error%', index.ts:278) | no_email | resend | unknown_channel | other; raw error text never leaves the query. pending/sending reported as in-flight; 'sending' older than 10 min is transient (reaper re-pends, 0403:128-145), attempts>=3 parks as failed (0403:218). (5) Pairing: show push sends per Pacific day beside push.opened per day, split by push.opened `kind` = action (open|dismiss|checkin|extend|checkout) - label it 'taps by action', NOT opens by notification kind. Say inline: per-kind open rate is impossible until the client forwards data.outboxId / data.alertKey prefix, which the dispatcher already sends (index.ts:248). (6) Reachable devices: daily snapshot count(user_push_tokens) by platform; note the dispatcher deletes DeviceNotRegistered tokens (index.ts:275, 415-417) so day-over-day decline is token churn, not a bug. (7) App-side follow-up, separate work-order item, optional: _enqueue_push gains p_kind text and writes p_kind||':'||gen_random_uuid() with kinds friend_request | friend_accepted | event_invite | group_invite | event_join | plan_join | referral_nudge | points_bonus; the dedupe marker in 0240 should move to its own column or table so 'sent' means sent.

<details><summary>Lens verdicts</summary>

- **already-emitted** — real (high): No studio coverage: `grep alert_notification_outbox|user_push_tokens` across analytics-studio hits only prose at config/events.json:1232 (`_whatIsStillNotSeen` inside gap `doggle_product_blind`, status landed) and the taxonomy entry push.opened at config/events.json:377; no script in scripts/ (campaigns, collect, digest, economy, events, events-report, geo, plans, report, serve, shares, usage) reads either table, and config/reports.json / run-daily.ps1 have no push lane. Not registered as its own gap key. ### 6. [HIGH] `foundation_action_outcome_blind` — runNotificationAction resolves success / queued / timed_out / failed and tells only the lock screen, so a lock-screen answer that never saved is invisible to analytics
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** foundation

**Question it answers:** When someone presses Accept / Confirm / Check in on the lock screen, how often does the write land, queue offline, time out, or fail, per action?

**Evidence:** actions.ts:64-78: the outcome selects a NoticeCopy and posts it (:77); RunNotificationActionOptions (actions.ts:40-54) has no callback for the outcome. router.ts:102 fires onOpened BEFORE awaiting opts.onAction (:107), so an open with kind=action counts the attempt, not the result. Pickleague routes all eight handlers plus checkin_extend/court_checkin/play_start through it (notificationActions.ts:189-200, playSession.ts:164-173) with no emitter. Doggle (1.25, own code) likewise emits nothing after performCheckInFromNotification / performExtendFromNotification succeed or fail (PushNotificationContext.tsx:124-183): trackPushOpened at :202 is the only call.

**Proposal:** Kit: add onOutcome?: (outcome: 'success' | 'queued' | 'timed_out' | 'failed', ctx: { idempotencyKey: string }) => void to RunNotificationActionOptions, fired once right before postLocalNotice at actions.ts:77 (swallow throws like router.ts:101-105). App emits push.action_result { action, outcome, outbox_id } where action is the catalog action id, outcome the enum above and outbox_id parsed from idempotencyKey (router.ts:60 base before ':'). Denominator: push.opened with the same outbox_id and kind=action. Doggle: emit the same event from the four branches in performCheckInFromNotification/performExtendFromNotification until it adopts the kit.

**Ground truth already available:** The successful writes land in product tables (event_slot_votes, event_declines, matches via confirm_match, drill_session_rsvps, venue_checkins, play_sessions in pickleague; dog_place_checkins in doggle) but nothing marks that the lock screen was the path, so the rate per action is not recoverable from them.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Gap stands, with three narrowings. (1) The proposed denominator does not exist for Pickleague: pickleague/mobile/src/lib/push.ts:195-200 calls wireNotificationResponses with isAction/onAction/onTap/storageKey and NO onOpened, pickleague's analytics.ts has no push.* emitter (only track/trackScreen/resetSessionUser/endSession exported, :553/:583/:629/:664), and the only non-plumbing track() calls in the app are account.created, auth.login, appstore.click. config/events.json:377-383 registers push.opened for apps:["doggle"] only. So Pickleague must wire onOpened -> push.opened {kind, outbox_id} in the same change, or push.action_result has no attempt to divide by. (2) Doggle's push.opened carries only {kind} (doggle/app/src/lib/analytics.ts:666-668), no outbox_id, so the outbox_id join also needs that prop added on the Doggle side. (3) Doggle's lock-screen CHECK-IN outcome is already partly recorded: performCheckInFromNotification -> checkInManyOrQueue emits checkin.create {source:'manual', dogs, queued:true} when queued (checkinQueue.ts:192-199) and checkInDogPlaceMany emits checkin.create {source,dogs,queued:false} on success (dogPlaceSocial.ts:595) / checkin.failed {source, reason:'cooldown'|'error'} on rejection (dogPlaceSocial.ts:509, :591) - but source is the CheckinSource 'manual', identical to an in-app tap, so the lock-screen rate is not separable. Extend (dogPlaceSocial.ts:456-481, checkinQueue.ts:239-251) emits only Sentry breadcrumbs, and checkout emits checkin.out {source:'auto'|'manual'} (:542/:551) with no path marker. Doggle fix is therefore: either add a 'notification' CheckinSource value (the column join at dogPlaceSocial.ts stays valid) or emit push.action_result from the four branches at PushNotificationContext.tsx:124-183 as proposed; extend/checkout need the new event either way.
- *worth-it:* Keep title/app/severity. Replace proposal with: (a) Kit: add `onOutcome?: (outcome: 'success'|'queued'|'timed_out'|'failed', ctx: { idempotencyKey: string }) => void` to RunNotificationActionOptions, called inside its own try/catch immediately before postLocalNotice at actions.ts:77. Also change the router's `onOpened?: (kind: string)` (router.ts:39) to pass the parsed response's outbox base: `onOpened?: (kind: string, ctx: { outboxId: string | null })`, where outboxId is `data.outboxId` when a string (router.ts:54) else null. (b) Pickleague: wire `onOpened: (kind, ctx) => track('push.opened', { kind, outbox_id: ctx.outboxId })` in push.ts:198-203 (today it passes none, so there is no attempt event at all), and `onOutcome` in handleNotificationAction (notificationActions.ts:192) emitting `push.action_result { action: r.action, outcome, outbox_id }` with outbox_id = idempotencyKey before the first ':' (null when the base is a requestId or 'unknown'). Register `push.opened` for pickleague in events.json (currently doggle-only, :377-383) and add `push.action_result` to the push area for doggle+pickleague with `failure: true` semantics on timed_out/failed. (c) Doggle (until it adopts the kit): add `outbox_id` to trackPushOpened (analytics.ts:666) from `data.outboxId` in handleNotificationResponse, and emit `push.action_result { action: 'checkin'|'extend', outcome, outbox_id }` from the four branches of performCheckInFromNotification (:132 success/queued via res.queued, :146 failed) and performExtendFromNotification (:159 queued, :166 failed-as-'nothing to extend' -> use 'failed', :174 success, :178 failed). Do not add a new checkin event: checkin.create/checkin.failed already fire inside checkInDogPlaceMany (dogPlaceSocial.ts:591-595) with source 'manual' hardcoded by checkInManyOrQueue (checkinQueue.ts:193); the path lives in push.action_result, not in that enum. (d) Denominator rule: rate = push.action_result / push.opened where kind != 'open' and outbox_id matches; a zero result row with a matching opened row is a dropped emit, not a silent user. (e) Feasibility caveat the work order must carry: both apps' track() holds events in an in-memory buffer until resetSessionUser settles (pickleague analytics.ts:541-556 flushed from AppNavigator.tsx:227-231; doggle analytics.ts:544-547, :811). On a headless background launch the navigator may never mount, so the push wiring must settle identity itself from the local session (currentUserId, as notificationActions.ts:71-74 already does) before emitting, or the result event is lost exactly in the lock-screen case it measures.

Pickleague routes every handler through it: notificationActions.ts:189-200 handleNotificationAction -> runNotificationAction; handlers at :104-182 (event_accept, event_decline, match_confirm, drill_accept, drill_decline, drill_on_my_way, drill_cant_make_it, tournament_accept),…
- **ground-truth** — real (high): No product table records a lock-screen action outcome, and the non-success outcomes (queued/timed_out/failed) produce no row anywhere by definition. - **worth-it** — real (high): Code confirms the core claim. Pickleague: notificationActions.ts:189-200 routes every handler through runNotificationAction with no track(); the writes (event_slot_votes insert :107, event_declines :115, rpc confirm_match :126, respond_drill_request :134/:142, rsvp_drill_session :150/:158, tournament_respond_to_invite :167; courtCheckin.ts:187-203, courtGeofe…

### 7. [HIGH] `foundation_local_notice_blind` — scheduleLocalNotification and postLocalNotice are sends that touch no outbox and emit nothing, so a local reminder has no denominator anywhere
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** foundation

**Question it answers:** How many local reminders (check-in ending, session starting now, you are at a court) are scheduled, how many actually fire vs are cancelled, and what share are tapped or answered?

**Evidence:** localSchedule.ts:34-55 schedules under a deterministic id and returns a boolean; cancelLocalNotification :57-61; actions.ts:15-36 postLocalNotice: none takes or fires a hook, none writes a row. Pickleague schedules play_start reminders per upcoming drill/match (playSession.ts:138-147, categoryId play_start), checkin_extend reminders (courtCheckin.ts:86-93), and posts court_checkin geofence notices (courtGeofence.ts:46-48). Doggle does the same with raw expo-notifications (checkinReminders.ts:38, favoriteProximity.ts:17). When such a notice is tapped, wireNotificationResponses reports onOpened('open' or action) with no outboxId (router.ts:54: base falls back to requestId), so the open cannot be told from a remote open and has no send count beneath it.

**Proposal:** Kit: add an optional observer to both modules, e.g. scheduleLocalNotification(spec, { onScheduled?: (categoryId: string | null, idPrefix: string) => void }) fired at localSchedule.ts:51, cancelLocalNotification(id, { onCancelled? }) fired at :60, and postLocalNotice(..., { onPosted?: (categoryId) }) at actions.ts:32; or one module-level setLocalNoticeObserver(fn) so apps wire it once. App emits notice.scheduled { category, id_prefix } (id_prefix = the part of spec.id before the first ':', an app enum such as play-start / checkin-extend), notice.cancelled { category, id_prefix }, notice.posted { category }. Pair with push.opened { source: 'local', category } from foundation_push_opened_no_join: scheduled minus cancelled is the honest denominator of local opens. No PII: title/body never leave the device.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Scope: pickleague + doggle only (michi/tcgscan schedule no local notices). Narrow the 'scheduled' denominator: it is approximable from product tables for check-in reminders (pickleague venue check-ins, doggle check-ins each schedule one checkin_extend) and play_start (upcoming drills/matches per user), so the real unseen quantities are (a) cancellations before fire, (b) local postLocalNotice prompts (geofence court_checkin; doggle's nearby fallback when unreachable or !pushable), and (c) opens attributable to a local notice. Proposal must cover two code paths, not one: doggle does not use the kit — checkinReminders.ts:38, favoriteProximity.ts:17 and PushNotificationContext.tsx:107 call expo-notifications directly — so a kit observer alone leaves doggle blind until those wrappers are either migrated to the kit or given the same notice.scheduled/cancelled/posted emits. Pickleague prerequisite: push.ts:198 passes no onOpened, so push.opened must be wired there before a source:'local' split means anything.
- *ground-truth:* Gap stands as an app/kit instrumentation gap. Two refinements: (1) the studio can already publish an upper-bound denominator per app from ground truth without any new event - pickleague: venue_checkins (user_id, checked_in_at, expires_at, checked_out_at) rows whose expires_at - checked_in_at > 5 min, plus play_sessions upcoming rows; doggle: dog_place_checkins (expires_at, auto_closed, source in manual/passive) - and the proposal should state scheduled/cancelled events are what turns that bound into a true count. (2) The 'answered' leg for checkin_extend needs either the proposed push.opened { source:'local', category } OR a p_source enum ('app' | 'notification_action') on extend_checkin in both pickleague (migration_venue_checkins.sql:88) and doggle (0347:29); the column route is the ground-truth-preferred fix for that one outcome and should be named as the alternative.
- *worth-it:* Severity: medium (not high). Title: 'Local reminders are scheduled, cancelled and posted with no event, and pickleague emits no push.opened at all'. Split the proposal: (a) STUDIO LANE, no app change: read the checkin_extend reminder funnel from venue_checkins (pickleague) and dog_place_checkins (doggle): scheduled = check-ins, cancelled-before-fire = checked_out_at < expires_at - 5min, extended = expires_at bumped after first fire time. (b) APP/KIT events, in the existing push area, not a new 'notice' area: push.local_scheduled { kind, category }, push.local_cancelled { kind, category }, push.local_posted { kind, category } where kind is an explicit app enum passed by the caller (play_start | checkin_extend | court_checkin | favorite_proximity), NOT parsed out of spec.id, and category is the registered categoryId or null. Kit: scheduleLocalNotification(spec, { onScheduled?(kind, category) }) fired only on the true-return path at localSchedule.ts:51 (the <=now+1s early return at :40 is a cancel, report it as push.local_cancelled {reason:'past'}); cancelLocalNotification(id, { onCancelled? }) at :60; postLocalNotice(..., { kind?, onPosted? }) at actions.ts:32. runNotificationAction's result notice (actions.ts:77) must pass kind 'action_result' or be excluded, or every lock-screen button press inflates posted counts. Doggle must emit directly from checkinReminders.ts:38/:58 and favoriteProximity.ts:17 until it leaves foundation 1.25.0. (c) Denominator join: pickleague must add onOpened to push.ts:198 and emit push.opened { kind, source } with source = 'local' when requestId (router.ts:53) matches a scheduled local id or data carries no outboxId, else 'remote'; doggle adds source the same way. Pair: push.local_scheduled - push.local_cancelled is the denominator for push.opened {source:'local'}; push.local_posted is the denominator for court_checkin / favorite_proximity opens. 'Fired' is unobservable and must be labelled as scheduled-minus-cancelled, never as delivered. No michi/tcgscan change (no surface).

<details><summary>Lens verdicts</summary>

- **already-emitted** — real (high): Surface exists as claimed and nothing covers it. Kit: expo-foundation/src/platform/push/localSchedule.ts:34-55 (scheduleLocalNotification returns boolean, no hook), :57-61 (cancelLocalNotification), actions.ts:15-36 (postLocalNotice, no hook); grep of expo-foundation/src/platform/push/*.ts for onScheduled|onCancelled|onPosted|observer|track|analytics hits only two comments (authBound.ts:87, router.ts:104). router.ts:54 falls back to requestId when data.outboxId is absent, :102 reports onOpened(action|'open') with no source. Pickleague calls them at mobile/src/lib/playSession.ts:140-147 (id `play-start:${kind}:${id}`, categoryId play_start), :149,:159 (cancel), courtCheckin.ts:81,86-93 (REMINDER_ID, categoryId checkin_extend, expires_at-5min), courtGeofence.ts:46-49 (postLocalNotice, categoryId court_checkin). ### 8. [HIGH] `foundation_push_opened_no_join` — The kit's onOpened hook drops outboxId, requestId and category before analytics, so an open can never be joined to the send that caused it
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** foundation

**Question it answers:** Per notification kind, what share of sent pushes were opened or answered, and which kinds are ignored?

**Evidence:** router.ts:47-61 parse() has data (incl. data.outboxId when the dispatcher set it, outboxDispatch.ts:155), requestId and idempotencyKey; router.ts:102 calls opts.onOpened?.(isAction ? parsed.action : 'open') with ONE string and nothing else. The category id is on the Expo message only (outboxDispatch.ts:161) and never in data, so neither app can learn which button set the push carried. doggle's hand-rolled handler has the same shape: trackPushOpened(kind) at PushNotificationContext.tsx:202-212 ignores data.outboxId and data.alertKey that its own dispatcher puts on the payload (dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:248). Pickleague's dataFor puts notification_id/type/entity_type on data (send-push/index.ts:85-91) but onOpened cannot see it.

**Proposal:** Kit: widen the hook to onOpened(kind, ctx: { outboxId: string | null, requestId: string | null, kind: string | null, category: string | null, source: 'remote' | 'local' }) where ctx.kind/category are read from data (the dispatcher should add data.kind = row.kind and data.categoryId = resolved category next to outboxId at outboxDispatch.ts:155) and source = outboxId ? 'remote' : 'local'. App emits push.opened { kind, outbox_id, push_kind, category, source } (kind stays the action verb/id; outbox_id is a uuid, push_kind the outbox row kind, category the catalog id). Denominator: outbox rows with status='sent' and error is null (see foundation_outbox_suppressed_as_sent), joined on outbox_id. Doggle should read data.outboxId/data.alertKey into the same props now, before it upgrades, so the two apps land identical shapes.

**Ground truth already available:** push_outbox / alert_notification_outbox hold the sends (kind or alert_key, sent_at). The join key (outbox id) is already in every payload but is not emitted on the open side.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow/sharpen: (1) Pickleague is worse than the title states - it does not pass onOpened at all (mobile/src/lib/push.ts:198-203) and has no push.* event of any name, so widening the kit hook alone changes nothing there; pickleague must also pass onOpened and declare push.opened (and the studio must add 'pickleague' to the push.opened apps list at config/events.json:377-382). (2) Doggle already has push.opened {kind} (analytics.ts:666-667) - its fix is to add outbox_id (data.outboxId) and alert_key-derived push_kind (data.alertKey prefix before ':', an enum, never the raw key which carries ids) from PushNotificationContext.tsx:224's data read, not a new event. (3) Ground-truth note: the denominator for pickleague is push_outbox (status='sent', error is null) and for doggle alert_notification_outbox; the join is a studio-lane read once the open side carries outbox_id. Proposal otherwise stands; keep the event name push.opened (already in the taxonomy) rather than a new name.
- *ground-truth:* Scope correction only: (1) pickleague is worse than stated - push.ts:198-203 calls wireNotificationResponses with isAction/onAction/onTap/storageKey and no onOpened at all, and pickleague/mobile/src/lib/analytics.ts has no push.* events, so pickleague emits zero opens today; the work order must add the onOpened wiring there, not just widen it. (2) michi-maker/tcgscan-app are not yet affected: no push/outbox/notification migrations exist in michi-maker/supabase/migrations and no wireNotificationResponses/onOpened call sites in either app; list them as 'inherit on adoption', symmetric. (3) Denominator refinement: prefer {{prefix}}_deliveries status='ok' (push_outbox.template.sql:38-47; pickleague migration_push_outbox.sql:55-66) as the delivered-device denominator where it exists, falling back to outbox status='sent' and error is null; doggle has no deliveries table (0060:54-67, 0403:57-66) so it uses outbox status='sent' only. (4) Do not reuse push.opened's existing {kind} prop for a new meaning: keep kind = action verb, add outbox_id, push_kind, category, source exactly as proposed, so doggle's existing rows stay readable.
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap, retitle: "Push opens cannot be joined to sends: the kit's onOpened passes only the action string (and pickleague does not wire onOpened at all)". Proposal, corrected: (a) Kit: `onOpened?: (kind: string, ctx: PushOpenContext) => void` where `PushOpenContext = { outboxId: string | null; requestId: string | null; pushKind: string | null; category: string | null; source: 'remote' | 'local' }`, built in router.ts from a WHITELIST of data keys only (data.outboxId, data.kind, data.categoryId) - never spread data, never include parsed.text, data.title or deepLink. Dispatcher (outboxDispatch.ts:155) adds `kind: row.kind` and `categoryId: category` next to outboxId so category reaches data. (b) Pickleague: push.ts:198 adds `onOpened: (kind, ctx) => track('push.opened', { kind, outbox_id: ctx.outboxId, push_kind: ctx.pushKind, category: ctx.category, source: ctx.source })` - this is the first push event pickleague emits; add push.opened to events.json apps: ["doggle","pickleague"]. (c) Doggle now, before upgrading: read `data.outboxId` and `data.alertKey` at PushNotificationContext.tsx:202 and emit the same props, with push_kind = alertKey.split(':')[0] (the prefix, e.g. 'dog_news', 'planned_visit'), never the full alertKey (it carries an entity uuid) and never the notification title. (d) Studio lane: per app, join analytics_events push.opened.outbox_id to push_outbox.id / alert_notification_outbox.id on the same project; denominator = outbox rows with status='sent' and error is null; numerator = distinct outbox_id in push.opened; break out by push_kind and by kind (tap vs each action id). Sends with no token (outboxDispatch.ts:138 'No push tokens for recipient') are failed rows and stay out of the denominator. Severity: high for pickleague (no open signal at all), medium for doggle (has opens, lacks the join key).

### 9. [HIGH] `pickleague_push_open_blind` — A tapped push emits nothing and does not mark the notification read, so opens are invisible everywhere
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** What share of sent pushes are opened, per category and per entity type, and does a confirm/vote push get opened before its expires_at?

**Evidence:** push.ts:198-203 passes no onOpened to wireNotificationResponses (kit router.ts:39 declares it, :102 fires it). push.ts:173-185 routeNotification reads deepLink/entity_type/entity_id and ignores data.outboxId / data.notification_id (put there by outboxDispatch.ts:156 and send-push/index.ts:86-92); it navigates and never updates notifications.is_read - only NotificationsScreen.tsx:93-101 markRead (in-app list tap) does. No track( in push.ts. Doggle emits push.opened {kind} at PushNotificationContext.tsx:202-212; pickleague declares none of the four push.* names (config/events.json:356-382 apps: doggle only).

**Proposal:** Add pickleague to push.opened's apps and emit it from push.ts onTap with { kind: 'open', category, outbox_id, notification_id, type } (type = notifications.type enum: info|tournament|league|match|drill|...; entity ids only). Pair with push_outbox sent rows as the denominator; a push opened after expires_at is a 'late open' bucket in the studio, not a separate event. Separately, read notifications.is_read as 'opened the in-app list', never as 'noticed' (see pickleague_is_read_not_seen).

**Ground truth already available:** Sends: push_outbox (status, sent_at, expires_at, category, entity_type). Opens: none - notifications.is_read is set only by the in-app list.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowings. (1) Denominator: use push_deliveries rows with status='ok' (migration_push_outbox.sql:55-66, joined to push_outbox via outbox_id) rather than push_outbox sent rows - a 'sent' outbox row may have delivered to zero tokens, and deliveries is per-token so it matches the per-device tap. (2) Emit via the kit's existing onOpened hook (router.ts:39, fired at :102 with 'open' or the action id) instead of inside onTap, so button presses (confirm/vote/decline at notificationActions.ts:113,121) are counted as opens with kind=<action> and a plain tap as kind='open'; outbox_id/notification_id must be read from data.outboxId / data.notification_id (outboxDispatch.ts:156, send-push/index.ts:87), which requires widening PushData (push.ts:137-143). The doggle citation should read doggle/app/src/lib/analytics.ts:667 for the actual track('push.opened', { kind }) call.
- *ground-truth:* Narrow scope: plain-tap opens only. Action-button presses already leave product rows (event_slot_votes / event_declines inserts at mobile/src/lib/notificationActions.ts:113,121; RPCs at :95) and are answerable by a studio lane joining those tables to push_outbox (notification_id, sent_at, expires_at, category, entity_type). Keep the app proposal for push.opened {kind:'open', category, outbox_id, notification_id, type} from push.ts onTap; the 'late open' bucket compares the event timestamp to push_outbox.expires_at.
- *worth-it:* Title (tightened): "A tapped or button-pressed pickleague push emits no event; opens and action rates per category are invisible and cannot be read against push_outbox sends". Proposal: add 'pickleague' to push.opened.apps in config/events.json:377-382 (reuse, no new name). Emit from push.ts inside BOTH the onTap and onAction callbacks (not via the kit's onOpened, which passes only `kind` per router.ts:39 and has no access to data): track('push.opened', { kind, category, type, entity_type, outbox_id, notification_id }) where kind = 'open' for a plain tap or the action id for a button (e.g. 'confirm_match', 'event_decline', 'tournament_accept' - whatever notificationActions.ts registers; mirrors doggle's dismiss/checkin/extend/checkout/open), category = data.category ?? null (catalog key from send-push catalog.json; add `category: row.category` to baseData at send-push/index.ts:86-92 since it is not currently on the push), type = data.type (notifications.type enum: info|match|league|tournament|drill|event), entity_type = data.entity_type (enum of routesFor keys), outbox_id = data.outboxId, notification_id = data.notification_id. Extend PushData (push.ts:137-143) with outboxId and notification_id so they are typed, never title (content). Never pass data.title. Denominator in the studio: push_outbox where status='sent' joined to push_deliveries status='ok' (per device), bucketed by category/kind/entity_type; an open with event ts > push_outbox.expires_at is a 'late open' bucket, not a second event. Separate product work order (not an analytics prop): on tap, update notifications set is_read=true where id = data.notification_id, so the badge count stops lying after a push tap; until that lands, read notifications.is_read strictly as 'tapped in the in-app list'.

### 10. [HIGH] `pickleague_push_optin_blind` — Push opt-in emits nothing: no permission ask, no permission result, no token registered
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Of signed-in native users, how many ever flip Push on, how many are then denied by the OS vs fail the token round trip vs register, and how many registered devices does each account keep?

**Evidence:** push.ts:40 REGISTRATION = { androidChannelId: 'default', androidChannelName: 'Default' } - the kit's onPermissionAsked / onPermissionResult(result, reason) / onTokenRegistered options (expo-foundation src/platform/pushNotifications.ts:62-72, fired at :81, :113, :116) are not supplied anywhere; push.ts:116 requestPushToken(REGISTRATION) and :61-87 createAuthBoundPushTokens (no onRegistered; kit authBound.ts:26 declares it, :85 fires it). push.ts:96-99 computes a three-way outcome (registered|unavailable|failed) and SettingsScreen.tsx:127-150 consumes it with no track. Doggle emits all three at analytics.ts:638-657 from PushNotificationContext.tsx:291-294, :394, :401, :506, :512. Only pickleague track( sites: GetTheAppButton.tsx:63, GuestJoinScreen.tsx:114, LoginScreen.tsx:48, RegisterScreen.tsx:93.

**Proposal:** Add pickleague to the apps of the existing push.permission_requested { surface: 'settings' | 'app' }, push.permission_result { result: granted | denied | unavailable, reason: unsupported_platform | no_module | threw | no_token } and push.token_registered { platform } - no new names. Wire them through the kit: pass onPermissionAsked/onPermissionResult/onTokenRegistered in REGISTRATION (push.ts:40, surface 'settings' from enablePushNotifications, 'app' from the auth-bound sync via sync({registrationOptions})) and onRegistered on createAuthBoundPushTokens (push.ts:61). Add push.permission_result reason 'store_failed' for the push.ts:122-126 failed branch (upsert error, not an OS answer) so 'unavailable' keeps doggle's meaning. Denominator for the grant rate is push.permission_requested per surface, never sessions.

**Ground truth already available:** push_tokens (current rows only; deleted at sign-out push.ts:83-85 and on DeviceNotRegistered) and user_preferences.prefs.pushEnabled (current value only) - a denial leaves no row anywhere.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowings. (1) File it as new evidence against doggle_product_blind rather than a fully independent gap: that entry's title names Pickleague but its status "landed" is doggle-only; either split out a pickleague_push_optin_blind key or reopen doggle_product_blind for pickleague. (2) The proposed 'store_failed' reason does not cover every push.ts failed branch. The kit only wraps the permission calls in try/catch (pushNotifications.ts:107-121); setNotificationChannelAsync and getExpoPushTokenAsync at :126-136 are unwrapped, so a throw there surfaces as push.ts:117-118 `{status:'failed'}` WITHOUT the kit having fired onPermissionResult. Add a second pickleague-emitted reason (e.g. 'token_threw') for the :117-118 catch, alongside 'store_failed' for :124-125, so every 'failed' outcome at SettingsScreen.tsx:135 has a push.permission_result row. Also note the auth-bound 'app' surface can only show the sheet when pushEnabled is already true (authBound.ts:69-74 passes requestIfNeeded:false otherwise), so 'app'-surface asks are re-grants on a new device or re-login, not first opt-ins.
- *worth-it:* Keep title/app/names; fix the proposal in four places. (a) Drop the new reason 'store_failed' on push.permission_result: an upsert error is not a permission answer, and doggle already leaves a store failure as permission_result=granted with no push.token_registered (PushNotificationContext.tsx:399-401, :510-512). Read push.ts:122-126 the same way - derived, no new enum value - so the event keeps one meaning across apps. (b) Emit push.token_registered from exactly ONE hook: authBound onRegistered (authBound.ts:82-85, fires only after the store, matching doggle's 'accepted by the backend' at :401/:512). Do NOT pass onTokenRegistered: it fires when a token is merely obtained (pushNotifications.ts:143) and would fire twice per Settings enable (push.ts:116 and again inside the :128 sync()). Do not add a direct track after push.ts:123 either - the :128 sync() re-runs register (kit's token is null) and fires onRegistered once. Note token_registered then fires on every cold start with push on (authBound.ts:116-117 start()->sync()), as doggle's does; 'registered devices per account' stays ground truth push_tokens. (c) The kit's onPermissionResult does not cover a throw from setNotificationChannelAsync/getExpoPushTokenAsync (pushNotifications.ts:126-136 has no result() call), and that is exactly what reaches push.ts:117-118. Emit push.permission_result { result: 'unavailable', reason: 'threw' } from that catch so every push.permission_requested pairs with an answer. (d) Narrow the question: a toggle flip while the OS permission is already granted emits no push.* event in either app by design (kit :111 skips result(); doggle :289 'not an ask, so not counted'). Current opt-in count is user_preferences.prefs.pushEnabled, not the stream. Ask: grant rate per surface ('settings' from enablePushNotifications via requestPushToken({...REGISTRATION, onPermissionAsked, onPermissionResult}); 'app' via the REGISTRATION default used by the auth-bound sync, which raises the sheet only when pushEnabled=true on a device whose OS permission is not granted, e.g. a new device) and reachable-device rate = push.token_registered / permission_result{granted}. Also mark doggle_product_blind as landed-for-doggle-only in its detail when this is registered.
