### 11. [HIGH] `pickleague_push_outbox_unread` — The studio reads nothing from push_outbox / push_deliveries / push_tokens, so every push rate above has no denominator
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** studio

**Question it answers:** How many pushes went out per day per category, how many were suppressed by preference, failed, expired unsent, or bounced on receipt, and how many devices are reachable right now?

**Evidence:** Ground truth exists: supabase/migration_push_outbox.sql:28-48 push_outbox (status pending|sending|sent|failed|expired, attempts, sent_at, expires_at, category, kind, entity_type, recipient_id), :55-64 push_deliveries (per-device ticket_id, status ok|error, receipt_checked_at), migration_push_notifications.sql:24-31 push_tokens. Quirks the lane must handle: outboxDispatch.ts:197-200 marks a preference-suppressed row status='sent' with error='suppressed by preference' (not a status of its own); migration_push_outbox.sql:128-139 send_test_push writes kind='test' rows; :258-263 prunes sent/expired/failed after 30 days so ALL_WINDOW is impossible and the 30d window is the ceiling. Studio: grep of analytics-studio/scripts/*.mjs and scripts/lib for push_outbox|push_deliveries|push_tokens returns nothing; config/events.json truth block lists only michi/tcgscan tables; digest.mjs:304-307 pickleague sections are matches/league/tournament/votes. The registered note at config/events.json:1232 says sends and opens 'are read together or not at all' for doggle; pickleague has no entry.

**Proposal:** A studio lane (scripts/push.mjs, data/push.json committed as counts, roster sidecar gitignored) that reads push_outbox per window: queued, sent (error is null), suppressed (status='sent' and error='suppressed by preference'), failed, expired, attempts>1, plus push_deliveries ok/error/receipt-error and push_tokens per platform; exclude kind='test' and excluded accounts via exclusionCte on recipient_id; state inline that history is capped at 30 days by push_prune. Register truth.push_send = { app: pickleague, table: push_outbox, userCol: recipient_id, tsCol: sent_at } and the doggle twin in config/events.json so push.opened / push.action_result divide by it.

**Ground truth already available:** push_outbox, push_deliveries, push_tokens on the pickleague project (and alert_notification_outbox on doggle, same shape after 0403).

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Three narrowings. (1) `push.action_result` does not exist anywhere: not in config/events.json taxonomy (only push.permission_requested :356, push.permission_result :363, push.token_registered :370, push.opened :377), not in doggle/app/src/lib/analytics.ts, not in pickleague/mobile/src/lib/analytics.ts. Drop it from the proposal; the only client-side numerator is push.opened, emitted by doggle only (doggle/app/src/lib/analytics.ts:667). (2) pickleague/mobile/src/lib/analytics.ts has no push.* track() call sites at all, so for pickleague the outbox lane is the whole measurement of push (sends/suppressed/failed/expired/receipt errors/reachable devices), not a denominator for an open rate; an open rate for pickleague needs a separate app-instrumentation gap (push.opened emitter, mirroring doggle). (3) The doggle twin is defined at doggle/db/migrations/0060_alert_notifications.sql (create table alert_notification_outbox), not a "0403" migration; its column shape was not verified against pickleague's and must be checked before registering it under the same truth key. Minor: push_prune removes only terminal rows (sent/expired/failed), so pending/sending rows can outlive 30 days and the "30d ceiling" applies to terminal statuses only.
- *ground-truth:* Keep as a studio-lane gap. Doggle twin is narrower than 'same shape': alert_notification_outbox (0060_alert_notifications.sql:54-67, 0403:55-61) has recipient_id, channel, alert_key, status pending|sending|sent|failed, error, created_at, sent_at - no expired, no attempts, no kind/category, no per-device deliveries table. Register truth.push_send for doggle with table alert_notification_outbox, userCol recipient_id, tsCol sent_at, filter channel='push', group by alert_key; the suppressed/expired/attempts>1/receipt columns apply to pickleague only.
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap as a STUDIO-LANE gap (app: studio, severity high) but split the question and fix the props/pairing: 1) Lane scripts/push.mjs, data/push.json (counts only), data/push-roster.json gitignored. Per pickleague window (24h/7d/14d/30d; state inline 'history capped at 30 days by private.push_prune, no all-time view'), excluding kind='test' and excluded_users on recipient_id: queued (count by created_at), sent_ok (status='sent' and error is null), suppressed (status='sent' and error='suppressed by preference'), failed, expired, pending_or_sending (stuck), retried (attempts>1); grouped by kind (bounded by notifications.type check info|tournament|league|match plus any new kinds mapped through a fixed allowlist with an 'other' bucket) and by category ONLY via the KNOWN_PREF_KEYS set in send-push/index.ts with 'other' for anything else - never group by or commit the raw category or error strings. Deliveries: per outbox row count tickets ok / error, bucket delivery error as enum {device_not_registered, other} (outboxDispatch.ts:186 already special-cases DeviceNotRegistered), receipt_pending (status='ok' and receipt_checked_at is null), never commit token or ticket_id. Reachability: count(distinct user_id) and count(*) from push_tokens per platform in {ios, android, other}, excluded_users removed, stated as point-in-time (not windowed). 2) Register truth.push_send = { app: pickleague, table: push_outbox, userCol: recipient_id, tsCol: sent_at, filter: \"status='sent' and error is null and kind<>'test'\" } - the filter is required or suppressed rows inflate the denominator. 3) Doggle gets its OWN truth entry, not a twin: truth.push_send += { app: doggle, table: alert_notification_outbox, userCol: recipient_id, tsCol: sent_at, filter: \"channel='push' and status='sent'\" }; its statuses are pending|sending|sent|failed only (0403:61), category = alert_key, reachability from user_push_tokens, and no deliveries/receipts panel exists for doggle. 4) Drop 'so push.opened / push.action_result divide by it' for pickleague: push.opened is doggle-only today and push.action_result is not in the taxonomy. State the open-rate pairing as DEPENDENT on a separate pickleague client gap (push.opened {kind, entity_type, outbox_id} and push.action_result {kind, action, result enum} emitted from wireNotificationResponses in mobile/src/lib/push.ts) and until that lands render pickleague opens as 'not instrumented', not zero. The doggle open rate CAN be computed now: push.opened (events.json:377) over the doggle truth.push_send row.

### 12. [HIGH] `studio_outbox_lane_missing` — No studio lane reads any outbox or token table, although the schema already answers sends, failures, expiries, retries, per-device delivery and receipts
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** studio

**Question it answers:** How many pushes went out per day per kind, how many failed or expired, how long from enqueue to send, how many devices per platform are reachable, and (pickleague only) how many tickets Expo later rejected?

**Evidence:** grep -i 'outbox|push_tokens|notification_pref' over analytics-studio/scripts and config matches only prose: config/events.json:1232 (gaps.doggle_product_blind._whatIsStillNotSeen: 'Push SENDS ... live in doggle's alert_notification_outbox and the two are read together or not at all'); scripts/events.mjs:839 reads only the client event prop. Tables that exist: pickleague push_outbox (migration_push_outbox.sql:28-47: kind, category, entity_type, status, error, expires_at, attempts, claimed_at, created_at, sent_at), push_deliveries (:55-67: token, ticket_id, status ok|error, error, receipt_checked_at), push_tokens (migration_push_notifications.sql:24-31: platform, created_at, updated_at); doggle alert_notification_outbox (0060_alert_notifications.sql:54-67 + 0403:57-64: alert_key, status, error, created_at, sent_at, claimed_at, attempts), user_push_tokens (0060:30-38). Asymmetry: doggle's dispatcher is its own code importing only _kit/auth.ts (dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:38), has no deliveries table and polls no receipts (grep 'deliveries' in doggle/db hits only a blog post, 0285:64), so doggle 'sent' means Expo issued a ticket, while pickleague 'sent' can be checked against receipts.

**Proposal:** A read-only lane (scripts/push.mjs -> data/push.json counts, gitignored data/push-roster.json) with all four staples, per app and per window on created_at: sends = status='sent' and error is null; suppressed = status='sent' and error='suppressed by preference' (kit) stated inline; failed, expired, pending>15min (health); attempts histogram; p50/p95 of sent_at - created_at; per-kind table (push_outbox.kind / alert_notification_outbox.alert_key prefix, mapped to the catalog category by the same prefix table as dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:207-215); pickleague deliveries: ok vs error, receipt_checked_at not null and status='error' = post-send receipt failures, DeviceNotRegistered prunes; token fleet = count(*) by platform (ALL_WINDOW). Exclusions via recipient_id against exclusionCte(). State inline that doggle has no receipt data. Opens from push.opened join on outbox_id once foundation_push_opened_no_join lands; until then show sends and opens as two unjoined numbers per kind, as doggle_product_blind already warns.

**Ground truth already available:** pickleague: public.push_outbox, public.push_deliveries, public.push_tokens. doggle: public.alert_notification_outbox, public.user_push_tokens.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the proposal's metric set per app. pickleague only: expired (push_outbox status 'expired', migration_push_outbox.sql:41,235), suppressed = status 'sent' and error 'suppressed by preference' (kit sentinel, expo-foundation/server/deno/outboxDispatch.ts:197, used via pickleague send-push/index.ts:26), deliveries ok|error, receipt failures, DeviceNotRegistered prunes. doggle only has pending|sending|sent|failed (0060:63 as amended by 0403:61), no expires_at column (grep across doggle/db/migrations finds none on alert_notification_outbox), no deliveries, no receipts, and no suppressed sentinel - its 'failed' rows carry a free-text error (dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:425) that the lane must bucket to a fixed enum, never print. Shared across both: sends/failed/pending>15min per window on created_at, attempts histogram, p50/p95 sent_at - created_at, per-kind table (pickleague push_outbox.kind; doggle alert_key prefix via CATEGORY_BY_PREFIX at dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:207-215), token fleet by platform (ALL_WINDOW). doggle recipient column is recipient_id -> profiles (0060:194-209); pickleague is recipient_id -> auth.users (migration_push_outbox.sql:133,153) - the exclusionCte() join differs per app. `foundation_push_opened_no_join` is not a registered gap in config/events.json; refer to it as a sibling proposal, and until any join lands show sends and push.opened as two unjoined numbers per kind.
- *ground-truth:* Narrow the 'expired' bucket to pickleague only: doggle's alert_notification_outbox status check is pending|sending|sent|failed (0060_alert_notifications.sql:61-62, 0403_alert_outbox_hardening.sql:53-55) and the table has no expires_at column (grep 'expires_at|expired' across 0060 and 0403 returns nothing), so doggle's health block is failed + pending>15min + attempts only. Likewise 'suppressed by preference' is pickleague-only in practice: doggle's dispatcher does not use _kit/outboxDispatch.ts (index.ts:37-41), so the lane should state inline that doggle has neither suppression, expiry nor receipt data.
- *worth-it:* Keep the lane (scripts/push.mjs -> data/push.json counts, gitignored data/push-roster.json) with these fixes. (1) COLUMNS: select only id, recipient_id, kind|alert_key, category, status, error, attempts, created_at, sent_at, claimed_at, expires_at, platform; NEVER title, body, deep_link, push_deliveries.token or ticket_id into any output. Classify `error` server-side in SQL into a fixed enum and emit only the enum: failure_class in {device_not_registered, suppressed_pref, expo_ticket_error, expo_receipt_error, expired, other}; raw error strings never leave the query. (2) SUPPRESSION is pickleague-only: suppressed = status='sent' and error='suppressed by preference' (pickleague/supabase/functions/_kit/outboxDispatch.ts:198). Doggle gates prefs at enqueue (0060_alert_notifications.sql:200,271) so suppressed pushes have no row; print inline for doggle: 'preference-suppressed pushes are not enqueued and cannot be counted here' - not a zero. (3) OPENS: push.opened exists only in doggle (analytics.ts:667, prop kind; config/events.json:377). For doggle show sends and opens per kind as two unjoined numbers (prefix table CATEGORY_BY_PREFIX, dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:208-216). For pickleague show sends only and state inline 'opens: not instrumented (no push.opened emitter in pickleague/mobile)'. Do not reference foundation_push_opened_no_join as a registered gap; it is not in config/events.json gaps - cite doggle_product_blind._whatIsStillNotSeen (events.json:1232) instead. (4) DENOMINATORS: enqueued = count(*) by created_at window is the denominator for sent / failed / expired / pending; sent is the denominator for deliveries ok|error (pickleague) and for opens (doggle); token fleet by platform (ALL_WINDOW) is the denominator for reach. pending>15min counts rows with status in ('pending','sending') and created_at < collectedAt - 15min, labelled with the collectedAt it was measured against. (5) TOKEN PRUNES: both apps delete DeviceNotRegistered tokens (doggle index.ts:275,413-414; kit :186, receipt path :306-309); only pickleague leaves a push_deliveries row with status='error'. Report doggle prunes as 'not recorded' and show the fleet count day-over-day instead. (6) EXCLUSIONS: join recipient_id -> auth.users u and apply exclusionCte(appId); state excluded count inline per window per app. (7) Register in config/reports.json group Product, apps [doggle, pickleague], data-app-scope per app; add data/push-roster.json to .gitignore in the same change; add to run-daily.ps1 after the DAU lane.

### 13. [MEDIUM] `doggle_kit_pin_drift_kind_vocab` — Doggle at 1.25.0 hand-rolls what 1.32-1.35 hoisted, and its push.opened kind vocabulary differs from the kit's onOpened contract, so the two apps' opens will not share one shape when pickleague wires the hook
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Can the studio read push.opened { kind } from doggle and pickleague as one series, and can doggle's prompt declined/dismissed split ever be seen without an upgrade?

**Evidence:** doggle/app/package.json:19 pins 1.25.0 (installed 1.25.0); PushNotificationContext.tsx:316-402 and :444-458 reimplement signOutPending / inFlightStore / token read / bounded unregister that authBound.ts:58-62, :128-147 ship in 1.35; :185-241 reimplement router.ts; :409-427 raw setNotificationCategoryAsync instead of registerNotificationCategories (pushNotifications.ts:179). Vocabulary: doggle maps actions to lowercase verbs 'dismiss' | 'checkin' | 'extend' | 'checkout' | 'open' (PushNotificationContext.tsx:202-212) while the kit passes the catalog action id verbatim (router.ts:102; doggle's ids are CHECK_IN / DISMISS / EXTEND_CHECKIN / CHECK_OUT per dispatch-alert-notifications/catalog.json, pickleague's event_accept / match_confirm etc.). New evidence on a registered gap: config/events.json gaps.doggle_product_blind._explainPromptsAreWrapped says the explain onEvent observer is 'unreleased'; it shipped in 1.26.0 on 2026-09-04 (UPGRADING.md:345-353, git f456cff) - the blocker is doggle's pin, not an unpublished kit.

**Proposal:** Decide one contract and write it into the kit doc at router.ts:38: onOpened passes the catalog action id verbatim and 'open' for a tap; the studio maps ids to labels per app in config/events.json (a qualifier like QUALIFIERS at scripts/events.mjs:825-843 keyed push.opened -> kind). Doggle keeps its five current values until it upgrades; register both spellings in the taxonomy so neither reads as unrecognised. Update gaps.doggle_product_blind._explainPromptsAreWrapped to say 'shipped in 1.26.0, doggle pinned to 1.25.0' so the next reader does not wait on a release that already happened. The upgrade itself (1.25 -> 1.36, UPGRADING.md:14-62 'Crossing several releases') is the work order that retires the hand-rolled code; doggle's dispatcher could then adopt createOutboxDispatcher and gain push_deliveries + receipts (outboxDispatch.ts:141-151, 304-360), closing the doggle half of studio_outbox_lane_missing.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowings. (a) Pickleague emits NO push.opened today: pickleague/mobile/src/lib/push.ts:198-203 calls wireNotificationResponses with isAction/onAction/onTap/storageKey and omits onOpened, and src/lib/analytics.ts has no push.* names. So the "one series" question is currently moot - the first work item is pickleague wiring onOpened, and the studio's push.opened taxonomy entry (config/events.json:377) must gain "pickleague" in apps when it does. (b) The drift is wider than spelling: doggle's server catalog (doggle/supabase/functions/dispatch-alert-notifications/catalog.json) defines six categories with FRIEND_ACCEPT, FRIEND_DECLINE, EVENT_GOING, EVENT_MAYBE, PLAN_JOIN, DM_REPLY, but the client registers only doggle.checkin and doggle.checkin.extend (notificationCategories.ts:4-12, PushNotificationContext.tsx:409-427), and the mapping at :202-212 sends every other action id to 'open'. Today those buttons never render on device (category unregistered), so no rows are lost yet; the moment doggle adopts registerNotificationCategories from the catalog, six distinct actions would collapse into 'open' under the current client code. The proposal should therefore state the contract as "verbatim action id, 'open' for the default tap" and have the studio label ids per app, and the doggle work order should include registering the four missing categories rather than only renaming five values.
- *ground-truth:* Gap stands as an app + studio-taxonomy gap. One addition to the proposal: the DENOMINATOR for a per-kind open rate is already ground truth - doggle alert_notification_outbox.alert_key (0060_alert_notifications.sql:58) and pickleague push_outbox.kind (migration_push_outbox.sql:33, = notifications.type). The push.opened -> kind qualifier in config/events.json must therefore map onto the same label set the outbox lane uses for alert_key / kind, so sends (table) and opens (stream) join on one key per app; that is the doggle half of the already-registered studio_outbox_lane_missing, not a new gap.
- *worth-it:* Split into three and retitle. (1) [medium, pickleague, NEW] "pickleague never emits push.opened": pickleague/mobile/src/lib/push.ts:198-203 calls wireNotificationResponses without onOpened, so no pickleague push open is ever recorded; fix is one line - onOpened: (kind) => track('push.opened', { kind }) - and add "pickleague" to config/events.json push.opened.apps. Denominator is ground truth: pickleague.push_outbox + push_deliveries (migration_push_outbox.sql:28-61). (2) [low, doggle + kit, vocabulary] "push.opened kind is the catalog action id verbatim, 'open' for a plain tap" - write this into router.ts:38 as the fleet contract. Doggle should switch NOW, not after the upgrade: replace the ternary at PushNotificationContext.tsx:202-212 with trackPushOpened(action && action !== DEFAULT ? action : 'open'); zero rows exist (data/events.json:10813 neverFired), so no dual spelling needs registering, and the change also stops FRIEND_ACCEPT/EVENT_GOING/PLAN_JOIN/DM_REPLY collapsing into 'open' (catalog.json). Studio: add "push.opened": "kind" to QUALIFIERS (scripts/events.mjs:825-843) and per-app id->label maps in config/events.json. Denominator: doggle.alert_notification_outbox (0060_alert_notifications.sql:54-66, status/sent_at already there). (3) [doc fix, registered gap] gaps.doggle_product_blind._explainPromptsAreWrapped (config/events.json:1233): change "unreleased" to "shipped in expo-foundation 1.26.0 (f456cff, 2026-09-04); blocked only by doggle's 1.25.0 pin (package.json:19)". Drop the "hand-rolls what 1.32-1.35 hoisted" framing from the tracking-gap list: it is a kit-upgrade work order (UPGRADING.md "Crossing several releases"), and the only analytics consequence is per-device deliveries/receipts (outboxDispatch.ts:141-151), which belongs with the sibling studio_outbox_lane_missing proposal - cite it as a sibling, not as a registered key.

### 14. [MEDIUM] `doggle_local_notice_blind` — Local notifications emit nothing when scheduled or cancelled, and their taps are counted as push.opened indistinguishable from server pushes
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (2/3; dissent from ground-truth)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** How many extend nudges actually fire (scheduled minus cancelled), how often are Extend vs Check out pressed, and does the local nearby fallback convert like the server push?

**Evidence:** Three local sites, zero track() calls: extend nudge scheduled at check-in+25min (app/src/lib/checkinReminders.ts:38-47, cancelled 54-62); 'Check in nearby?' fallback when the server RPC is unreachable or the user has no token (app/src/lib/favoriteProximity.ts:13-29, condition 62-68, per 0311_nearby_prompt_pushable.sql:1-12); confirmation notices after lock-screen actions (app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:107-117, posted at 138-150, 160-181). All are delivered to handleNotificationResponse (481-484) and counted at 202-212 with kind only; a tap on 'Checked in 🐾' is a push.opened 'open'. Only the extend nudge carries data.kind ('checkin-extend', checkinReminders.ts:43); the proximity fallback carries data {deepLink} alone (favoriteProximity.ts:21); confirmations carry no data. The scheduled-vs-cancelled lifecycle of the extend nudge is invisible: a visit checked out at minute 20 cancels a nudge that was never shown.

**Proposal:** notice.scheduled {kind: checkin_extend|nearby_prompt|confirm, fire_in_s: number|0} at the three schedule sites; notice.cancelled {kind: checkin_extend} in cancelCheckinReminder when a pending request existed. Stamp data.kind at every local site so push.opened can carry origin: local and alert_kind: checkin_extend|nearby_prompt|confirm (see doggle_push_open_unjoinable; origin is derived as data.outboxId absent). Denominator for local prompts = notice.scheduled - notice.cancelled (a floor on delivered, stated inline: the OS gives JS no delivery callback for a killed app); outcome = push.opened origin=local by kind, then checkin.create/checkin.extend. Confirmation notices get notice.scheduled kind=confirm only so their taps can be excluded from open rates.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowing corrections and one widening. (a) The proposal's outcome event `checkin.extend` does not exist: extendCheckin at doggle/app/src/data/dogPlaceSocial.ts:456-483 and extendOrQueue at app/src/lib/checkinQueue.ts:239-244 emit no track() at all (only breadcrumb), unlike checkin.create/checkin.out/checkin.failed at dogPlaceSocial.ts:440-595. So the extend OUTCOME is blind too, from both the lock-screen action (PushNotificationContext.tsx:158) and the in-app WalkIsland button (app/src/components/WalkIsland.tsx:213). Add checkin.extend {source: 'notification'|'island', queued: boolean} as a new taxonomy name; the only ground-truth proxy is dog_place_checkins.expires_at - created_at > 30 min (extended at least once, no count, no source). (b) `notice.cancelled ... when a pending request existed` is not knowable from the cancel site: cancelScheduledNotificationAsync returns void and cancelCheckinReminder is called unconditionally on every check-out (dogPlaceSocial.ts:540 auto, :564 manual) including visits that never had a nudge (walk visits, scheduleExtendNudge returns at :410 for source 'walk'; google-only sites at :413). The client must keep a module-level pending set keyed by dogPlaceId in checkinReminders.ts (set at :38, cleared at :58 and at fire/tap) and emit notice.cancelled only when the id was in it. Also scheduleCheckinReminder cancels-then-reschedules on extend (checkinReminders.ts:35 then :38, reached from extendCheckin :479-481); that path must be stamped reason: 'reschedule' or not counted as a cancel, or the floor `scheduled - cancelled` undercounts. (c) Keep the rest: origin derived from data.outboxId absent is sound, since the local nearby prompt carries only {deepLink} (favoriteProximity.ts:21) and confirmations carry no data (PushNotificationContext.tsx:111).
- *ground-truth:* Split. (a) studio lane: read public.dog_place_checkins (profile_id, dog_place_id, google_place_id, source, created_at, ended_at, expires_at, auto_closed) — nudge-eligible = source in (manual,passive) and dog_place_id not null; nudge fired (floor) = ended_at is null or ended_at >= created_at + 25min; extended = expires_at > created_at + 30min; checked out vs forgot = auto_closed; pair with push.opened kind in (extend, checkout), which are already nudge-only taps. Caveat inline: web visits have no nudge (checkinReminders.ts:16) and the table has no platform column. (b) Narrow doggle app gap, low severity, title "Local 'Check in nearby?' fallback has no impression row and confirmation taps count as push.opened open": the outbox skips token-less users (0063:197-213) and the offline branch never reaches the server, so emit notice.shown {kind: nearby_prompt, reason: no_token|unreachable} at favoriteProximity.ts:63 only, and stamp data.kind at favoriteProximity.ts:21 ('nearby-prompt') and postLocalNotice (PushNotificationContext.tsx:107-117, 'confirm') so push.opened can carry alert_kind and confirm taps are excluded. Outcome = push.opened kind=checkin with alert_kind=nearby_prompt then checkin.create. Drop notice.scheduled/notice.cancelled for checkin_extend entirely; fix the proposal's `checkin.extend` reference (event is checkin.out; no extend event exists).
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap; rewrite proposal under the existing 'push' area. (1) push.scheduled {kind: 'checkin_extend'|'nearby_checkin'|'confirm', fire_in_s: number} emitted AFTER scheduleNotificationAsync resolves (checkinReminders.ts after :47, fire_in_s = round((fireAtMs-now)/1000); favoriteProximity.ts after :25 with fire_in_s 0; PushNotificationContext.tsx after :113 with kind 'confirm', fire_in_s 0) - never before the :37 past-time no-op. (2) push.cancelled {kind: 'checkin_extend'} in cancelCheckinReminder only when getAllScheduledNotificationsAsync() lists reminderId(dogPlaceId) before cancelling. (3) Stamp data.kind at all three local sites using the SAME enum ('checkin_extend' replaces the current 'checkin-extend' at :43; 'nearby_checkin' matches the outbox alert kind from 0311; 'confirm' for postLocalNotice) and extend trackPushOpened(kind, origin, alert_kind) so push.opened carries origin: 'server'|'local' (server iff data.outboxId is a string) and alert_kind: data.kind for local, outbox kind for server (dispatch must add data.kind = row.kind; see doggle_push_open_unjoinable). (4) Add the missing outcome event checkin.extend {source: 'notification'|'island', queued: boolean, result: 'extended'|'closed'|'queued'|'error'} in performExtendFromNotification (PushNotificationContext.tsx:156-183) and WalkIsland.tsx:213 - no checkin.extend exists today. (5) Fix the double-count: lock-screen Check out (PushNotificationContext.tsx:228-237) emits checkin.out twice ('auto' + 'manual'); emit once with source 'notification' from the handler and suppress the two inner tracks on that path, and move trackCheckout('manual') at dogPlaceSocial.ts:551 after the error check to match :542. (6) Report denominators from ground truth where it exists: nudge-eligible visits = dog_place_checkins where source in ('manual','passive') and dog_place_id is not null and coalesce(ended_at, now()) > created_at + 25 min; extended visits = expires_at - created_at > 30 min; forgotten = auto_closed. push.scheduled - push.cancelled is the client-side floor for native sessions only (web schedules nothing: checkinReminders.ts:16, favoriteProximity.ts:14), stated inline. Mark the whole family dependent on doggle_product_blind reaching fixed, since push.opened has zero observed rows.

### 15. [MEDIUM] `doggle_notification_prefs_untracked` — Notification preference changes, radius changes and test pushes emit nothing, and the preferences table keeps no history
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Which alert kinds do people turn off, how soon after receiving one, and does a burst of nearby prompts precede a master opt-out?

**Evidence:** app/src/components/AlertDeliveryPanel.tsx: setEmailMaster 53-65, setPushMaster 67-91, setTypePref 93-106 (10 kinds x 2 channels), sendTestPush 108-120; FavoriteCheckinSettingsPanel.tsx:128-146 and WalkAutoCheckinToggle.tsx:73 radii. grep -rn "track" on those files -> none. Server: update_notification_preferences / update_alert_type_notification_prefs overwrite user_notification_preferences in place (0060:367-398, 0081:173-200); the table has updated_at only, no change rows. Platform defaults are written silently at sign-in (PushNotificationContext.tsx:243-249), so a row's existence is not a user decision.

**Proposal:** alerts.pref_changed {kind: master|dog_news|place_news|place_fun|friend_checkin|planned_visit|nearby_checkin|auto_checkin|event_reminder|event_join|direct_message, channel: email|push, on: boolean} at the three panel handlers after the RPC resolves; alerts.radius_changed {radius: walk_favorite|favorite|other_nearby, m: number} ; alerts.test_push {ok: boolean, tokens: number} from sendTestPush. Point-in-time opt-out rates per kind come from the ground-truth table in the push lane (ALL_WINDOW snapshot); the events give the transitions and what preceded them (join to outbox sends for the same recipient in the prior 24h).

**Ground truth already available:** public.user_notification_preferences (current state only: push_alerts_enabled, email_alerts_enabled, push_<kind>_enabled, favorite_radius_m, walk_favorite_radius_m, other_nearby_radius_m, updated_at)

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow in two places. ### 16. [MEDIUM] `doggle_prompt_suppression_invisible` — Nearby check-in prompts that the server refuses (cooldown, already checked in) or the client cannot deliver are recorded nowhere, so the prompt has no attempt count
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** How many times did a geofence or passive scan put someone next to a place, and how many of those became a prompt (server push or local), a suppression, or nothing because we were offline?

**Evidence:** enqueue_nearby_checkin_prompt returns {enqueued:false, reason:'already_checked_in'|'cooldown'} without writing anything (db/migrations/0311_nearby_prompt_pushable.sql:46-63) and {enqueued, pushable} otherwise. The client receives it in promptCheckinNearby (app/src/lib/favoriteProximity.ts:48-50) and logs it ONLY via recordGps (51-56), an opt-in GPS debug session (app/src/lib/gpsDebug.ts:1-4), then chooses local vs none (62-68). The 'unreachable' branch (offline) is likewise unrecorded. Server-side pref-off and no-token suppression in _enqueue_alert_delivery (0081:84-104) and _enqueue_push (0105:17) also write nothing, but those are computable from ground truth. Walk-time in-app prompts ARE counted (prompt.shown surface 'walk', WalkCheckinPromptHost.tsx:76); the background/passive path is the one with no denominator.

**Proposal:** checkin.prompt_requested {outcome: enqueued_push|local_fallback|suppressed_checked_in|suppressed_cooldown|unreachable, trigger: geofence|passive_scan} emitted once in promptCheckinNearby right after the RPC resolves (favoriteProximity.ts:50), trigger threaded from handleFavoriteProximity vs passiveFavoriteService callers. This is the denominator; the outbox row ('nearby_checkin:<place>') and notice.scheduled kind=nearby_prompt are the impressions; push.opened and checkin.create are the outcomes. Note inline that background emission from a killed app may be lost (analytics buffer is in-memory, analytics.ts:544-553), so the count is a floor.

**Ground truth already available:** Partial: user_notification_preferences x user_push_tokens answers 'who could never receive a nearby push'; nothing answers attempts.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two corrections to the proposal. (1) Drop the reference to `notice.scheduled kind=nearby_prompt` as the impression leg: doggle has no notice.scheduled emitter anywhere in app/src (grep empty; analytics.ts:602-752 lists every typed emitter). The impression for the push path is the alert_notification_outbox row with alert_key 'nearby_checkin:<place>' (0081_direct_messages.sql:84-104, 0311:66-70); the impression for the local path has no row at all, so `outcome: local_fallback` in the proposed checkin.prompt_requested is itself the only record of a local notification and must be stated as such. (2) Ground truth is a bit wider than 'partial: prefs x tokens': walk_nearby_checkin_cooldowns.last_prompted_at (0073_walk_nearby_checkins.sql:8-13, upserted at 0311:72-75) proves a user/place was enqueued at least once but is a single latest timestamp per (profile, place), not a count, and alert_notification_outbox only sees enqueued AND pref-on AND token-present. Neither can see suppressed_checked_in, suppressed_cooldown, unreachable, or local_fallback - so the gap remains an app-instrumentation gap, narrowed to: 'one event at favoriteProximity.ts:50 with outcome + trigger (geofence from favoriteGeofenceTask.ts:34, passive_scan from passiveFavoriteService.ts:54/74)'. Keep the floor caveat: analytics.ts:541-560 buffers in memory until cachedUser settles, and the geofence task runs in a fresh JS context (favoriteGeofenceTask.ts:1-4), so background emissions may be lost.
- *ground-truth:* Three fixes to the proposal, gap otherwise stands. (a) The proposal's impression 'notice.scheduled kind=nearby_prompt' does not exist anywhere - not in doggle/app/src/lib/analytics.ts, not in michi's analytics, not in config/events.json; the local-fallback impression is only the proposed event's own outcome=local_fallback (scheduleLocalPrompt, favoriteProximity.ts:13-29, emits nothing). Drop that reference. (b) The client wrapper enqueueNearbyCheckinPrompt (app/src/data/walkNearbyCheckins.ts:39-51) types the result as {enqueued, pushable} and discards the server's `reason`, so suppressed_checked_in vs suppressed_cooldown cannot be distinguished until the wrapper passes `reason` through; proposal must name that one-line change. (c) outcome=enqueued_push is already ground truth in alert_notification_outbox (0060:54-66, alert_key 'nearby_checkin:<place>', created_at, status) - the event adds the three unrecorded outcomes (suppressed_*, local_fallback, unreachable); the studio lane should read the outbox as the un-droppable cross-check on enqueued_push rows (a background emission lost from a killed app shows as outbox > event).
- *worth-it:* Keep key/app/severity. Split the denominator by who can honestly see it. (1) SERVER (both triggers, the only place a geofence attempt from a killed app is observable): in enqueue_nearby_checkin_prompt (0311) record every call's outcome before returning - either add a nullable-user analytics_events insert {app:'doggle', name:'checkin.prompt_requested', props:{outcome:'enqueued_push'|'enqueued_no_token'|'suppressed_checked_in'|'suppressed_cooldown'|'suppressed_pref_off', trigger}} with trigger passed as a new p_trigger text param ('geofence'|'passive_scan') from the two callers (favoriteGeofenceTask.ts:34 via handleFavoriteProximity; passiveFavoriteService.ts:54,74), or add prompted_count/suppressed_count integer columns to walk_nearby_checkin_cooldowns bumped on every call. Note 'enqueued_no_token' and 'suppressed_pref_off' must be derived inside the RPC (today _enqueue_alert_delivery swallows both silently, 0081:84-104). (2) CLIENT, foreground only: checkin.prompt_requested {outcome:'unreachable'|'local_fallback', trigger:'passive_scan'} emitted in promptCheckinNearby after scheduleLocalPrompt resolves (favoriteProximity.ts:62-68); this IS the local impression - drop the reference to notice.scheduled, which does not exist. State inline that trigger='geofence' client rows will be ~0 by construction (headless context never calls resetSessionUser), not a floor, so geofence attempts are read from the server record only. (3) Outcome attribution: add `alert` to push.opened (PushNotificationContext.tsx:202), a fixed enum derived from the deep-link prefix ('walk/checkin/' -> 'nearby_checkin', 'chat/' -> 'direct_message', etc., never the payload), so opens of this prompt can be paired with its impressions; also set the same `alert` on the local notification's data so local and server prompts read alike. Impressions = outbox rows alert_key like 'nearby_checkin:%' (server push) + client local_fallback; outcomes = push.opened{alert:'nearby_checkin'} and checkin.create{source:'passive'}.

### 17. [MEDIUM] `doggle_push_surface_mislabeled` — Onboarding's push ask is recorded with surface 'settings', so the per-surface grant rate the events exist for is blended
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** What share of new users grant push when asked during onboarding versus later from Settings, and how many iOS accounts burned their one-shot ask at onboarding?

**Evidence:** app/src/screens/OnboardingScreen.tsx:320-329 enablePermissions() calls enablePushAlerts() (323). enablePushAlerts hardcodes requestPushPermission('settings') (app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:503). PromptSurface declares 'onboarding' (app/src/lib/analytics.ts:577-584) and no call site passes it (grep trackPushPermissionRequested: only :291 with the surface argument of requestPushPermission, whose callers pass 'app' at :389 and 'settings' at :503). The doc comment at :268-269 states 'Onboarding and settings are not the same ask and must not share a grant rate'. Unchanged on next-build: OnboardingScreen.tsx:323 still enablePushAlerts(), permissionTracking(surface) at next-build PushNotificationContext.tsx:290. Side effect: on web, onboarding reaches requestPushPermission and emits push.permission_result unavailable/unsupported_platform (:272-274) with no preceding push.permission_requested, so result rows can exceed request rows.

**Proposal:** enablePushAlerts(surface: 'onboarding' | 'settings') and pass it to requestPushPermission; OnboardingScreen passes 'onboarding', AlertDeliveryPanel passes 'settings'. Skip the OS-sheet path entirely when Platform.OS is not ios/android before any result event, or keep the unavailable result but pair it by emitting push.permission_requested first, so request and result stay a pair per surface. Denominator: push.permission_requested {surface}; outcome: push.permission_result joined on session_id, read per surface.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrowing only: the per-surface blend is between 'onboarding' and 'settings' (both land as 'settings'); the third surface 'app' (PushNotificationContext.tsx:389, provider-mount re-registration) is labelled correctly and unaffected. The web orphan (result without request) is onboarding-only because AlertDeliveryPanel.tsx:71 gates enablePushAlerts behind isNative and OnboardingScreen.tsx:323 does not; add `if (!isNative) skip` (or emit push.permission_requested first) at the onboarding call site, not just inside requestPushPermission.
- *ground-truth:* Gap stands as an app gap. One supplement for the verifier, not a reclassification: the GRANT side can be cross-checked from ground truth by joining user_push_tokens.created_at (0060:34) to profiles creation time - a token created within the onboarding window is an onboarding grant - but that cannot produce a rate because denials and asks have no row, and it cannot see iOS accounts that burned the one-shot ask.
- *worth-it:* Keep event names as-is (push.permission_requested, push.permission_result - both already registered). Fix the pairing directly rather than via session join: (a) enablePushAlerts(surface: 'onboarding' | 'settings') in PushNotificationContext.tsx; OnboardingScreen.tsx:323 passes 'onboarding', AlertDeliveryPanel.tsx:72 passes 'settings'; (b) thread surface into trackPushPermissionResult so push.permission_result props become {surface, result, reason?} - requestPushPermission already has surface in scope at every emit site (:273, :284, :294, :297) and the two callers at :394/:506 know theirs; (c) return early from requestPushPermission on non-ios/android WITHOUT emitting any result event (the 'unavailable/unsupported_platform' row at :272-274 has no request to pair with and the AlertDeliveryPanel already gates on isNative at :71, so only onboarding on web produces it). Read: grant rate per surface = count(push.permission_result where result='granted' and surface=S) / count(push.permission_requested where surface=S); iOS burned asks = push.permission_result {surface:'onboarding', result:'denied'} joined to session platform. Apply the same change on next-build: permissionTracking(surface) at next-build PushNotificationContext.tsx:290-296 should pass surface into onPermissionResult, and :362 should take the surface argument instead of the literal 'settings'.

### 18. [MEDIUM] `foundation_outbox_suppressed_as_sent` — The kit dispatcher marks preference-suppressed rows status='sent' and never persists the resolved category, so the outbox overstates sends and cannot group by button set
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** foundation

**Question it answers:** How many pushes were actually delivered to Expo versus closed because the recipient had the kind turned off, and which button set did each send carry?

**Evidence:** outboxDispatch.ts:196-199: if resolvePrefs returns false the row is marked { status: 'sent', sent_at: now, error: 'suppressed by preference' } - the only discriminator is free text in the error column, which the status check constraint (push_outbox.template.sql:24-25) cannot express. The category used for the push is resolved at send time (outboxDispatch.ts:154) and put on the Expo message (:161) only; the template has no category column (push_outbox.template.sql:16-32). Pickleague's own `category` column (migration_push_outbox.sql:33) is the preference key copied from notifications.category, not the button set. Pickleague vendors this exact dispatcher (send-push/index.ts:26, 182-192; _kit stamp 1.34.0).

**Proposal:** Kit template + dispatcher: add 'suppressed' to the status check (push_outbox.template.sql:24-25) and write status='suppressed' with error null at outboxDispatch.ts:197 (migration for pickleague: alter the constraint first, as 0403 did for doggle); add category_id text to the outbox and set it in mark() on the sent/failed path from the resolved value at outboxDispatch.ts:154. Until that lands, the studio lane must count sends as status='sent' and error is null and report error='suppressed by preference' as its own inline line, never as a send. Applies to pickleague's push_outbox today; doggle's own dispatcher does not have this branch (dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:422-425 marks sent only after sendPush).

**Ground truth already available:** push_outbox is the ground truth; the flaw is in how the kit writes it.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the question's first half: "how many pushes actually reached Expo" IS already answerable today without a schema change - push_deliveries rows are written only after sendExpoPush (outboxDispatch.ts:170-184), so a suppressed row has zero deliveries rows, and the sent path writes error null (:215). The studio lane can count true sends as `status='sent' and error is null` (equivalently `exists push_deliveries`) and report `error='suppressed by preference'` as its own inline line today, as the proposal already says. The part that is genuinely unrecoverable from ground truth is the button set: the resolved categoryId is never persisted (not on push_outbox, not on push_deliveries), so "which button set did each send carry" needs the kit change (category_id column set in mark() from the :154 value, plus 'suppressed' in the status check). Scope: pickleague push_outbox only; doggle gates preferences at enqueue (0060:184,255) and has no suppressed-as-sent branch. Also note the kit is at 1.36.0 while pickleague's vendored stamp is 1.34.0 - the fix must land in the kit and be re-vendored, not patched in pickleague's _kit copy.
- *ground-truth:* Title: "Kit outbox never persists the resolved button-set category; preference-suppressed rows are distinguishable only via push_deliveries absence / a fixed error string". App: foundation (kit) + pickleague. Drop the "overstates sends" claim - the studio lane can count it now: studio lane: read push_outbox (id, recipient_id, kind, category, status, error, created_at, sent_at) LEFT JOIN push_deliveries (outbox_id, token, status, ticket_id, receipt_checked_at); sent-to-Expo = exists deliveries row; suppressed = status='sent' and error='suppressed by preference' (no deliveries rows), reported as its own inline line; snapshot daily because push_prune drops rows >30d. Keep the kit proposal for a 'suppressed' status (hygiene, not a blocker) and keep the real gap: add category_id text to the outbox (or to deliveries) written in mark() from the value resolved at outboxDispatch.ts:154.
- *worth-it:* Keep the proposal; two refinements so the number is actionable rather than just honest. (a) Pickleague's resolvePrefs collapses two different causes into one false (send-push/index.ts:72 `pushEnabled !== true` = never opted in; :74-75 = specific category muted). Change the kit hook signature to return a reason enum (`resolvePrefs?: (row) => Promise<true | 'push_disabled' | 'category_muted'>`) and have mark() write it to a new `suppressed_reason text check (suppressed_reason in ('push_disabled','category_muted'))` column, with status='suppressed' and error null - never the free-text error column. Without the split, the studio cannot tell an opt-in problem from a mute problem, which are different product decisions. (b) `category_id` must be nullable and stay null on suppressed and expired rows, because categoryFor runs only inside sendPushRow (outboxDispatch.ts:154) after the pref check; set it on the sent/failed paths only, from the value already resolved at :154 (pass it back out of sendPushRow's result). Interim studio rule stands: sends = status='sent' and error is null; error='suppressed by preference' is its own inline line per window, and the lane also shows the opt-in denominator (count of user_preferences rows with prefs->>'pushEnabled'='true' vs recipients with outbox rows) so the suppressed count has a population.

<details><summary>Lens verdicts</summary>

- **already-emitted** — real (high): Confirmed in code, not rows. Kit: C:/Users/Brian/source/repos/expo-foundation/server/deno/outboxDispatch.ts:196-198 marks a resolvePrefs=false row `{ status: 'sent', sent_at: now, error: 'suppressed by preference' }`; the real send path marks `error: null` (:215). Category is resolved at :154 and placed only on the Expo message (:161); mark() never receives it. Template push_outbox.template.sql:24-25 allows only pending/sending/sent/failed/expired and has no category column (:16-32); push_deliveries (:38-47) has no category column either. Kit is at 1.36.0 (package.json:3) and the line is unchanged since 1.33.0 (git log: 64ae54f, 071b77e) - no later fix. Pickleague vendors the identical bran…

### 19. [MEDIUM] `foundation_prefs_change_blind` — Neither app nor the kit records when a notification preference flips, and all three prefs tables overwrite in place, so opt-outs have no timing and no trigger
**Status:** **PARTIAL** (2/2 lenses that answered said real; the rest failed on a transient error)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** doggle+pickleague

**Question it answers:** After which notification kinds do people turn push off, how soon after enabling, and how many have push on in prefs but no reachable device?

**Evidence:** Kit template notification_prefs.template.sql:16-23 stores enabled + updated_at per (profile_id, kind, channel) and nothing else; the kit has no client-side prefs helper to hang a hook on. Doggle: data/notifications.ts:111-139 updateNotificationPreferences / updateAlertTypeNotificationPrefs call RPCs (0060_alert_notifications.sql:367-381 overwrites) with no track(); grep "track\('(prefs|notification|settings)" over doggle/app/src returns nothing. Pickleague: user_preferences is one jsonb blob + updated_at (migration_add_user_preferences.sql:12-16); SettingsScreen.tsx:127-150 togglePush and userPreferences.ts saveUserPreferences :100-118 emit nothing. The dispatcher honours the current value at send time only (outboxDispatch.ts:196; send-push/index.ts:67-77).

**Proposal:** App-level event (same name in both apps): push.pref_changed { kind, channel, enabled } where kind is the preference key enum (doggle AlertKind / pickleague KNOWN_PREF_KEYS; null for the master switch), channel 'push' | 'email', enabled boolean - emitted where the RPC/upsert resolves (doggle notifications.ts:115-121, :129-136; pickleague SettingsScreen togglePush and the per-category toggles). Denominator: page.view of the settings route for the toggle rate; the outbox lane's last sent kind before each off-flip for 'what caused it' (join on recipient_id and timestamp, studio side). Optionally the kit template adds a {{prefix}}_pref_changes audit table written by a trigger on {{prefix}}_prefs (profile_id, kind, channel, enabled, changed_at), which would make this ground truth instead of a stream event; pickleague's jsonb blob cannot get that for free.

**Ground truth already available:** Current state only: doggle user_notification_preferences (0060:12-18), pickleague user_preferences.prefs.pushEnabled + per-category keys. Reachability now: push_tokens / user_push_tokens. Neither gives the moment or the sequence.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two citation fixes, no change to substance: (1) pickleague has no KNOWN_PREF_KEYS symbol - the per-category enum is the notify* keys of the Prefs type in mobile/src/lib/userPreferences.ts (notifyMatchResults, notifyMatchConfirms, notifyEventReminders, notifyLeagueUpdates, notifyTournamentUpdates, notifyChallenges, notifyDrillRequests) toggled at SettingsScreen.tsx:472-524; use those as the kind enum. (2) doggle has no supabase/functions/send-push directory - the only confirmed send-time pref read is pickleague outboxDispatch.ts:196; drop the send-push/index.ts:67-77 cite or re-locate it. Also note the pickleague settings denominator is page.view route 'Settings' (AppNavigator.tsx:350), already emitted.
- *ground-truth:* Two refinements, verdict unchanged. (1) Pickleague has a PARTIAL ground-truth bracket the gap missed: expo-foundation/server/deno/outboxDispatch.ts:196-198 marks a pref-suppressed row status='sent', sent_at=now(), error='suppressed by preference' in push_outbox (migration_push_outbox.sql:28-48, with recipient_id, kind, category, created_at). A studio lane can read push_outbox (recipient_id, kind, category, sent_at, error) and take the last genuinely-sent kind before the first 'suppressed by preference' row per recipient as 'what preceded the opt-out', with the flip bracketed between those two timestamps. It is an approximation (only as fine as the send cadence, and it cannot see re-enables or email), so the event/audit proposal still stands; it is a day-one interim for pickleague only. Doggle has no equivalent because 0060:192-200 gates on insert and never writes the suppressed row - the evidence line citing outboxDispatch.ts:196 / send-push/index.ts:67-77 is pickleague-only. (2) Prefer the audit-table variant over the stream event, per 'ground truth beats the stream': a trigger on doggle user_notification_preferences can diff OLD vs NEW per *_enabled column, and pickleague's jsonb blob does NOT preclude it - a trigger on user_preferences can diff OLD.prefs vs NEW.prefs over pushEnabled and KNOWN_PREF_KEYS, so 'cannot get that for free' is overstated; the app-level push.pref_changed event is then the denominator-side companion (settings page.view) rather than the primary source.

### 20. [MEDIUM] `pickleague_court_alert_permission_blind` — The Always-location ask behind court arrival alerts emits nothing, and the toggle itself lives only in AsyncStorage
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** How many people turn on court arrival alerts, how many are refused Always location (iOS one-shot), and how many end up with zero fenced courts because they have no league home court or check-in history?

**Evidence:** courtGeofence.ts:139-147 setCourtAlertsEnabled writes ENABLED_KEY to AsyncStorage (:23, :141) - no table, no event. :156-160 syncGeofences(..., { skipPermissionRequest: !interactive, minRadiusM, maxRadiusM }) omits the kit's onPermissionAsked(kind) / onPermissionResult(kind, granted) hooks (expo-foundation src/tracking/geofence.ts:79-80, fired :102, :105). SettingsScreen.tsx:78-85 toggleCourtAlerts calls it with no track; :83 flips the toggle back off when 0 courts fenced, so a denial and 'no courts to fence' are indistinguishable even to the user. Doggle declares location.permission_requested / location.permission_result {scope} (config/events.json taxonomy, apps: doggle).

**Proposal:** Add pickleague to the existing location.permission_requested { scope: 'background' | 'foreground' } and location.permission_result { scope, result: granted | denied } via the geofence.ts:79-80 hooks passed at courtGeofence.ts:156. NEW courts.alerts_toggled { enabled: boolean, fenced: <count> } from setCourtAlertsEnabled (fenced is the return value; 0 with enabled=true is the 'nothing to fence' outcome). Denominator for the grant rate is location.permission_requested with scope='background'.

**Ground truth already available:** None. The toggle is per-device AsyncStorage; the OS permission is not in any table.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowings. (1) Fire courts.alerts_toggled from SettingsScreen.toggleCourtAlerts, not from setCourtAlertsEnabled: SettingsScreen.tsx:83 calls setCourtAlertsEnabled(false) on the n===0 path, so an emitter inside the lib would double-fire (enabled=true fenced=0, then enabled=false fenced=0) for one user action. One event per toggle with { enabled, fenced } at the screen level, or a reason enum ('ok' | 'no_courts' | 'denied'), is the honest shape. (2) Partial ground truth exists for the downstream outcome: venue_checkins.source='geofence' (migration_venue_checkins.sql:25) already counts check-ins that arrival alerts produced, so the proposal should not add a check-in event; the gap is strictly the toggle and the Always-location ask/result. Also note the startup path AppNavigator.tsx:263 syncCourtGeofences() runs with skipPermissionRequest=true, so hooks would fire only from the Settings toggle - the denominator location.permission_requested{scope:'background'} is correct and unaffected.
- *ground-truth:* Minor additions, not a reclassification: (a) the studio can already read the downstream outcome from venue_checkins where source='geofence' (user_id, checked_in_at) as the 'alerts led to a check-in' numerator - state it beside the new events so the toggle-to-checkin funnel has a ground-truth last stage. (b) Optionally persist the toggle into the existing user_preferences.prefs jsonb (migration_add_user_preferences.sql:12-16) alongside the event, since that table was created precisely to move per-device AsyncStorage prefs cross-device; the event remains necessary for the permission denial and fenced=0 outcomes, which no table can hold.
- *worth-it:* Keep the title and app. Rewrite the proposal: (a) Add 'pickleague' to the existing location.permission_requested { scope: 'foreground' | 'background' } and location.permission_result { scope, result: 'granted' | 'denied' } by passing onPermissionAsked / onPermissionResult into syncGeofences at courtGeofence.ts:156 (hooks defined at expo-foundation geofence.ts:79-80). Do NOT carry doggle's optional free-text `reason` prop (doggle analytics.ts:678-681) - the hooks only yield a boolean, and reason as a string violates the no-free-text rule. (b) Rename the new event to the existing area word: checkin.alerts_toggled { enabled: boolean, candidates: <count>, fenced: <count> } - emitted ONCE per user action from SettingsScreen.toggleCourtAlerts (not from setCourtAlertsEnabled, which SettingsScreen.tsx:83 calls a second time for the automatic revert and would double-count). `candidates` is pickRegions().regions.length (courtGeofence.ts:154, computed BEFORE the permission ask), `fenced` is the syncGeofences return. This makes the zero unambiguous without a join: enabled=true,candidates=0 is 'nothing to fence'; enabled=true,candidates>0,fenced=0 is 'permission refused'; the location.permission_result row confirms which. Denominator for grant rate: location.permission_requested scope='background'. Denominator for the toggle funnel: page.view of the Settings route (already emitted). (c) Downstream outcome stays on ground truth: venue_checkins where source='geofence' (migration_venue_checkins.sql:25) counts check-ins the alerts produced - read it in the studio lane, do not add a client event for it.

### 21. [MEDIUM] `pickleague_local_notice_blind` — Local notices (check-in reminder, play-start, court arrival) have no impression anywhere, so their buttons have no denominator
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** How many arrival notices lead to a check-in, how many 5-minute reminders are extended vs checked out vs ignored (lapse), and does the play-start notice ever start a session?

**Evidence:** courtCheckin.ts:85-94 scheduleLocalNotification(checkin-extend, category checkin_extend); playSession.ts:140-147 scheduleLocalNotification(play-start:<kind>:<id>, category play_start); courtGeofence.ts:46-49 postLocalNotice(court_checkin) after the 3h RENOTIFY throttle :26,:41. Kit localSchedule.ts:34-55 returns a boolean and persists nothing; cancellation :57-61 is silent. These never enter push_outbox (the trigger at migration_push_outbox.sql:144-165 fires only on notifications inserts). Button writes: checkin_extend/checkin_out courtCheckin.ts:187-202, court_checkin courtGeofence.ts:67-76 (source 'geofence'), play_start playSession.ts:164-173 - all through handleNotificationAction with no track. Outcome tables: venue_checkins.source in ('manual','geofence','session') (migration_venue_checkins.sql:25) shows geofence check-ins happened; play_sessions (migration_play_sessions.sql:14-23) has no origin column.

**Proposal:** NEW notice.scheduled { category, venue_id? , source_id? } from courtCheckin.ts:85 and playSession.ts:140 when scheduleLocalNotification returns true, NEW notice.shown { category, venue_id } from courtGeofence.ts:46 after postLocalNotice (immediate, so 'shown' is honest there; 'scheduled' is the honest word for a future date the app cannot observe firing). Their button presses reuse push.opened { kind: <action id>, category, outbox_id: null } and push.action_result from pickleague_push_action_blind. Studio pairs notice.scheduled(checkin_extend) with venue_checkins extend/checkout/lapse, and notice.shown(court_checkin) with venue_checkins.source='geofence' within 30 min. Add a `source` column to play_sessions ('island' | 'notice') if the play-start question matters; otherwise read play_sessions.kind only.

**Ground truth already available:** Numerators only: venue_checkins where source='geofence' (arrival notice converted), venue_checkins extended (expires_at moved) vs checked_out_at vs lapsed. No impression table.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the button-press half: the kit already exposes the hook. expo-foundation src/platform/push/router.ts:39 declares `onOpened?: (kind: string) => void` and :100-105 calls it with the action id (or 'open') before onAction/onTap, wrapped so analytics cannot block the action. Pickleague push.ts:198-203 passes isAction/onAction/onTap/storageKey but not onOpened, so push.opened { kind } is a one-line wire there, not a new emitter; it lands in the same place as pickleague_push_action_blind, so this gap should depend on that one rather than re-propose it. Keep the impression half as stated (notice.scheduled / notice.shown are new names; nothing in taxonomy :55-460 collides). Two refinements: (a) notice.shown(court_checkin) must fire after the throttle at courtGeofence.ts:41 (post-throttle is what the user saw), and the proposal already places it there - keep it. (b) For the check-in outcome names, reuse the existing doggle taxonomy checkin.create / checkin.out (config/events.json:419-432) with apps extended to pickleague, adding a `source` prop ('manual'|'geofence'|'session') mirroring venue_checkins.source, rather than inventing a pickleague-only outcome vocabulary; the impression event remains the only truly new name.
- *ground-truth:* Two refinements, not refutations. (a) groundTruth overstates venue_checkins: 'extended (expires_at moved)' is not readable from the table. extend_checkin (migration_venue_checkins.sql:87-104) overwrites expires_at in place and checkin_venue accepts p_minutes 15..240 (:78-80), and the original duration is not stored, so 'expires_at - checked_in_at > 120 min' is a heuristic that confuses a long initial check-in with an extension. Either read extend vs checkout vs lapse from the proposed push.opened{kind:'checkin_extend'|'checkin_out'} / push.action_result events, or add an `extended_at timestamptz` (or `extend_count int`) column set by extend_checkin. Lapse = checked_out_at is null and expires_at < now() is derivable as stated. (b) notice.scheduled must be deduped by notice id in the studio: scheduleReminder fires on every setCurrent (courtCheckin.ts:80, including refresh/reconcile), and syncPlayStartReminders (playSession.ts:131-154) cancels and reschedules every upcoming item on each sync, so raw scheduled counts overcount impressions several-fold per check-in / per upcoming play. Carry checkin_id (checkin_extend) and `${kind}:${source_id}` (play_start) as the id prop and count distinct.
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap, fix names and the denominators. (a) Area word: `notice.` is not in the taxonomy; `push.` is (events.json:356-377). Rename to `push.local_scheduled { category, checkin_id? | source_id?, play_kind?, venue_id? }` and `push.local_shown { category, venue_id }`. (b) checkin_extend: the impression is already in ground truth. Every check-in schedules the reminder (courtCheckin.ts:80 -> :85-94) and the only UI caller passes no minutes (CourtCheckinCard.tsx:89, default 120 at courtCheckin.ts:139), so the studio can read scheduled = venue_checkins rows, extended = expires_at - checked_in_at > 120 min, checked out = checked_out_at < expires_at, lapsed = checked_out_at is null and expires_at < now, all from migration_venue_checkins.sql:18-28. The event adds only platform (web check-ins get no reminder: localSchedule.ts:16 returns false off-native); if kept, it MUST carry `checkin_id` because setCurrent re-schedules on every refresh and every extend (courtCheckin.ts:129,168), so the studio dedupes to the last per checkin_id. Mark this part a studio-lane computation first, event second. (c) court_checkin: `postLocalNotice` returns void (expo-foundation actions.ts:15-36), so 'shown' cannot be conditioned on success; emit it unconditionally after the call and label it 'posted'. The funnel also has no top: arrival alerts are opt-in per device in AsyncStorage only (courtGeofence.ts:23,141), with zero server trace, so a zero in push.local_shown(court_checkin) cannot distinguish 'nobody opted in' from 'fences never fired'. Add `push.local_alerts_toggled { on: boolean, fenced: number }` from setCourtAlertsEnabled (courtGeofence.ts:139-147, `fenced` is its return value). (d) play_start: denominator exists in drill_sessions/matches within 7d (data/playSessions.ts:65-93) but scheduling only runs when PlayIsland mounts (PlayIsland.tsx:33), so push.local_scheduled(play_start, source_id, play_kind) carries real information; keep it. Drop the proposed play_sessions.source column: play_sessions has no origin (migration_play_sessions.sql:14-23) but the button press itself is the origin signal, so `push.opened { kind: 'play_start', category, outbox_id: null, source_id }` from handleNotificationAction (notificationActions.ts:189-200) joined to play_sessions.source_id within 5 min answers the question. (e) Reusing push.opened is correct, not a duplicate: add 'pickleague' to its apps (events.json:377-384) and note outbox_id null means local.

### 22. [MEDIUM] `pickleague_push_pref_changes_blind` — Category mutes emit nothing and the preference blob keeps no history, so suppression is only visible as outbox error text
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Which categories do people mute, how soon after enabling push, and how many match-confirm pushes were suppressed because notifyMatchConfirms was off (the exact failure migration_match_confirm_notify_category.sql:3-16 was written to stop)?

**Evidence:** userPreferences.ts:100-118 saveUserPreferences upserts the whole prefs JSONB (migration_add_user_preferences.sql:12-16, updated_at only, no per-key history); SettingsScreen.tsx:471-525 seven notify* ToggleRows and :463 pushEnabled call savePrefs with no track. send-push/index.ts:67-76 resolvePrefs reads the blob at send time; a muted category is recorded only as outboxDispatch.ts:198 status='sent', error='suppressed by preference' - the same status as a real send. The foundation prefs template (expo-foundation server/sql/notification_prefs.template.sql:16-23) is per-kind rows with updated_at, which pickleague does not use.

**Proposal:** NEW push.pref_changed { key: pushEnabled | notifyMatchResults | notifyMatchConfirms | notifyEventReminders | notifyLeagueUpdates | notifyTournamentUpdates | notifyChallenges | notifyDrillRequests, enabled: boolean } emitted from SettingsScreen savePrefs by diffing prev vs next (keys are the fixed Prefs enum, userPreferences.ts:26-36). Denominator: push.token_registered users (a mute only matters once push is on). The studio lane in pickleague_push_outbox_unread should count 'suppressed by preference' as its own status column, never inside 'sent'.

**Ground truth already available:** Current state: user_preferences.prefs (pushEnabled + 7 keys). Suppressed sends: push_outbox where status='sent' and error='suppressed by preference', grouped by category. No change history.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Title: Preference toggles emit nothing and the prefs blob keeps no history, so mute TIMING is invisible (current mute state and suppressed sends are answerable from tables). Proposal: NEW push.pref_changed { key: <Prefs enum key, userPreferences.ts:26-36>, enabled: boolean } from SettingsScreen.tsx savePrefs (:111) by diffing previous vs next; emit only for boolean keys (skip defaultMatchType/defaultScoreLimit). Denominator: NOT push.token_registered (doggle-only, config/events.json:370-376; pickleague has no push.* emitter) - use public.push_tokens rows per user (migration_push_notifications.sql:24-31) or prefs.pushEnabled=true, or pair with a pickleague push-lifecycle emitter gap if one is filed. Studio-lane part (not app instrumentation): current mute distribution from user_preferences.prefs; suppressed count from push_outbox where error='suppressed by preference' as its own status column, never counted in 'sent' (outboxDispatch.ts:198).
- *ground-truth:* Split the gap. (a) Studio lane (NOT an app gap): read user_preferences (user_id, prefs->>'pushEnabled', prefs->>'notifyMatchResults' ... prefs->>'notifyDrillRequests', updated_at) for the current mute distribution, with denominator = users having pushEnabled=true (or a row in push_tokens), and read push_outbox (recipient_id, category, status, error, created_at) grouped by category where error='suppressed by preference' as its own column, never inside 'sent'; state inline that this conflates master-off with category-mute and covers 30 days only (push_prune). (b) Server-side fix, preferred over an app event for the suppression count: outboxDispatch.ts:198 / send-push resolvePrefs should write a distinct fixed-enum error ('suppressed: push disabled' vs 'suppressed: <category key>') so the outbox itself answers the match-confirm question; no client emitter needed and it cannot be lost to a crash. (c) App event remains justified ONLY for the timing/history question: push.pref_changed { key: <Prefs enum key>, enabled: boolean } from SettingsScreen savePrefs by diffing prev vs next (SettingsScreen.tsx:111-113 already holds `previous` and `next`), with denominator = push_tokens / pushEnabled=true in ground truth, not push.token_registered (not emitted by pickleague). ### 23. [LOW] `doggle_email_alert_click_blind` — Alert emails are a sent ledger with no click signal: the web ?alert= arrival navigates and records nothing, and the link carries no outbox id
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Does anyone click the dog-news / place-comment emails, and is email worth keeping as a channel next to push?

**Evidence:** Email rows are written by the same outbox writers (0060:193-197, 0081:84-90) and marked sent (index.ts:379-387, 422). The CTA URL is ?alert=<deepLink> for dog/, place/ and google/ links only, bare site otherwise (index.ts:107-116); no outbox id or kind in the URL. Web client reads ?alert and calls navigateDeepLink only (app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:486-490); no track(). page.view that follows cannot be told from an organic visit because landingCampaign allowlists only code/utm_* (analytics.ts:116).

**Proposal:** Dispatcher: append &n=<outboxId> to ctaUrl (index.ts:107-116) for every link, including the bare-site fallback. Web client: when ?alert or ?n is present, emit alert.email_clicked {outbox_id: uuid, alert_kind: enum from the outbox key prefix passed as &k=} before navigating (PushNotificationContext.tsx:486-490), and strip n/k from the landing route. Denominator: outbox email rows with sent_at; outcome: alert.email_clicked joined on outbox_id. Add email_clicked to the push lane next to push.opened.

**Ground truth already available:** public.alert_notification_outbox where channel='email' (sends only)

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Gap stands; two narrowings. (a) The proposal says "add email_clicked to the push lane next to push.opened" - no such lane exists yet: nothing in analytics-studio/scripts reads alert_notification_outbox, so the denominator (email rows with sent_at) and the push.opened/outbox pairing noted at config/events.json:1232 are both an unwritten studio lane, not just an emitter gap. (b) Alternative that needs no client event: Resend supports per-email click tracking and `tags` (e.g. tag outbox_id, alert_key) plus email.clicked webhooks; index.ts:166-178 sends none today. Either path is valid, but if the ?n= client route is chosen, the bare-site fallback at index.ts:115 must also carry it (it covers every alert_key that is not dog/, place/, google/, e.g. DMs from 0081:86-99), and navigateDeepLink (PushNotificationContext.tsx:49) handles dog/place/meetup/group but the dispatcher also emits google/ links, so the kind enum should come from the outbox alert_key prefix, not the deep link prefix.
- *ground-truth:* Minor refinement only: the proposal need not pass &k=<alert_kind> - alert_key is already on the outbox row (0060:58), so alert.email_clicked {outbox_id} joined on alert_notification_outbox.id yields the kind server-side; carrying only &n=<outboxId> keeps the URL minimal. Alternative with no client event: Resend send at index.ts:174-180 could add tags:[{name:'outbox_id',value:id}] and enable click tracking, with a new Resend webhook Edge Function stamping a new clicked_at column on the outbox - but that is a product-code change plus a schema column, not an existing table, so the gap stands.
- *worth-it:* Title: Alert emails are a sent ledger with no click signal; the web ?alert= arrival navigates and records nothing, and the link carries no outbox id. Proposal (preferred, ground-truth first): record the click in the outbox, not the stream. Dispatcher appends `&n=<outbox uuid>` only (no `k`) to ctaUrl for every branch including the bare-site fallback (index.ts:107-116). Add `clicked_at timestamptz` to public.alert_notification_outbox (new migration) and a service-role-only RPC `mark_alert_email_clicked(p_id uuid)` that sets clicked_at = coalesce(clicked_at, now()) where channel='email'. Client: in a URL handler that runs on BOTH web (window.location) and native (Linking.getInitialURL / addEventListener('url'), the ReferralHost.tsx:50-51 pattern, because app.json:29-33 associated domains can route the link into the app), validate `n` against UUID_RE, call the RPC via an Edge Function with verify_jwt=false (the record-invite-click shape: write-only, returns nothing), then strip n from the route and navigate. Also emit `alert.email_clicked {outbox_id: <uuid>, alert_kind: <enum>}` where alert_kind is resolved client-side from the deepLink prefix only (dog|place|google|other), never from a URL param. Denominator: outbox rows where channel='email' and status='sent' and alert_key not like 'test:%' (0064:28), bucketed by day of sent_at via dayExpr; outcome: same rows with clicked_at not null, grouped by split_part(alert_key, ':', 1). This is a NEW studio lane (no existing push lane reads the outbox); build it as the doggle notifications lane that reads outbox push sends beside push.opened and email sends beside clicked_at in one query, with the exclusionCte applied to recipient_id and the excluded count stated. Name check: `alert.email_clicked` introduces area word `alert`, which is not yet in config/events.json; `push.*` is the only notification area and would be a lie for email, and `alert` is the product's own term (alert_notification_outbox, alert_key, ?alert=), so accept the new area, register it in taxonomy with apps:["doggle"], stage engagement, and note in _doc that it is channel=email counterpart of push.opened. Props are a uuid and a 4-value enum; no free text, no PII.
