### 24. [LOW] `doggle_push_opened_double_count` — Production builds can record one tap as two push.opened rows and run the action twice, because the live listener and the launch replay both feed the handler with no dedupe
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-push · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Is a push.opened count on main builds an opens count or an opens-plus-replays count?

**Evidence:** app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:481-484: both addNotificationResponseReceivedListener(handleNotificationResponse) and getLastNotificationResponseAsync().then(handleNotificationResponse) are wired; handleNotificationResponse has no request.identifier/action memory and never calls clearLastNotificationResponseAsync, so the launch response is replayed on later provider mounts. The foundation router exists precisely to fix this (expo-foundation src/platform/push/router.ts:88-100: in-memory Set + AsyncStorage handled list + clearLastNotificationResponseAsync) and the work order names it as the reason for Phase 0b/2 (docs/foundation/port-live-features-work-order.md Phase 0b step 2, Phase 2 step 2), but it is only on next-build, which has shipped no binary. No rows exist yet to measure the inflation, so this is plausible from the code rather than observed.

**Proposal:** No new event. In the push lane, collapse push.opened rows that share session_id, kind and a ts within 2 s into one, and print the collapsed count next to the raw one with app_version (already on analytics_sessions) so the main-vs-next-build split is visible; drop the collapse once a next-build binary is the majority. Promote to fixed when a next-build build is live and the raw and collapsed counts agree.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Title: "Doggle main cold-start taps can record one push.opened as two rows (listener + getLastNotificationResponseAsync replay, no dedupe) and run check-in/extend actions twice; fixed only on next-build via wireNotificationResponses, which has shipped no binary." Proposal: no new app event. There is no studio push lane yet (only scripts/events.mjs reads push.*), so when one is added, collapse push.opened rows sharing session_id and kind within 2 s and print raw vs collapsed per app_version (analytics_sessions.app_version is already selected in scripts/events.mjs:76; main is app.json version 1.0.0, next-build 1.0.1). The row count is currently zero (data/events.json coverage.neverFired includes push.opened), so this is a precondition for reading push.opened honestly, not an observed inflation. Promote to fixed when a next-build binary is live and raw == collapsed for its app_version.
- *ground-truth:* Keep as stated. One refinement to the collapse rule: a replay from getLastNotificationResponseAsync fires on every provider mount in the same process, not only at launch, so a late remount can replay the tap well past 2 s. Collapse on (session_id, kind) to the FIRST row per distinct notification where possible; since props carry no request id, fall back to session_id+kind within a short window and state the residual as a caveat. The outbox (alert_notification_outbox: recipient_id, channel, alert_key, sent_at) remains the send-side denominator for the push lane.
- *worth-it:* Title: Production (main) builds can emit two push.opened rows and run the notification action twice for one cold-start tap, because the live listener and getLastNotificationResponseAsync both feed handleNotificationResponse with no dedupe and no clearLastNotificationResponseAsync (PushNotificationContext.tsx:481-484, 185-245); fixed on next-build via wireNotificationResponses (router.ts:74-110) but no next-build binary has shipped. Proposal (studio-lane, no new event, no new props): when the doggle push lane is written (it does not exist yet; events.mjs only counts push.opened in coverage), read push.opened beside alert_notification_outbox rows with channel='push' and status='sent' (db/migrations/0060_alert_notifications.sql:54-67) as the denominator, and collapse push.opened rows sharing session_id + props.kind with ts within 2 s into one. Print raw and collapsed side by side, split by analytics_sessions.app_version (analytics.ts:293), with an inline note that main builds can double-count cold-start taps. Promote to fixed when a next-build binary is live and raw == collapsed for its app_version over 7d; drop the collapse once next-build is the majority of sessions. Also note the second half is a product bug (checkin/extend/checkout action runs twice, lines 215-233), already covered by work order Phase 0b step 2, so the app fix is not a new work-order item.

### 25. [LOW] `foundation_live_session_blind` — createLiveSession and useResumeLiveSession expose no analytics observer, so how a live session was started (island, screen, lock-screen notice) and how often a persisted one is reconciled away is invisible
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (2/3; dissent from worth-it)  
**Lane:** foundation-push · **App:** foundation

**Question it answers:** Of play sessions, how many were started from the 'starting now' notice versus the island or a screen, and how often does the server say 'none' for a session the device still showed?

**Evidence:** createLiveSession.ts:106-157: start/pause/resume/end/clear/restore mutate the store and notify UI subscribers only; LiveSessionOptions (:28-33) has storageKey and getUserId, no onEvent. useResumeLiveSession.ts:16-26 offers onResume/onNone as navigation callbacks; pickleague PlayIsland.tsx:29 passes neither. Pickleague startPlay/endPlay (playSession.ts:66-88) and the play_start lock-screen handler (:164-173) emit nothing; analytics.ts has no play.* names. Doggle at 1.25 does not have /session (UPGRADING.md:144-160, 1.30.0).

**Proposal:** Kit: LiveSessionOptions.onEvent?: (e: { type: 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'end' | 'clear' | 'restored', id: string | null }) => void fired from set()/restore() (createLiveSession.ts:94-104, :139-153), and useResumeLiveSession firing a 'reconciled' { result: 'none' | 'adopted' | 'kept' | 'unreachable' } through the same observer at :45-55. App emits play.start { kind, source } with source enum 'island' | 'screen' | 'notice' (the notice path is playSession.ts:164-173), play.end { seconds }, and play.reconciled { result }. Denominator: play.start per source against play_sessions rows per day; reconciled 'none' against restored sessions. Read play_sessions first for anything that does not need the entry surface.

**Ground truth already available:** pickleague public.play_sessions via start_play_session / end_play_session (data/playSessions.ts:30-42, :50) is ground truth for start, end, kind, source_id and venue_id; only the entry surface and the reconcile outcome are missing.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowings. (1) The source enum is wrong: the island never STARTS a session - PlayIsland.tsx:42-72 offers only Enter score / Stay 1 more hour / End. The only start call sites are CourtCheckinCard.tsx:152 (checked-in, venue known) and :185 (no venue), plus the lock-screen notice handler playSession.ts:171. So play.start { kind, source } with source in 'checkin_card' | 'notice' (optionally with_venue boolean), not 'island' | 'screen' | 'notice'. (2) Drop play.end { seconds }: duration is ground truth (play_sessions.ended_at - started_at, migration_play_sessions.sql:20-21, server-expired at 6h by expire_play_sessions :93-97), so an app event adds nothing. Keep play.reconciled { result } but note getActivePlay (playSession.ts:96-101) returns the local session as 'active' when it is under 10 minutes old and the server has no row, so 'none' only ever means the server disagreed with a session older than the offline-queue grace window; and 'unreachable' needs the kit's catch at useResumeLiveSession.ts:55 to call the observer, which it does not today. Also: a studio lane over play_sessions (starts/ends per day, kind mix, median duration, 6h-expired share) answers most of the question with no app change and should be filed as the first step; the client events are only for entry surface and reconcile outcome.
- *worth-it:* Not an app-instrumentation gap. Reclassify as a studio-lane gap: 'pickleague play_sessions lane' - read public.play_sessions (kind, source_id, venue_id, started_at, ended_at) with exclusionCte(); entry surface = CASE WHEN source_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'notice' ELSE 'checkin_card' END (the island never starts a session; CourtCheckinCard.tsx:152/:185 and playSession.ts:171 are the only startPlay call sites); abandoned = ended_at = started_at + interval '6 hours' (expire_play_sessions cron). Drop the proposed kit onEvent observer and play.start/play.end/play.reconciled events; if a reliability counter for lost queued starts is ever wanted, it belongs beside the offline-queue instrumentation, not here.

### 26. [LOW] `foundation_push_onopened_no_payload` — The kit's onOpened analytics hook passes only a kind string, so an open cannot be attributed to its outbox row through the hook the kit offers
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** foundation

**Question it answers:** Can both apps emit one identical push.opened with an outbox id from the kit's seam, or must each read parsed.data in its own onTap/onAction and keep two emitters that can drift?

**Evidence:** expo-foundation src/platform/push/router.ts:39 `onOpened?: (kind: string) => void`; :102 `opts.onOpened?.(isAction ? parsed.action : 'open')` while parsed.data (holding data.outboxId, stamped by every dispatcher at _kit/outboxDispatch.ts:156) is in scope at :89 and already used for the idempotency key :54. UPGRADING.md:123 documents onOpened as the analytics seam. Doggle's hand-rolled push.opened {kind} (PushNotificationContext.tsx:202-212) has the same hole; pickleague, on the kit, has no hook wired at all (push.ts:198-203). Both apps are meant to converge on this kit.

**Proposal:** Additive kit change: onOpened?: (kind: string, info: { outboxId: string | null; requestId: string | null; data: Record<string, unknown> }) => void (second argument, so existing callers are unchanged). Then pickleague and doggle each pass one onOpened that emits push.opened { kind, category, outbox_id, notification_id } from info, and doggle's hand-rolled handleNotificationResponse moves onto wireNotificationResponses. Category is not in parsed data today; send-push should add category to data at functions/send-push/index.ts:86-92 (it already resolves it at :155) so the client never guesses it from the action id.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow and fix two details. (1) Pickleague is wider than 'no outbox id': it declares NO push.* events at all (no permission_requested/permission_result/token_registered/opened), so for pickleague the gap is 'push.opened absent', with the kit hook being the cheapest fix. (2) Line cites: pickleague send-push already puts notification_id in data at supabase/functions/send-push/index.ts:86-92 (baseData) and the kit dispatcher adds outboxId at outboxDispatch.ts:155, so pickleague opens could carry BOTH ids today; category is resolved per row via categoryFor at send-push/index.ts:189 (resolveButtons :94-166), not :155 (that line is the tournament_registrations query). Doggle's payload carries outboxId + alertKey but no category (dispatch-alert-notifications/index.ts:248), so a category prop for doggle needs a dispatcher change too. Proposal otherwise stands: additive second arg `info: { outboxId, requestId, data }` on onOpened at router.ts:39/:102; doggle moves handleNotificationResponse onto wireNotificationResponses; both apps emit push.opened { kind, outbox_id, notification_id?, category? } from the same hook; studio then joins to alert_notification_outbox / pickleague outbox by id for a sends->opens rate per category.
- *ground-truth:* Evidence path fix only: dispatcher stamps data.outboxId at expo-foundation/server/deno/outboxDispatch.ts:155 (src/_kit/outboxDispatch.ts does not exist). Proposal stands as an app/kit gap; once push.opened carries outbox_id, the studio lane reads it against {{prefix}}_outbox / alert_notification_outbox / push_outbox (id, recipient_id, kind, status, sent_at) as the send denominator.
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap, severity low, with these fixes to the proposal. (a) Kit (additive, router.ts:39/:102): `onOpened?: (kind: string, info: { outboxId: string | null; requestId: string | null; data: Record<string, unknown> }) => void`, called with `{ outboxId: typeof parsed.data.outboxId === 'string' ? parsed.data.outboxId : null, requestId: parsed.requestId, data: parsed.data }`. (b) Emitter contract, both apps, same name and props: `push.opened { kind: <action id | 'open' | 'dismiss'>, category: <catalog id | null>, outbox_id: <uuid | null>, notification_id: <uuid | null> }`. Never spread `info.data` into props: pickleague's data carries `title` (free text, send-push/index.ts:90) and doggle's carries `alertKey`/`dogPlaceId`. Pull the four fields by name only. (c) Pickleague: pass `onOpened` in setupNotificationTapHandling (push.ts:198-203); `notification_id` comes from data.notification_id (send-push/index.ts:87), `category` from data once send-push adds it inside buttonsFor/resolveButtons (index.ts:168-180, where category is already resolved per row) - not at :86-92, which runs before category is known. (d) Doggle: pinned to kit 1.25.0 (package.json:19), so do NOT block on moving to wireNotificationResponses; first change trackPushOpened(kind) (analytics.ts:666) to take `{ kind, outbox_id: data.outboxId ?? null, category: data.alertKey ?? null, notification_id: null }` read in handleNotificationResponse (PushNotificationContext.tsx:185-212, `data` is already read at :222). The kit migration is a separate upgrade item. (e) Denominator (required for the zero to be unambiguous): the impression is the outbox row, ground truth, not a client event - pickleague `push_outbox.status='sent'` joined to `push_deliveries.status='ok'` (migration_push_outbox.sql:28-66), doggle `alert_notification_outbox.status='sent' and channel='push'` (0060_alert_notifications.sql:54-67). Register a studio lane that reads each app's outbox table and joins push.opened on `props.outbox_id = outbox.id` (same project per app), reporting sent / opened / open-rate by kind and category per window, with the exclusion CTE applied to recipient_id. No client 'received' event: a delivered-and-ignored push is indistinguishable from an undelivered one (PushNotificationContext.tsx:198-201). (f) Taxonomy: keep the existing name push.opened (events.json:377), add "pickleague" to its apps list (:380), and also declare push.permission_requested / push.permission_result / push.token_registered for pickleague if it wires the kit's onPermissionAsked/onPermissionResult/onTokenRegistered callbacks (UPGRADING.md 1.32.0), so the open-rate funnel has its top. Promote to fixed only on the first observed pickleague push.opened row carrying a non-null outbox_id that joins to a push_outbox row.

### 27. [LOW] `pickleague_is_read_not_seen` — notifications.is_read means 'tapped in the in-app list', not 'seen', because a push tap never marks the row read
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-push · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** How many notifications were ever acted on, counting push taps and lock-screen buttons, not only list taps?

**Evidence:** NotificationsScreen.tsx:154-155 handleTap -> markRead :93-101 is the only writer of is_read (plus markAllRead :103-112). push.ts:173-185 routeNotification deep-links from a tapped push without touching notifications, though data.notification_id is present (send-push/index.ts:87). HomeScreen.tsx:110 counts unread for the bell badge. Any studio or digest query that reads is_read as engagement would therefore undercount exactly the users push is working for.

**Proposal:** No new event. Product fix in the app repo work order: routeNotification marks notifications.is_read = true by data.notification_id (authenticated update policy exists, migration_add_notifications.sql:25-27) so is_read becomes 'opened by any path'; until that lands, every studio read of is_read states inline that it excludes push taps. Studio-side, 'acted on' = is_read OR a push.opened/push.action_result row with that notification_id.

**Ground truth already available:** notifications.is_read (in-app list only). The push half needs push.opened / push.action_result from the gaps above.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Minor sharpening only: the proposal's studio-side 'acted on' = is_read OR push.opened/push.action_result requires pickleague to first emit push.opened (taxonomy already has the name, apps:["doggle"] - extend apps to pickleague rather than coin a new name) with notification_id as a prop; today pickleague emits zero push events, so until the app-repo fix lands the only honest studio read is is_read labelled 'in-app list taps only'. Lock-screen button presses (notificationActions.ts:95-173) also leave is_read false, so the product fix should mark read in handleNotificationAction as well as routeNotification.
- *ground-truth:* Minor sharpening only: the lock-screen action half is partly answerable from ground truth already - event_slot_votes, event_declines (notificationActions.ts:113,121) and the RPC-backed tables for confirm_match / respond_drill_request / rsvp_drill_session / tournament_respond_to_invite (:132-173) hold one row per action with user_id and timestamp, joinable to push_outbox.entity_id/recipient_id (migration_push_outbox.sql:30-36). Studio-side 'acted on' should be: is_read OR a matching action row in those tables OR push.opened. Only the plain-tap-without-action case truly needs the routeNotification is_read fix (or push.opened). Severity low stands.
- *worth-it:* Keep key/app/severity. Proposal, corrected: (a) Product fix in the pickleague work order: mark `notifications.is_read = true where id = data.notification_id` on BOTH push paths, not only the plain tap - in push.ts setupNotificationTapHandling wrap onTap AND onAction (or use the foundation `onOpened` hook, router.ts:39/:102, which fires once per response before either branch and already receives the action id) - because the gap's own question counts lock-screen buttons, and notificationActions.ts runs with the local session so the authenticated update policy (migration_add_notifications.sql:24-27) applies there too. Fire-and-forget, never block routing or the action. (b) Denominators, stated explicitly: in-app impression = `notifications` rows for the user (created_at in window); push attempt = `push_outbox` rows with status='sent' joined to `push_deliveries` status='ok' (migration_push_outbox.sql:28-63). Report 'acted on' as two rates - acted / notifications rows, and acted / pushes delivered - never as a bare count, so a zero reads as 'delivered but ignored' versus 'never delivered'. (c) Studio-side definition: 'acted on' = `notifications.is_read` once the fix is observed in production; drop the `push.action_result` clause (name does not exist in config/events.json) and drop the `push.opened ... with that notification_id` clause unless the sibling pickleague push.opened gap adds `notification_id` as a prop - today pickleague emits no push.opened (push.ts:196-204 passes no onOpened) and doggle's carries only {kind}. Until the fix is observed, every studio read of is_read states inline: 'in-app list taps only; push taps and lock-screen buttons not counted'. (d) Lock-screen button outcomes already have ground truth (event_slot_votes, event_declines, matches.team1/2_confirmed_by) - count them from those tables, attributed by entity_id, rather than from any new event.

### 1. [HIGH] `doggle_oauth_login_untracked` — OAuth sign-in (Google/Apple) emits neither account.created NOR auth.login - the registered gap understates it
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (2/3; dissent from ground-truth)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** How many real Doggle accounts came in through Google/Apple vs email/phone, and did the printed QR campaigns produce any of them? Today the stream funnel stage 'Created an account' and 'Signed in' both see only password users.

**Evidence:** doggle/app/src/lib/authOAuth.ts has no import of track and no analytics call anywhere (lines 1-132): runProviderSignIn returns { ok: true } at :67 (web redirect) and :86 (native setSession); signInWithAppleNative returns { ok: true } at :126. LoginScreen.tsx:123 and :138 consume those results and call goAfterSignIn() with no track. AppNavigator.tsx:197-205 onAuthStateChange only calls resetSessionUser/endSession - no auth.login on SIGNED_IN. auth.login is emitted ONLY by the password/OTP wrappers in data/auth.ts:44,58,73 and account.created only at :37,51. config/events.json gaps.doggle_oauth_signup_untracked.detail says an OAuth signup 'emits only auth.login plus the session claim' - the code shows no auth.login either. data/events.json apps.doggle.coverage: account.created has zero rows ever and auth.login rows come only from excluded accounts.

**Proposal:** Correct gaps.doggle_oauth_signup_untracked (detail is wrong about auth.login). Fix in authOAuth.ts on each ok path (:86 native, :126 Apple; web redirect lands via INITIAL_SESSION so handle in AppNavigator.tsx:197-205 when event === 'SIGNED_IN' and provider is google|apple): emit auth.login { via: 'google' | 'apple' } and, when session.user.created_at is within 60s of now, account.created { via } (the emitter merges the campaign code itself, analytics.ts:782). Guard against double-emit with the password wrappers by keying on app_metadata.provider. Denominator: page.view route 'Login' (already fires).

**Ground truth already available:** auth.users (created_at, raw_app_meta_data->>'provider') and profiles.created_at answer the COUNT of OAuth signups; only the campaign-code attribution and the per-session funnel need the stream.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Reclassify as a correction to the existing registered gap `doggle_oauth_signup_untracked` (config/events.json:1197), not a new key. Corrected title: "Doggle OAuth sign-in emits neither account.created nor auth.login; only the session claim sees it". Corrected detail: replace "emits only auth.login plus the session claim" with "emits no event at all - the OAuth return (authOAuth.ts:67/:86/:126, AppNavigator.tsx:203-210) is visible solely as the session claim (upgraded_at via resetSessionUser → claimSession, analytics.ts:810-835)". Severity: raise from low to medium (not high): the funnel's "Signed in on that visit" stage already captures OAuth sign-ins through the claim, and the OAuth signup COUNT has ground truth in auth.users raw_app_meta_data->>'provider' + profiles.created_at, so only the per-provider `via` split and the campaign-code attribution on account.created are truly lost. Corrected fix: in AppNavigator.tsx onAuthStateChange, when event === 'SIGNED_IN' and s.user.app_metadata.provider is 'google' or 'apple' (password/OTP wrappers already emit for 'email'/'phone', so keying on provider avoids double-emit), emit auth.login { via: provider } and, when s.user.created_at is within 60s of now, account.created { via: provider } (the campaign code is merged centrally at analytics.ts:782). Denominator already present: page.view route 'Login' (analytics.ts:769). Separate studio-lane item: a provider-split signup count from auth.users is ground truth and needs no app change.
- *ground-truth:* studio lane: read auth.users (id, created_at, is_anonymous, raw_app_meta_data->>'provider', raw_app_meta_data->'providers') or auth.identities (user_id, provider, created_at) for the google|apple|email|phone split, joined to public.analytics_sessions (user_id, landing_route, upgraded_at, started_at) for the QR-code -> account join; feed the doggle funnel 'Signed in' / 'Created an account' stages from analytics_sessions.upgraded_at + auth.users.created_at instead of auth.login / account.created events. Separately amend config/events.json gaps.doggle_oauth_signup_untracked.detail: OAuth sign-in emits NEITHER account.created nor auth.login (authOAuth.ts has no track call; AppNavigator.tsx:203-209 only resets identity) - only the session claim records it; keep severity low.
- *worth-it:* Severity: medium (not high). Title: "OAuth sign-in (Google/Apple) emits neither account.created nor auth.login; registered gap doggle_oauth_signup_untracked wrongly says auth.login fires". Action 1: amend config/events.json:1197-1204 detail to "emits NO stream event at all - only the session claim (upgraded_at)", and keep the funnel caveat. Action 2 (one emitter, not two): do NOT touch authOAuth.ts. In AppNavigator.tsx onAuthStateChange (:203-210), when event === 'SIGNED_IN' and s.user.app_metadata.provider is 'google' or 'apple', emit track('auth.login', { via: provider }) and, if Date.now() - Date.parse(s.user.created_at) < 60_000, track('account.created', { via: provider }) (emit() merges the campaign code at analytics.ts:782). Guard with a module-level once-per-process flag so a re-fired SIGNED_IN (tab refocus on web) does not double-count; the password/OTP wrappers are excluded by the provider check (their provider is 'email'/'phone'). Web redirect and native both reach SIGNED_IN, so this single site covers :67, :86 and :126. Props: via in {'google','apple'} only (matches doggle's existing method-level enum email/phone/email_link; michi's coarser via:'oauth' stays as is, no symmetry requirement across apps). Denominator: page.view {route:'Login'} (already fires). Note for the studio: the campaign lane already attributes OAuth converters through gainedIdentity (campaigns.mjs:371-376); this fix only makes the stream funnel and the sign-in method split honest, it does not change the campaign numbers.

### 2. [HIGH] `doggle_plus_gates_blind` — Doggle+ cap gates and the paywall emit nothing about being hit, dismissed, or converted
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** How often do free users hit the 3-favorites or business-map limit, how many of those open the Doggle+ page, and how many purchases start vs cancel vs fail? Today only the end state (profiles.is_plus) is visible.

**Evidence:** Favorites cap gate: components/FavoriteToggleButton.tsx:58-62 computes isFavoritesKindFull(favorites, kind, plus) (data/favorites.ts:15-19 FREE 3 / PLUS 10) and opens ReplaceFavoriteModal with no track; the server-side refusal path at :69-77 likewise. Business map gate: screens/LocalBusinessesScreen.tsx:179 setGateOpen(true), :186 map tab locked for non-Plus, :342-370 modal with 'PlusUpgrade' navigate at :368 and dismiss at :370 - no track. Paywall: screens/PlusUpgradeScreen.tsx:125 startPlusPurchase() and :147 restorePlusPurchases() return { ok, reason: 'not_configured' | 'cancelled' | 'no_offering' | 'error' } (data/billing.ts:81-85, 124-150) and nothing records the outcome. grep of lib/analytics.ts exports across src finds no call in any of these files. Taxonomy already has cap.gate_shown / cap.gate_dismissed (michi,tcgscan) and iap.purchase_start / iap.purchase_result { product, status } / iap.restore (tcgscan) - doggle declares none. Caveat: EXPO_PUBLIC_PLUS_BILLING_ENABLED defaults false (app/.env.example:56; lib/purchases.ts:70-73, 89), so purchase events may be structurally zero until billing is switched on; the two gates are live regardless.

**Proposal:** Reuse existing names, no new ones: cap.gate_shown { limit: 'favorites_per_kind' | 'business_map', surface: 'place'|'discover'|'home'|'settings' } at FavoriteToggleButton.tsx:60 and LocalBusinessesScreen.tsx:179; cap.gate_dismissed { limit } on ReplaceFavoriteModal onClose and LocalBusinessesScreen.tsx:370; the :368 navigate is the accepted path and is already visible as page.view 'PlusUpgrade' (route registered in config/events.json routes.PlusUpgrade). iap.purchase_start { product: 'plus_monthly' } before startPlusPurchase at PlusUpgradeScreen.tsx:125 and iap.purchase_result { product, status: 'ok'|'cancelled'|'no_offering'|'not_configured'|'error' } from the returned reason; iap.restore { status } at :147. Impression/denominator pairing: page.view PlusUpgrade -> iap.purchase_start -> iap.purchase_result; cap.gate_shown -> page.view PlusUpgrade. Extend _capGatesDoc: for doggle `limit` is a fixed enum because doggle has no tier_caps table.

**Ground truth already available:** profiles.is_plus / plus_until (set by the RevenueCat webhook, data/billing.ts:31-35) answers conversions. Nothing answers gate hits or purchase attempts/cancels.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Gap stands; narrow and widen as follows. (1) The business gate has TWO reasons, not one: LocalBusinessesScreen.tsx:48 FREE_LIMIT=6 directory cap ('list', fired via :155 gated / :199 auto-open / :301 locked footer) and the map lock ('map', :186-187); the call site is openGate() at :177-182, not a bare setGateOpen(true) at :179. `limit` enum must be 'favorites_per_kind' | 'business_list' | 'business_map'. (2) The favorites gate lives in two components: FavoriteToggleButton.tsx:58-62/:69-77 AND FavoriteHeaderButton.tsx:72, :87-89, :98 (same ReplaceFavoriteModal, onClose at :155) - instrument both or the header path stays blind. (3) Two more Plus entry points have no gate modal and are not in the proposal: PlaceDogMatchCard.tsx:86-90 (locked card rendered for every non-Plus viewer, navigate on press) and SettingsScreen.tsx:205. Add cap.gate_shown { limit: 'dog_match', surface: 'place' } on the locked-card render so the PlusUpgrade page.view has a complete denominator by referrer; Settings is a plain nav and page.view suffices. (4) Paywall caveat is stronger than stated: PlusUpgradeScreen.tsx:212 renders the buy button only when plusPurchaseEnabled() && plan (features.ts:19-21 -> isPurchasingAvailable, which is false until EXPO_PUBLIC_PLUS_BILLING_ENABLED=true, purchases.ts:70-73, 89; .env.example:56 defaults false). So 'not_configured' and 'no_offering' are structurally unreachable from the UI and iap.purchase_result status reduces to 'ok' | 'cancelled' | 'error'; iap.* rows will be zero by design until billing is switched on - register them planned:true for doggle and say so in _iapDoc so the zero is read as not-live, not as no-demand. The gates and the page.view PlusUpgrade impression (routes.PlusUpgrade, config/events.json:719) are live today and are the measurable part. (5) groundTruth should name plus_subscriptions (0280_plus_billing.sql) beside profiles.is_plus: it already carries status/last_event/will_renew from the webhook, so post-purchase cancellation and expiry are answered by the product table, not the stream - only gate hits and in-sheet attempt/cancel/fail are the instrumentation gap.
- *ground-truth:* Evidence is right but the business gate has TWO reasons, not one: LocalBusinessesScreen.tsx:177-189 openGate(reason: 'list' | 'map') - 'list' is the free-cap wall on the directory list (FREE_LIMIT, modal copy at :360-364 'You're seeing the top N'), 'map' is the map-tab upsell. The `limit` enum should be 'favorites_per_kind' | 'business_list' | 'business_map' (or carry gateReason) so the two walls are not merged. Also: startPlusPurchase returns ok:true WITH message when the store paid but awaitServerEntitlement timed out (billing.ts, 6x800ms poll) - iap.purchase_result status should split 'ok' from 'ok_pending' so a webhook lag is not read as a clean conversion, and plus_subscriptions.status/will_renew/current_period_end (0280:20-22) is the studio-lane source for conversions and churn, as groundTruth already says.
- *worth-it:* Title: "Doggle+ gates (business list/map, favorites cap) and the paywall emit nothing about being hit, dismissed, or converted". Proposal: add typed emitters in app/src/lib/analytics.ts (house rule at :555-563: names written once, never raw track() at call sites): trackCapGateShown(limit, surface, trigger?), trackCapGateDismissed(limit, via), trackIapStart(product), trackIapResult(product, status), trackIapRestore(status). Events: cap.gate_shown { limit: 'business_list' | 'business_map' | 'favorites_per_kind', surface: 'businesses' | 'place' | 'discover' | 'home' | 'header', trigger: 'auto' | 'tap' } - fire at LocalBusinessesScreen.tsx:177 openGate (limit from reason; trigger 'auto' from :199, 'tap' from :186/:301), at FavoriteToggleButton.tsx:60 and :72 and FavoriteHeaderButton.tsx:88 and :100 (limit 'favorites_per_kind', add kind: FavoriteKind enum from data/favorites.ts:21). cap.gate_dismissed { limit, via: 'navigate' | 'not_now' | 'close' | 'replace' } - LocalBusinessesScreen.tsx:368 via 'navigate', :370 via 'not_now', :342 onRequestClose via 'close'; ReplaceFavoriteModal onReplace via 'replace', onClose via 'close'. Pairing: every gate_shown is closed by exactly one gate_dismissed, so navigate-rate = via:'navigate' / gate_shown with no dependence on page.view ordering; passive upsell taps at PlaceDogMatchCard.tsx:90 and SettingsScreen.tsx:205 stay as page.view PlusUpgrade only (they are not gates). iap.purchase_start { product: 'plus_monthly' } immediately before PlusUpgradeScreen.tsx:125; iap.purchase_result { product, status: 'ok' | 'ok_pending' | 'cancelled' | 'no_offering' | 'not_configured' | 'error' } from res.reason, 'ok_pending' when res.ok && res.message (billing.ts:113-116, entitlement not yet visible) - never carry res.message; iap.restore { status: 'ok' | 'not_configured' | 'nothing' | 'error' } at :147. Denominators: page.view PlusUpgrade -> iap.purchase_start -> iap.purchase_result (1:1); cap.gate_shown -> cap.gate_dismissed (1:1). ### 3. [HIGH] `doggle_search_blind` — Home and Discover search emit nothing: no query count, no result count, no no-results rate
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Does anyone use search, what share of searches return nothing, and did the 2026-10-02 any-word search rewrite change the no-results rate? Today there is no number before or after the rewrite.

**Evidence:** components/HomeGlobalSearch.tsx:116-151 runs searchDogPlaces(q, {limit: 8}) and searchPeopleAndDogs(q, 8) per debounced query (MIN_QUERY_LEN = 2 at :38) and computes placeHits/peopleHits/dogHits plus client-filtered groups/meetups (:153-170, hasResults at :165) - no track call in the file. Search opens at :221 setOpen(true) - no track. components/walk/DiscoverPlacesMapPanel.tsx:279 saveLastPlaceSearch(...) writes profiles.last_place_search, a single overwritten jsonb (db/migrations/0104:7, lib/lastPlaceSearch.ts:34-38) - it keeps the LAST search only. The RPC rewrite f98461a (db/migrations/0402_search_dog_places_words.sql) logs nothing server-side. Taxonomy has card.search (michi,tcgscan) and search.no_results (michi, failure:true); doggle declares neither.

**Proposal:** search.run { surface: 'home' | 'discover', places, people, dogs, groups, meetups } (all COUNTS; never the query text) fired once per debounced query >= MIN_QUERY_LEN at HomeGlobalSearch.tsx:131-136 after both results settle, and at the Discover places search where DiscoverPlacesMapPanel.tsx:279 saves the search. Reuse search.no_results { surface } when every count is 0 (add doggle to its apps). Denominator: search.open { surface } at HomeGlobalSearch.tsx:221 so 'opened search, typed nothing' is countable; search.run itself is the denominator for the no-results rate. Split any before/after comparison on 2026-10-02 (0402 deploy).

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the Discover surface. DiscoverPlacesMapPanel.tsx:279 saveLastPlaceSearch is a map center/radius change (fires on 'use my location' :298, address pick :321 and radius chip :440), not a place-name search, so it should not emit search.run - at most discover.area_change { source: 'gps'|'address'|'radius' }, a different question. The real second caller of the 0402 RPC is src/lib/placeSearch.ts:8 searchDogPlacesAsCourtResults (VenuePicker localSearch for walk/meetup place pickers, limit 6), so surface should be 'home' | 'picker' rather than 'home' | 'discover'. Keep search.run { surface, places, people, dogs, groups, meetups } at HomeGlobalSearch.tsx:131-136 after both results settle, search.open { surface } at :221, and add doggle to search.no_results apps. Drop the before/after 2026-10-02 split as a goal: there is no pre-rewrite data anywhere (no emitter, no server log, last_place_search holds one jsonb), so only a post-rewrite baseline is possible.
- *ground-truth:* Path fix: the cited file is app/src/components/HomeGlobalSearch.tsx (not components/HomeGlobalSearch.tsx); line cites otherwise verified (MIN_QUERY_LEN :38, effect :114-151, hasResults :165, setOpen(true) :221). Proposal refinement: DiscoverPlacesMapPanel.tsx:279 `persist()` saves a geocoded location + radius, not a text query, and has no result counts in scope, so the Discover emission should be a different shape - search.run { surface:'discover', radius_m, places } fired where the radius query resolves, or dropped from this gap and left as a 'home' surface-only proposal. The '2026-10-02 (0402 deploy)' date was not verified against git log in this check; verify before splitting before/after on it.
- *worth-it:* Title: 'Doggle text search (Home + three place pickers) emits nothing: no query count, no result count, no no-results rate'. Drop 'discover' from the proposal - DiscoverPlacesMapPanel is a radius browse already covered by discover.tab. Events: (1) search.open { surface: 'home' } at HomeGlobalSearch.tsx:221 (the only surface with a discrete open). (2) search.run { surface: 'home' | 'plan_day' | 'place_picker' | 'quick_meetup', places: int, people: int, dogs: int, groups: int, meetups: int } fired once per settled debounced query >= MIN_QUERY_LEN: Home after Promise.all resolves at HomeGlobalSearch.tsx:131 (compute groups/meetups counts there from the same matchesQuery filter used at :153-162; people/dogs/groups/meetups are 0 on non-home surfaces); PlanDayScreen.tsx:659 after rows; DogPlaceGooglePicker.tsx:73 and lib/placeSearch.ts:13 after hits (thread a surface arg through searchDogPlacesAsCourtResults' opts). (3) search.no_results { surface } only when the run SUCCEEDED and every count is 0 - add 'doggle' to its apps in events.json (its michi prop is kind; doggle's is surface). (4) search.failed { surface, rpc: 'places' | 'people' } (failure: true) emitted from the catch branches at HomeGlobalSearch.tsx:130 and :137 and PlanDayScreen.tsx:661 INSTEAD of counting them as zero - this is what keeps 'nothing matched' distinct from 'the RPC died'. Never the query text, never the label/address from last_place_search. Denominators: search.open -> search.run (home only) measures typed-nothing; search.run -> search.no_results measures the no-results rate; search.failed sits beside it. Drop the 2026-10-02 before/after claim: no emitter existed before f98461a, so that comparison can never be made; the value is prospective (next rewrite, and the Google Places key decision at DiscoverPlacesMapPanel.tsx:419-420). Add the new names to doggle's apps list in events.json and to the doggle emitter wrappers in src/lib/analytics.ts next to trackDiscoverTab (:751).

### 4. [MEDIUM] `doggle_onboarding_permission_asks_mislabeled` — Onboarding's permission asks are recorded as 'settings' (push) or not at all (location); PromptSurface 'onboarding' has no caller
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** What is the grant rate for push and for location when asked during onboarding, as opposed to from Settings or the walk flow? Today onboarding push asks are pooled into 'settings' and onboarding location asks are invisible, so the notify step cannot be evaluated.

**Evidence:** lib/analytics.ts:577-584 defines PromptSurface including 'onboarding', and lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:268-269 says 'Onboarding and settings are not the same ask and must not share a grant rate'. But requestPushPermission(surface) is called with exactly two literals: 'app' at :389 (auto-register when prefs.push_alerts_enabled) and 'settings' at :503 inside enablePushAlerts(), which takes no surface argument. enablePushAlerts() is called from BOTH screens/OnboardingScreen.tsx:323 (the 'notify' step CTA, :722) and components/AlertDeliveryPanel.tsx:72 (Settings) - so onboarding asks are labelled 'settings'. Location: OnboardingScreen.tsx:27 imports requestForegroundLocationPermission from @just-messin-around/expo-foundation/platform and calls it at :324, bypassing lib/currentLocation.ts:31-38 where trackLocationPermissionRequested/Result live (grep: the only location trackers are currentLocation.ts:33-38 and favoriteGeofenceTask.ts:71-88) - the onboarding location ask emits nothing. Overlaps the push lane for the push half; the location half and the funnel reading are product.

**Proposal:** No new names. Thread surface through: enablePushAlerts(surface: PromptSurface) with 'onboarding' from OnboardingScreen.tsx:323 and 'settings' from AlertDeliveryPanel.tsx:72. Route the onboarding location ask through a wrapper that emits location.permission_requested / location.permission_result ('foreground') and add a `surface` prop (PromptSurface enum) to both location events so onboarding vs walk vs geofence asks read separately. Pairing already exists: requested -> result; the onboarding denominator is walkthrough.step 'notify' once doggle_onboarding_steps_blind lands.

**Ground truth already available:** push_tokens / notification preferences rows (push_alerts_enabled) answer the end state for push only; nothing records the ask or the location answer.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the comparison and widen the location half. (1) The walk flow is NOT a tracked comparator: components/walk/WalkTrackPanel.tsx:344 and components/walk/DiscoverPlacesMapPanel.tsx:285 also call expo-foundation's untracked requestForegroundLocationPermission directly, so every user-initiated foreground location ask in doggle (onboarding, walk start with GPS, discover 'use my location') emits nothing; location.permission_requested/result today fire only from passive favorite-geofence paths (currentLocation.ts via useNearbyFavoritePlaces/passiveFavoriteService/gpsTestMonitor, and favoriteGeofenceTask.ts). Title should read: 'Onboarding push asks are labelled settings; every user-initiated location ask (onboarding, walk, discover) bypasses the location trackers'. (2) The proposal must also add `surface` to push.permission_result (analytics.ts:676 takes only result/reason), since requested and result are separate rows with no shared key; otherwise the per-surface grant rate is still a session-ordering inference. (3) The wrapper for location should replace all three foundation call sites (OnboardingScreen:324, WalkTrackPanel:344, DiscoverPlacesMapPanel:285), passing surface 'onboarding' | 'walk' | 'discover', and keep currentLocation.ts/favoriteGeofenceTask.ts on a passive surface so a background re-check is never pooled with a person's answer.
- *worth-it:* Title: Permission asks carry no entry-point attribution: onboarding push asks are labelled 'settings', and onboarding AND walk-flow location asks emit nothing (they call expo-foundation's untracked helper). Proposal (no new names, props only): (a) enablePushAlerts(surface: PromptSurface) in lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:502, passing 'onboarding' from screens/OnboardingScreen.tsx:323 and 'settings' from components/AlertDeliveryPanel.tsx:72; (b) add `surface` to push.permission_result (analytics.ts:647) and thread it through every trackPushPermissionResult call in requestPushPermission (:273,:284,:294,:297), the auto-register path (:394, surface 'app') and enablePushAlerts (:506) so the grant rate is a props rollup on one event, not a sequence join; (c) add a doggle-side wrapper in lib/currentLocation.ts, e.g. requestForegroundLocation(surface: PromptSurface): Promise<boolean>, that checks getForegroundPermissionsAsync first (same already-granted guard as currentLocation.ts:31-32 so re-checks are not counted as asks), emits location.permission_requested { scope: 'foreground', surface } and location.permission_result { scope, surface, result, reason? }, then delegates to the foundation helper; route OnboardingScreen.tsx:324 ('onboarding'), WalkTrackPanel.tsx:344 ('walk'), DiscoverPlacesMapPanel.tsx:285 ('discover') and the existing currentLocation.ts:33-38 path ('home' or 'app' for the passive favourites hook) through it, and pass surface 'app' from favoriteGeofenceTask.ts:71-88; (d) in the studio, declare props on the four events at config/events.json:356-397 ({ surface } on requested, { surface, result, reason } on push result, { scope, surface } / { scope, surface, result, reason } on location) and register this as a doggle gap with status open, direction 'push grant rate by surface is a blend; location grant rate undercounts every ask raised from onboarding or the walk flow'. Pairing: requested only fires when the OS sheet is actually raised (PushNotificationContext.tsx:289-291, currentLocation.ts:31-33) and result fires for every ending including 'unavailable', so requested is the denominator and result the numerator per surface; the onboarding step-reach denominator stays dependent on the separate doggle_onboarding_steps_blind finding (OnboardingScreen emits no step event today), but the ask itself is a sufficient impression for the grant-rate question.

<details><summary>Lens verdicts</summary>

- **already-emitted** — real (high): Code confirms the gap and no existing registration covers it. Push half: doggle/app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:271 requestPushPermission(surface) is called with exactly two literals - 'app' at :389 and 'settings' at :503 inside enablePushAlerts(), which has no surface parameter (:502). enablePushAlerts() is called from screens/OnboardingScreen.tsx:323 (enablePermissions, wired to the notify-step CTA at :722) and components/AlertDeliveryPanel.tsx:72, so onboarding asks emit push.permission_requested { surface: 'settings' }. ### 5. [MEDIUM] `doggle_onboarding_steps_blind` — The 9-step onboarding emits nothing per step; only start (account.created) and finish (profiles.onboarded) exist
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Where do new accounts drop out of onboarding - the profile/location step, the dog form, or the notify step - and how many skip adding a dog? Today a new account that never reaches Home is indistinguishable from one that quit on step 2.

**Evidence:** screens/OnboardingScreen.tsx:71 STEPS = ['welcome','how','profile','dog','matchA','matchB','traits','notify','done']; next/back at :210-224 (goTo), skipDog at :314-316 jumps to 'notify', createDogAndFinish :331-371 ends with markOnboarded() (:371 -> data/profiles.ts:136 rpc mark_onboarded). No track import or call anywhere in the file. account.created (data/auth.ts:37,51) marks the start only for password signups (see doggle_oauth_login_untracked). Taxonomy has walkthrough.shown { walkthrough, width, tier } / walkthrough.step { walkthrough, step } / walkthrough.done { walkthrough, via, last_step } (michi, _walkthroughDoc) - doggle declares none.

**Proposal:** Reuse the michi vocabulary: walkthrough.shown { walkthrough: 'onboarding' } once on mount; walkthrough.step { walkthrough: 'onboarding', step: <STEPS index or key enum> } on every goTo/setStep (:210-224); walkthrough.done { walkthrough: 'onboarding', via: 'complete' | 'skipped_dog' | 'abandoned', last_step } at :371 and on skipDog, with the abandon path closed out from the lifecycle listeners the way openPrompts is (analytics.ts:618-629). Denominator: walkthrough.shown; walkthrough.done/shown is the completion rate. Add doggle to those three names' apps.

**Ground truth already available:** profiles (onboarded flag via mark_onboarded) answers 'finished vs not'; dogs.created_at answers 'added a dog'. Nothing answers which step was last.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow and fix the evidence: (1) The DENOMINATOR already exists: page.view { route: 'Onboarding' } fires on every entry to the screen (analytics.ts:765-773 via AppNavigator.tsx:174) and is already observed (studio data/events.json:9497, reports/events.md:457 shows route Onboarding = 4). It covers OAuth signups too, so 'start is only visible for password signups' is wrong - account.created is not the start marker, page.view is. What is missing is only walkthrough.step and walkthrough.done. If walkthrough.shown is still emitted for symmetry with michi, it must carry an entry prop ('signup' | 'legacy' | 'settings'), because the screen is also reached from SettingsScreen.tsx:337 (re-run) and AppNavigator.tsx:299 (legacy accounts with no flag), so unlike michi's shown it is not once-per-person. (2) markOnboarded is called from finish() at OnboardingScreen.tsx:368-371 (the 'done' step CTA at :725), NOT from createDogAndFinish, which ends with goTo('notify') at :360 and on failure leaves the user on 'traits' (:362). So walkthrough.done { via: 'complete' } belongs in finish(), and 'skipped_dog' is a property of the path (skipDog :314), not a separate done - propose walkthrough.done { via: 'complete' | 'abandoned', dog: 'added' | 'skipped', last_step } instead; the abandon close-out from the AppState lifecycle listeners (analytics.ts:618-629 pattern) is still required or every exit reads as abandoned. (3) Two adjacent mislabels on the notify step that the fix should carry: enablePushAlerts() (OnboardingScreen.tsx:323) hardcodes requestPushPermission('settings') (PushNotificationContext.tsx:502-503), so the onboarding push ask is recorded with surface 'settings' - PromptSurface 'onboarding' (analytics.ts:584) is declared and never emitted; and the location ask at :324 calls expo-foundation's requestForegroundLocationPermission directly (expo-foundation/src/platform/location.ts:120), bypassing trackLocationPermissionRequested (only wired in lib/currentLocation.ts:33 and lib/favoriteGeofenceTask.ts:71,83), so the onboarding location ask emits nothing. Corrected title: 'Doggle onboarding emits page.view on entry and nothing per step; which of the 9 steps a new account quit on is invisible, and the notify step's permission asks are mis-surfaced as settings / untracked.'
- *ground-truth:* Minor tightening only: (1) groundTruth should name profiles.onboarded_at (0165_onboarded_at.sql:6), not a boolean "onboarded flag", and add two more coarse proxies the studio can read today - profiles.first_name/neighborhood non-null = passed the profile step, user_push_tokens row = passed notify (0060_alert_notifications.sql:30); (2) note that public.onboarding_progress (0117_onboarding.sql:10) is a DIFFERENT surface (Home Getting-started checklist, claim-gated) and must not be used as the wizard denominator; (3) the enum for step must be the STEPS key, and via must include 'skipped_dog' fired from skipDog (OnboardingScreen.tsx:314-316) separately from 'complete' at :369.
- *worth-it:* Severity: low (4 signups/30d; Onboarding route users == Home route users in the 30d stream). Title: "Doggle onboarding's 9 steps emit nothing between page.view{Onboarding} and onboarded_at; ground truth sees profile/dog/done only". Ground truth: profiles.first_name + home coords (saveProfileAndNext :255-290) = passed 'profile'; dogs.created_at = completed dog sub-steps; profiles.onboarded_at (0165) = done. Blind: welcome->how, matchA/matchB/traits individually, notify. Proposal (doggle emits, add 'doggle' to the three names' apps at config/events.json:499-518): walkthrough.shown { walkthrough:'onboarding' } once per mount of OnboardingScreen (no width/tier) - DOCUMENT in _walkthroughDoc that doggle's .shown is per-attempt, not once-per-person, so denominators use distinct users; walkthrough.step { walkthrough:'onboarding', step:<STEPS key enum: welcome|how|profile|dog|matchA|matchB|traits|notify|done> } from a single effect on `step` change (covers goNext/goBack/goTo :209-225 in one place) ; walkthrough.done { walkthrough:'onboarding', via:'complete', last_step:'done', dog_added:0|1 } in finish() :369-377 only. Drop via:'skipped_dog' (skipDog :314 is a step transition, visible as step 'dog'->'notify' with dog_added:0). Drop via:'abandoned' and the lifecycle close-out: backgrounding does not end onboarding (AppNavigator.tsx:288-299 re-routes until onboarded_at; step state :119 resets to welcome), so abandon = a .shown with no .done, last_step = max walkthrough.step per user; re-entry shows up as a second .shown. Completion rate = distinct users with .done / distinct users with .shown, cross-checked against profiles.onboarded_at. Two one-line fixes to ride along: enablePushAlerts should take a PromptSurface and OnboardingScreen :323 pass 'onboarding' (today PushNotificationContext.tsx:503 hardcodes 'settings'); OnboardingScreen :27/:324 should call the tracked wrapper in lib/currentLocation.ts instead of expo-foundation directly so location.permission_requested { scope:'foreground' } fires from the notify step.

### 6. [MEDIUM] `doggle_ota_banner_blind` — The OTA 'restart to update' banner emits nothing when shown, restarted or dismissed, and no OTA id is recorded anywhere
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** After an OTA ships, how many sessions see the restart nudge, how many restart vs tap Later, and how many sessions are still running the stale bundle a day later? Today none of the three is visible.

**Evidence:** doggle/app/src/lib/providers/UpdateNudgeHost.tsx:8-10 renders <UpdateReadyBanner text=... /> only. Installed foundation 1.25.0 copy (app/node_modules/@just-messin-around/expo-foundation/src/ui/UpdateReadyBanner.tsx:20-29) exposes props text/restartLabel/laterLabel/style only; 'Later' is onPress={() => setDismissed(true)} at :54 and Restart is onPress={() => void restart()} at :57 with no callback prop; foundation HEAD 1.36.0 (expo-foundation/src/ui/UpdateReadyBanner.tsx:21-29, 55-58) is the same. grep for expo-updates / Updates.updateId in doggle/app/src and App.tsx returns nothing, and analytics_sessions.app_version is Constants.expoConfig.version (lib/analytics.ts:279,293), which an OTA does not change. config/events.json gaps.doggle_product_blind._whatIsStillNotSeen already notes 'which build a stranded user is on' is a report over app_version - that is true for binaries but not for OTA bundles.

**Proposal:** Foundation-first (doggle/CLAUDE.md rule 2): add onEvent?: (e: 'shown' | 'restart' | 'later') => void to UpdateReadyBannerProps in the expo-foundation repo, publish, bump doggle. In UpdateNudgeHost map it onto EXISTING names: prompt.shown { prompt: 'ota-restart', surface: 'app' } on 'shown', prompt.answered { prompt: 'ota-restart', surface: 'app', response: 'accepted' | 'declined' } on 'restart' | 'later'; an unanswered banner closes as 'abandoned' through abandonOpenPrompts (analytics.ts:618) if trackPromptShown is used. Pairing: shown -> answered. Separately (optional, schema): add update_id to analytics_sessions from expo-updates Updates.updateId so stale-bundle share is a count over session rows.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Gap stands as described. Two narrowings: (1) the same uninstrumented component is mounted in pickleague at pickleague/mobile/src/navigation/AppNavigator.tsx:21,400 (<UpdateReadyBanner text="A Pickleague update is ready..." />), so the foundation-first onEvent prop fixes both apps and the pickleague host should map it onto pickleague's own vocabulary in the same change; (2) foundation already ships a sibling helper for 'how fast do people update after a release' (expo-foundation/src/platform/appVersion.ts:66-80 getInstallTime, and getNativeBuild :43 for the real native build number), but neither is in the installed 1.25.0 copy (no appVersion.ts under node_modules/.../src/platform) and neither reads the OTA Updates.updateId - so the update_id-on-analytics_sessions part of the proposal is still a new addition, best placed in foundation's platform/updates.ts next to usePendingUpdate rather than in doggle alone. Emit 'shown' once per pending bundle (guard on isUpdatePending transition), not once per render.
- *worth-it:* Keep the event names (prompt.shown / prompt.answered, prompt='ota-restart', surface='app', response accepted|declined|abandoned) but change the foundation contract so the restart answer can land before the reload. expo-foundation src/ui/UpdateReadyBanner.tsx: add `onShown?: () => void` (call from a useEffect keyed on `pending` becoming true, so it fires once per banner appearance, not per render), `onLater?: () => void` (call before setDismissed(true)), and `onBeforeRestart?: () => void | Promise<void>` which the Restart handler AWAITS with a bounded timeout (e.g. Promise.race against 1500ms) before calling restart() - the loading state already covers the wait. doggle UpdateNudgeHost.tsx: onShown -> trackPromptShown('ota-restart','app'); onLater -> trackPromptAnswered('ota-restart','app','declined'); onBeforeRestart -> an awaitable variant of trackPromptAnswered('ota-restart','app','accepted') (add `trackPromptAnsweredAsync` in analytics.ts that returns emit()'s insert promise instead of discarding it; same name/props, so the taxonomy is unchanged). Unanswered + backgrounded still closes as 'abandoned' via abandonOpenPrompts (analytics.ts:618) with no new code. Denominator for 'how many sessions were offered the restart' is prompt.shown; 'how many sessions after an OTA publish never saw it' needs the second half: add nullable `update_id uuid` to doggle analytics_sessions (migration) and set it from `Updates.updateId` (guarded require, null when expo-updates is disabled/dev/web) next to app_version at analytics.ts:293, then extend the studio select at analytics-studio/scripts/events.mjs:76 to read it. Stale-bundle share is then count(sessions where update_id != latest published id) over sessions, per day - a count over rows, no new event. Severity stays medium.

### 7. [MEDIUM] `doggle_place_page_funnel_blind` — Place pages record only last_visited_at per person; view counts, anonymous views and view-to-action per place are invisible
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** Which places get looked at most, what share of place views turn into a check-in, a walk or directions, and do signed-out visitors land on place pages from search or shares? Today a place with 200 views and one with 2 views look the same if the same five people visited both.

**Evidence:** screens/DogPlaceDetailScreen.tsx:334 recordEntityVisit('place', placeId ?? googlePlaceId) -> data/entityVisits.ts:16-19 rpc record_entity_visit; db/migrations/0139_entity_visits.sql:7-13 PK (profile_id, entity_type, entity_id) with :34-36 'on conflict ... do update set last_visited_at = now()' - one row per person per place, repeat views overwrite, and :31 returns early when auth.uid() is null so signed-out visitors (doggle's public pages) are never recorded. page.view carries only route: name (lib/analytics.ts:769) so 'DogPlaceDetail' views cannot be split by place. Actions on the page have no emitter: 'Walk here' navigate at :214/:291, directions at :222/:300, ShareButton at :227-232, uploadPlacePhoto at :118. The studio reads entity_visits as an activity source only (config/apps.json activitySources; scripts/usage.mjs:141-162).

**Proposal:** place.view { place_id, kind } once per DogPlaceDetail mount (an id, not a location: the person browsed a page) emitted next to :334 so it also fires for signed-out sessions; place.action { place_id, action: 'walk' | 'directions' | 'share' | 'photo' } at :214/:291, :222/:300, :227, :118. Denominator: place.view; join outcomes to dog_place_checkins by place id. Studio-lane alternative for the unique-viewer half now: count entity_visits rows per entity_id with last_visited_at in window.

**Ground truth already available:** dog_place_checkins (check-ins per place), dog_place_photos (uploads per place) answer the OUTCOME side; entity_visits gives unique signed-in viewers per place within a window (last_visited_at) but not counts or anonymous views.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Gap stands as written. Two refinements: (a) checkin.create props are { source, dogs, queued } (analytics.ts:732-734) with no place id, so the stream cannot do per-place view-to-checkin on its own; keep the join to dog_place_checkins.dog_place_id as the proposal says. (b) The key is the place identifier: DogPlaceDetail accepts placeId OR googlePlaceId (DogPlaceDetailScreen.tsx:336) and ids like 'osm:way/38025784' contain '/', while shares use the slug (AppNavigator.tsx:51-55, ShareButton entityKey=place.slug at :230). place.view should carry one canonical key (dog_places.id after fetchDogPlace resolves, falling back to googlePlaceId with kind='unconfirmed') or views arriving via share slug and via in-app id will not join.
- *ground-truth:* Minor: the entity_visits insert passes `placeId ?? googlePlaceId ?? ''` and entityVisits.ts:17 returns early on empty id, so unresolved Google-only places are not recorded even for signed-in users - the proposed place.view should carry { place_id, google_place_id } so those pages count too.
- *worth-it:* Title: 'Place pages record only last_visited_at per signed-in person; view counts and view-to-action per place are invisible, and shared-link arrivals that hit the sign-in wall are uncounted'. Drop the phrase 'doggle's public pages' - place pages are NOT public (dog_places RLS 0014:45); the OG card is. Proposal: (1) place.open { place_id, kind: 'doggle' | 'google' } once per DogPlaceDetail mount next to DogPlaceDetailScreen.tsx:334, fired regardless of auth (signed-in vs signed-out is read from the session row's user_id, no extra prop) - this is the denominator AND the login-wall count. (2) Add place_id to the EXISTING walk.start { dogs } (analytics.ts:689) when WalkTrackPanel started with a place (WalkTrackPanel.tsx:267-283 has dogPlaceId); optional place.walk_click { place_id } at :214/:291 if tap-to-start drop-off is wanted; outcome ground truth = walks.dog_place_id (data/walks.ts:76). (3) place.directions { place_id, ok } at :218-224 and :296-303 (ok = openDirections result, so 'could not open maps' is visible). (4) Share: emit from the shared ShareButton.tsx:39-48, not the place screen - share.link_copied { kind: ShareKind, entity_id, ok } (kind enum from lib/site.ts:14: place|group|meetup|dog|walk|post), reusing michi's share.* area and extending its apps to doggle; one call site covers all five share surfaces. (5) Add place_id to the EXISTING checkin.create { source, dogs, queued } (analytics.ts:733) so view->checkin pairs inside the stream; dog_place_checkins (0028:9) remains ground truth. (6) Drop the photo event: dog_place_photos (0189:27-35) answers it. Studio-lane half unchanged: count entity_visits rows per entity_id with last_visited_at in window for unique signed-in viewers now, stated as 'entities last opened in window', per usage.mjs:156-158.

<details><summary>Lens verdicts</summary>

### 8. [LOW] `doggle_events_join_lane_gap` — Meetup RSVPs and event invites are fully in product tables the studio never reads; the app-side gap is only the view denominator
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** How many meetups are created, how many people RSVP going vs maybe, how many invites are sent vs accepted, and what share of event page views turn into an RSVP? Today the usage lane reports walks and check-ins but no events at all.

**Evidence:** data/meetups.ts:290-306 rsvp() upserts meetup_attendees {status} and :314-320 cancelRsvp() deletes; :141 createMeetup inserts meetups; data/eventInvites.ts:21-23 sendEventInvites -> rpc send_event_invites, :55-56 dismissEventInvite. db/migrations/0231_notify_event_join.sql:7-33 trigger notify_meetup_join on meetup_attendees and :37-80 notify_plan_join on dog_place_checkin_plans enqueue to alert_notification_outbox (push lane). No analytics call in either data file. grep of analytics-studio/scripts for meetup_attendees|event_invites|meetups returns nothing; config/apps.json doggle.activitySources lists dog_place_checkin_plans but no meetup table. page.view 'MeetupDetail' has no meetup id (lib/analytics.ts:769).

**Proposal:** Studio-lane first: add meetups (created), meetup_attendees (joins, by status) and event_invites (sent vs dismissed) to scripts/usage.mjs beside walks/check-ins, excluded via exclusionCte('doggle'). App-side only for the denominator: event.view { meetup_id } on MeetupDetail mount, paired with the meetup_attendees row for the join rate. No RSVP event - the table is authoritative and cancelRsvp deletes the row, so read it as point-in-time.

**Ground truth already available:** meetups, meetup_attendees (status going|maybe), meetup_attendee_dogs, event_invites, dog_place_checkin_plans - all in doggle project repmpqbvexbqmocudvhz.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Three narrowings. (1) 'invites sent vs accepted' is NOT directly in event_invites: responded_at is written only by dismiss_event_invite (db/migrations/0120_event_invites.sql:153); the join-from-invite path calls plain rsvp() (components/UpcomingEventsSection.tsx:191,213) and never touches the invite row, so 'accepted' must be derived by joining event_invites(recipient_id, meetup_id|plan_id) to meetup_attendees / dog_place_checkin_plans; read dismissed=true as the only explicit response. (2) First-time 'going' RSVPs already leave a durable trace the studio reads: trigger on_attendee_award (0003_gamification.sql:86-99) writes points_ledger reason 'Joined a meetup', and points_ledger is in apps.json doggle.activitySources (:24-28) - so RSVP history survives cancelRsvp deletion via that ledger, and the studio lane should count joins from points_ledger reason='Joined a meetup' (durable) alongside meetup_attendees (point-in-time). (3) The denominator is not only MeetupDetail: RSVPs are also taken from the Home events feed (UpcomingEventsSection.tsx:191,213) without visiting MeetupDetail, so a MeetupDetail-mount event.view undercounts the impression; the proposal should pair it with an impression on the feed card (or scope the join-rate question to detail-screen RSVPs only).
- *ground-truth:* Keep real, app=doggle, severity low, but fix the invite metric: studio lane reads event_invites (inviter_id, recipient_id, kind, meetup_id, plan_id, created_at) for SENT, and derives ACCEPTED as exists(meetup_attendees where profile_id=recipient_id and meetup_id=event_invites.meetup_id and joined_at >= event_invites.created_at) for kind='meetup', or exists(dog_place_checkin_plans where profile_id=recipient_id and plan matches place+planned_at) for kind='plan'. Do not report 'dismissed': dismiss_event_invite (0120_event_invites.sql:145-156) is the only writer of dismissed/responded_at and no screen calls dismissEventInvite or listMyEventInvites (defined in app/src/data/eventInvites.ts:50-57, no consumers), so that column is structurally ~0 - a zero that means 'cannot see', not 'did not happen'. Meetup created / RSVP going vs maybe: read meetups (host_id, created_at, start_at) and meetup_attendees (profile_id, status, joined_at) point-in-time, excluding exclusionCte('doggle'); note meetup_attendees has no updated_at so a maybe->going flip is not timestamped. Push sends for joins already land in alert_notification_outbox via _enqueue_push (0105_friend_invite_alerts.sql:18, trigger 0231_notify_event_join.sql:7-36,37-80) - ground truth, no app event needed. App-side remains only the denominator: event.view { meetup_id } on MeetupDetail mount (page.view carries route only, lib/analytics.ts:769; AppNavigator.tsx:438).
- *worth-it:* Title: Meetups, RSVPs and event invites live in doggle product tables the studio never reads; the only app-side gap is a per-meetup view denominator. Proposal (studio-lane, scripts/usage.mjs beside walks/check-ins, all through exclusionCte('doggle'), one fetch per table, windows computed server-side): (1) meetups created: count(meetups) by created_at, excluding excluded hosts (host_id). (2) joins: meetup_attendees by joined_at (NOT created_at), split by status going|maybe, EXCLUDING rows where profile_id = meetups.host_id (QuickMeetupSheet.tsx:106 auto-RSVPs the creator). Read as point-in-time and say inline that cancelRsvp deletes the row so cancels are invisible. (3) invites: event_invites by created_at, split kind meetup|plan, with four outcomes per invite - dismissed (dismissed = true), joined (kind='meetup': exists meetup_attendees where meetup_id = ei.meetup_id and profile_id = ei.recipient_id; kind='plan': exists dog_place_checkin_plans for recipient matching the invite's plan place + planned_at), unanswered (neither), so sent is always the denominator and a zero is unambiguous. Do not use responded_at as 'accepted' - only dismiss writes it (0120_event_invites.sql:59). (4) hover rosters on each tile from the gitignored sidecar; add meetups, meetup_attendees (userCol profile_id, tsCol joined_at) and event_invites (userCol inviter_id, tsCol created_at) to config/apps.json doggle.activitySources so the DAU lane counts them as activity. App-side (doggle only, no michi/tcgscan mirror needed - surface exists only in doggle): rename event.view -> `meetup.view { meetup_id }` (uuid only), fired once per mount in MeetupDetailScreen's load effect, registered in config/events.json taxonomy as {label:'Opened a meetup', stage:'engagement', apps:['doggle']}. Pair it in the report as 'joins per meetup view' with the inline caveat that RSVPs also happen from the home Events feed (UpcomingEventsSection.tsx:191/213) without a detail view, so it is a ratio, not a funnel. No RSVP, invite or cancel event in the app - the tables are authoritative.

The only track( call in data/ is auth.ts:37-73. screens/MeetupDetailScreen.tsx, MeetupsScreen.tsx, MeetupEditScreen.tsx, components/EventInviteMo…
- **ground-truth** — real (high): Ground truth is correctly named and the proposal is already a studio lane, so the gap stands (rule: groundTruth + studio lane => real). ### 9. [LOW] `doggle_geofence_prompt_blind` — Favorite-geofence enter/exit and the local 'you are passing X' prompt emit nothing; taps on it have no shown denominator
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** How often does a favorite geofence fire, how many of those produce a nearby-check-in prompt, and how many prompts are tapped? Today push.opened kind 'checkin' is a numerator with no denominator for the local-prompt share.

**Evidence:** lib/favoriteGeofenceTask.ts:25-43 TaskManager task: Enter -> recordGps('geofence_enter') + handleFavoriteProximity (:32-34), Exit -> handleFavoriteDeparture (:35-38). recordGps only queues while a GPS test session is active (lib/gpsDebug.ts:64-70 'if (!session) return') so production geofence fires leave no row. handleFavoriteProximity -> promptCheckinNearby (lib/favoriteProximity.ts:43-70) schedules a LOCAL notification via scheduleLocalPrompt (:13-20, :63) with no analytics call; the foreground passive path (lib/passiveFavoriteService.ts:54,74) calls the same function. Taps come back as push.opened { kind: 'checkin' | 'open' } (lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:202-210), but a local notification is never in alert_notification_outbox, so this kind has no send-side count. Caveat for any fix: track() buffers until resetSessionUser settles (lib/analytics.ts:544-553); a headless TaskManager invocation never runs AppNavigator's auth wiring, so an emitter inside the background task would buffer and drop.

**Proposal:** Foreground path (passiveFavoriteService.ts:54,74): prompt.shown { prompt: 'nearby-checkin-local', surface: 'app' } where scheduleLocalPrompt is invoked, paired with push.opened { kind: 'checkin' | 'open' } as the answer. Background path: do not emit from the TaskManager task (buffer drops); instead record the fire in a product table via a SECURITY DEFINER RPC (e.g. nearby_prompt_log with dog_place_id, source 'geofence' | 'passive', created_at) so the studio reads shown from the table and taps from the stream. Mark this as adjacent to the push lane.

**Ground truth already available:** dog_place_checkins with source 'passive' answers the OUTCOME (passive check-ins); alert_notification_outbox covers only the server-pushed variant (0311_nearby_prompt_pushable), not local prompts.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow in three places. (a) Split the denominator by origin: the server-pushed share already has a count in alert_notification_outbox (channel='push', alert_key like 'nearby_checkin:%', status/sent_at, 0060:54-66) and no studio lane reads it (grep of scripts/ for alert_notification_outbox returns nothing) - that half is a STUDIO-LANE gap, not an app gap. Only the local-fallback share (no push token, or RPC unreachable) is an instrumentation gap. (b) The tap side needs a distinguishing prop or the pairing fails: add a fixed `origin: 'local' | 'push'` to push.opened derived from notification data (scheduleLocalPrompt can set data.local=true at favoriteProximity.ts:21; server pushes carry none), so prompt.shown{prompt:'nearby-checkin-local'} pairs with push.opened{origin:'local'} and outbox rows pair with origin:'push'. Prompt key must not reuse 'walk-checkin-nearby' (WalkCheckinPromptHost.tsx:38). (c) The foreground path emitting prompt.shown is only correct if emitted inside the `if (res.unreachable || (res.enqueued && !res.pushable))` branch at favoriteProximity.ts:62, not at the passiveFavoriteService.ts:54/74 call sites - most calls return enqueued=false (cooldown/already_checked_in, 0311:53,63) or pushable=true and show no local notification. The background buffer-drop caveat is sound: resetSessionUser is called only from src/navigation/AppNavigator.tsx:206-209, so a headless geofence invocation (App.tsx:26 imports the task at module load) has cachedUser unset and track() buffers (analytics.ts:544-553); a product-table write via SECURITY DEFINER RPC (or simply making the existing RPC insert the outbox row with a 'local' channel/status when !v_has_push) is the right fix for geofence fires. Severity low stands.
- *ground-truth:* Title: 'Nearby-check-in prompts to users WITHOUT a push token (local fallback) and RPC-suppressed geofence/passive fires have no per-occurrence row; token-holding users are already covered by alert_notification_outbox'. Ground truth: alert_notification_outbox (recipient_id, channel='push', alert_key like 'nearby_checkin:%', status, created_at, sent_at) is the shown-side denominator for the main path - read it in the push lane that events.json:1232 already calls for; walk_nearby_checkin_cooldowns (profile_id, dog_place_id, last_prompted_at) gives a daily lower bound that includes no-token users; dog_place_checkins.source='passive' is the outcome. Proposal: drop the client-side prompt.shown { prompt: 'nearby-checkin-local' } - prompt.shown is the in-app dialog family paired with prompt.answered (events.json:270) and a notification has no 'answered'. Fix on the server instead, inside enqueue_nearby_checkin_prompt, which BOTH paths already call (so the background buffer-drop caveat is moot): insert one row per call into a new nearby_prompt_log (profile_id, dog_place_id, created_at, outcome enum 'enqueued_push' | 'local_fallback' | 'suppressed_checked_in' | 'suppressed_cooldown', trigger enum 'geofence' | 'passive' | 'walk' passed as a new p_trigger arg) before each early return at 0311:244/254 and after 0311:276. Client side, one enum prop on the existing push.opened: source 'server' | 'local', derivable from presence of data.outboxId (0403_alert_outbox_hardening.sql:45 says every server push carries it; scheduleLocalPrompt favoriteProximity.ts:17-25 sets none), so local taps get their own numerator against the log's local_fallback rows. The offline 'unreachable' local prompt stays unseen by design and must be stated as such. App: doggle (server RPC + one prop), adjacent to the studio push lane.
- *worth-it:* Title: Local 'Check in nearby?' notifications have no send-side record and taps cannot be told from server pushes. Severity: low. Fix in three parts. (A) SERVER (migration, no client change, covers geofence + foreground, no buffer problem): inside enqueue_nearby_checkin_prompt (0311) insert one row per call into a new append-only table nearby_prompt_log(profile_id uuid, dog_place_id text, outcome text check in ('pushed','local','suppressed_checked_in','suppressed_cooldown'), created_at timestamptz) at each return point (:53, :63, :85 split on v_pushable); RLS, no client policies, studio reads it via Management API. This is the denominator for every prompt that reached the server and replaces the lossy last_prompted_at upsert as the count. (B) CLIENT, foreground and background both safe because the emit happens only where track() can flush: in favoriteProximity.ts:62 branch emit push.scheduled { kind: 'checkin', origin: 'local', reason: 'no_token' | 'unreachable' } (reason = res.unreachable ? 'unreachable' : 'no_token'; the only offline-visible record) - do NOT use prompt.shown. Keep it in the push.* area; it is a notification, not a dialog. From the headless task this will buffer and drop, which is acceptable because (A) carries the background denominator; state that in the taxonomy doc. (C) TAP: extend trackPushOpened to push.opened { kind, origin: 'remote' | 'local' } where origin = data.outboxId ? 'remote' : 'local' (PushNotificationContext.tsx:189-211, data read at :222). Pairing: push.scheduled{origin:'local'} + nearby_prompt_log(outcome='local') -> push.opened{origin:'local', kind:'checkin'|'open'} -> dog_place_checkins(source='manual') within N minutes; alert_notification_outbox(alert_key like 'nearby_checkin:%', status='sent') -> push.opened{origin:'remote'}. Drop the groundTruth claim about source 'passive'. Mark as blocked on a push-outbox studio lane (none exists yet) and on doggle_product_blind reaching 'fixed' for the push family.
