### 10. [LOW] `doggle_walk_start_sheet_denominator` — The walk start sheet emits nothing when opened or closed without starting, so walk.start has no intent denominator and the no-dogs dead end is invisible
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** doggle-product · **App:** doggle

**Question it answers:** How many people open the walk sheet and leave without starting, and how many are blocked because they have no dog yet? Today walk.start counts successes with nothing above it.

**Evidence:** components/walk/WalkStartSheet.tsx:39 WalkStartSheet({ visible, onClose }); mounted from lib/WalkStartContext.tsx:16 <WalkStartSheet visible={open} onClose=.../>. onStart at :104-115 refuses with a toast when selectedDogIds.size === 0 (:105-108) and otherwise navigates Walk { autoStart: true } which reaches startWalk -> trackWalkStart (data/walks.ts:99). The 'Add a dog on your profile before tracking a walk' state renders at :131. No track import in WalkStartSheet.tsx or WalkStartContext.tsx. Walk pause does not exist in the product (no match for pause/resume in lib/walkTrackingService.ts or components/walk/WalkTrackPanel.tsx beyond the 'Start or resume GPS tracking' comment at :179), so 'pause' is not a missing emitter.

**Proposal:** Existing vocabulary only: trackPromptShown('walk-start-sheet', 'walk') when visible becomes true (:56) and trackPromptAnswered('walk-start-sheet', 'walk', 'accepted' | 'declined') from onStart success and onClose; the no-dog refusal at :105-108 as prompt.answered response 'declined' with a second prompt id 'walk-start-no-dogs' shown at :131 so the dead end is countable. Pairing: prompt.shown -> prompt.answered -> walk.start.

**Ground truth already available:** walks.started_at answers starts (already read by usage.mjs:134-160); nothing answers opens of the sheet.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the proposal's no-dogs mechanics: the onStart refusal at WalkStartSheet.tsx:105-108 is NOT the no-dogs path. The Start button is disabled when dogs.length === 0 OR selectedDogIds.size === 0 (:189), so :105-108 is an effectively unreachable guard for the all-deselected case (:154-156). The no-dog dead end is a render state (dogs.length === 0 after the :55-87 load) that can only end via onClose, so emit trackPromptShown('walk-start-no-dogs','walk') when loading finishes with dogs.length === 0 and trackPromptAnswered(...,'dismissed') from onClose; do not route it through onStart. Two further guards the proposal needs: (a) onClose is called from onStart at :110 before navigating, so emit 'accepted' in onStart before calling onClose (or flag it) so the same close is not also counted 'declined'; (b) the active-walk shortcut at :69-72 calls onClose() and navigates without a start - that close is neither accepted nor declined and should not emit prompt.shown at all (skip the shown until `active` resolves null at :74), otherwise every tab press during a live walk reads as a decline.
- *worth-it:* Title: The walk quick-start sheet emits nothing, so walk.start has no attempt denominator and 'opened with no dog' is uncountable. Proposal (doggle only; no michi/tcgscan surface): two NEW walk.* events, not prompt.*. (1) walk.sheet_shown { dogs: number } emitted in WalkStartSheet.tsx inside the load effect AFTER the active-walk check, next to setDogs at :74-77, with dogs = myDogs.length - so the active-walk redirect at :69-73 emits nothing and dogs === 0 IS the no-dog dead end (no second prompt id, no emit from the unreachable toast at :105-108). (2) walk.sheet_closed { via: 'start' | 'dismiss' } - 'start' from onStart at :110 (before navigateWhenReady), 'dismiss' from the BottomSheet onClose prop at :119 wrapped in the component (not from WalkStartContext's setOpen(false), which cannot tell the two apart). Pairing documented in events.json: walk.sheet_shown -> walk.sheet_closed{via} -> location.permission_requested/result (already live) -> walk.start; a shown with no closed is 'left open / app backgrounded', reported separately, same reading as _promptsDoc. Dead-end rate = sheet_shown where dogs=0 over sheet_shown; conversion = walk.start over sheet_closed via=start, with location.permission_result denied as the named leak between them. Studio-lane companion (not an app gap): count profiles with zero rows in public.dogs in usage.mjs beside the walks tile at scripts/usage.mjs:273 for the population denominator.

## 3. Pickleague product surface

Courts, live play, geofence arrival, match confirm, RSVPs, the store-rating prompt, the update banner.

### 1. [BLOCKING] `pickleague_product_blind_open` — Pickleague still emits nothing about the product; the registered fix landed in doggle only
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** For any pickleague account, what did they try to do, where did it refuse them, and what did they abandon? Today the journeys lane can show only the screens they visited (page.view) and the sign-in.

**Evidence:** Registered gap config/events.json gaps.doggle_product_blind: title "Doggle and Pickleague emitted nothing about the product", status "landed", fix "Landed 2026-09-04 in doggle/app: prompt.shown / prompt.answered ... push ... walk ... checkin ... discover.tab" - doggle only. Pickleague call sites, exhaustive (grep track( across mobile/src excluding lib/analytics.ts): GetTheAppButton.tsx:63 appstore.click; LoginScreen.tsx:48 auth.login; GuestJoinScreen.tsx:114 auth.login; RegisterScreen.tsx:93 account.created; AppNavigator.tsx:315,317 trackScreen. config/events.json taxonomy: pickleague appears on exactly session.start, page.view, auth.login, account.created. data/events.json apps.pickleague.coverage: declared 4, fired 3. Meanwhile 2026-10-09 shipped venue_checkins (75dca6d), play_sessions (0100cf6), geofence alerts (1d1a727), rating prompt (cde78c9), update banner (ad156a6) with zero emitters in CourtCheckinCard.tsx, courtCheckin.ts, courtGeofence.ts, playSession.ts, PlayIsland.tsx, ratingPrompt.ts (none import analytics except courtCheckin.ts/playSession.ts for uuidv4).

**Proposal:** Correct the registry first: split doggle_product_blind into doggle (keep landed) and a new pickleague_product_blind with status open so the coverage panel stops implying parity; or set this gap's status back to open with the doggle half noted. Then land the concrete emitters itemised in the gaps below (check-in, play session, match confirm via, event vote via, guest upgrade, rating ask, build distribution). Ground truth already covers OUTCOMES (venue_checkins, play_sessions, matches, event_slot_votes, bookmarks, player_shop_purchases, ftue_grants) so the stream work is intent, impressions and refusals only. One extra refusal worth carrying while there: shop.purchase_failed {item_id, reason: insufficient|server} at ShopScreen.tsx:237-243, denominator page.view route Shop (already intent pricing in routes).

**Ground truth already available:** venue_checkins, play_sessions, matches, event_slot_votes/event_declines, bookmarks, drill_requests, player_shop_purchases, ftue_grants, user_streak_rewards hold every completed action; the stream is blind to attempts and refusals.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow: this is a registry-classification gap plus an emitter gap, not a reversal of doggle_product_blind. Keep doggle's half landed; add pickleague_product_blind (status open) or append a per-app status to doggle_product_blind. Note appstore.click fires from GetTheAppButton.tsx:63 but is absent from the pickleague taxonomy list, and the foundation explain-prompt onEvent caveat (config/events.json:1233) applies to pickleague as well once prompt events are added.
- *ground-truth:* Narrow one prop: the proposed shop.purchase_failed `reason` enum should mirror the RPC's four distinct refusal branches at migration_add_pickles_shop.sql:126-144 - `not_found | unavailable | already_owned | insufficient | server` (server = the supabase error branch at ShopScreen.tsx:237) - rather than only `insufficient|server`. Also note for the geofence family: there is no server outbox in pickleague (notifications table is admin in-app only, courtGeofence.ts:43 is local AsyncStorage), so a geofence alert.shown/alert.opened client pair is the only possible source, unlike doggle where sends live in alert_notification_outbox.
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap and the registry split (new gap key pickleague_product_blind, status open; doggle_product_blind narrowed to doggle, keep landed). Tighten the proposal in four places: (1) Shop refusal pairing: `page.view route Shop` is not the attempt denominator. Pair as shop.purchase_click {item_id} at ShopScreen.tsx confirmBuy entry (line ~231) -> outcome either player_shop_purchases row (ground truth) or shop.purchase_failed {item_id, reason}. (2) Reason enum must come from branch, not text: ShopScreen.tsx:237 `error` from supabase.rpc -> reason 'server'; ShopScreen.tsx:240 `row.success===false` -> reason 'refused' (the RPC in migration_add_pickles_shop.sql:143 returns only message text 'Not enough pickles', no code; do not string-match the message; 'refused' is the honest enum until the RPC returns a code). Never log error.message or row.message. (3) Reuse doggle's existing names rather than inventing pickleague ones so the taxonomy stays one vocabulary: checkin.create / checkin.out / checkin.failed {venue_id, reason enum} already exist (config/events.json taxonomy, apps ["doggle"]) - add "pickleague" to their apps; same for push.permission_requested / push.permission_result / push.token_registered / push.opened. Push SENDS are ground truth in pickleague/supabase/migration_push_outbox.sql, not an emitter gap - mirror the doggle note. (4) Declare appstore.click in the taxonomy for pickleague (fires at GetTheAppButton.tsx:63, undeclared today) so coverage stops counting a fired-but-undeclared name. New names only where no doggle equivalent exists: play.start / play.end / play.cancel {session_id} (mirror walk.start/end/cancel shape), prompt.shown / prompt.answered {prompt: 'rating'|'update', answer enum} for ratingPrompt.ts and UpdateReadyBanner (michi vocabulary verbatim, as doggle did), geofence.alert_opened {venue_id} as the only client-visible half of 1d1a727.

### 2. [HIGH] `pickleague_appstore_click_unregistered` — appstore.click is emitted but not in the taxonomy, has never fired, and the App Store QR is unobservable by design
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Do flyer scans reach the App Store? The campaign spine expects QR -> signup joins, but the install QR bypasses the site and the web QR's attribution does not survive to account.created.

**Evidence:** GetTheAppButton.tsx:63 track('appstore.click',{from}) shipped 9b148a4 2026-09-04, rendered on LoginScreen.tsx:103 (from='login', web only). config/events.json taxonomy has no appstore.click entry (full name list checked). data/events.json apps.pickleague.coverage.unrecognised is [] in every window, and scripts/events.mjs:1168-1178 computes that set over all traffic including excluded accounts, so no one - not even QA - has clicked it in the 30 days to 2026-10-11 (30d: 15 web sessions, 15 Login page.views). public/landing.html:338,347 and scripts/generate-landing-qr.js:30 point the install QR straight at apps.apple.com; landing.html:316-330 says that scan 'records nothing'. The web QR (generate-landing-qr.js:36) carries utm_* but no `code`; analytics.ts:141 sets landingCampaign.code from the `code` param only and emit() :600-602 merges only `code` into account.created, so a utm-only arrival attributes through landing_route (campaigns.mjs:332 falls back to utm_campaign) but a signup days later carries nothing.

**Proposal:** (1) Studio: add appstore.click to config/events.json taxonomy {label 'Tapped the App Store link', stage acquisition, apps [pickleague]} with props {from: login|landing} so its first fire is recognised rather than unrecognised, and show it as neverFired until then. (2) Emitter: analytics.ts:141 `code: kept.get('code') ?? kept.get('utm_campaign')` so utm-only QR arrivals persist to account.created like `code` arrivals do. (3) Product/marketing: the install QR cannot be measured by this studio at all; route it through pickleague.club/?code=<campaign>&go=appstore (recording landing_route then redirecting) or accept App Store Connect's campaign token (ct=) as the only readout and say so in reports/campaigns.

**Ground truth already available:** analytics_sessions.landing_route carries utm_campaign for web QR arrivals (campaigns lane reads it). App Store installs exist only in App Store Connect.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Title: "appstore.click is emitted (GetTheAppButton.tsx:63) but absent from the taxonomy, and the marketing landing page (/landing) is entirely unobserved". Severity: medium, not high. Narrowings: (a) "has never fired" -> "has not fired in the 30 days the events lane fetches" — scripts/events.mjs:29 WINDOW_DAYS=30 and :86/:98 scope both sessions and events to `now() - interval '30 days'`, so all-time is not checked. (b) The flyer framing is wrong: printed pickleague codes (marketing-studio/assets/qr/campaigns.yaml entries pickleague-wom/hub/court) all target https://pickleague.club with `code=` and GetTheAppButton.tsx:12-14 says flyers "land on the site root"; flyer scans do reach the site with a code. The App Store QR at landing.html:338 is an on-page QR on the marketing page (served at /landing via mobile/vercel.json:15), scanned by someone already viewing the site on another screen. (c) landing.html carries no analytics at all — its only script (landing.html:379-) sets a localStorage flag and drives the lightbox — so the page view, the store-btn anchor at :346 and the QR are all unobserved; this is the real "unmeasurable by design" surface, broader than the QR. (d) The utm-only persistence loss (analytics.ts:141) affects only the landing.html web QR (generate-landing-qr.js:36 utm_campaign=footer); no printed campaign is utm-only (campaigns.yaml defaults deliberately empty, every entry has `code`). And the campaigns lane still follows that arrival forward by device_id (campaigns.mjs:252-253, 285, 376-383 gainedIdentity), which pickleague writes on every session (analytics.ts:304; data/campaigns.json capture pickleague with_device 150/150), so a same-browser signup days later is still credited as "became a member" — only the `signupsWithCode` (account.created.code) count misses it. Proposal (2) is therefore low-value; keep (1) taxonomy registration with props {from: login} (only one call site exists today, no `landing` instance) paired with the Login page.view as its impression denominator, and reframe (3) as: either give landing.html a minimal page.view + appstore.click emitter, or state in reports/campaigns that /landing and App Store installs are outside the stream (App Store Connect is the only ground truth).
- *ground-truth:* Minor path fix only: the landing page is mobile/public/landing.html (not public/landing.html); the QR img is at :337 and the store-btn href at :346. Everything else in the gap stands.
- *worth-it:* Title: "appstore.click shipped 2026-09-04 but is not in the taxonomy; the /landing page's App Store QR and utm-only web QR are unattributable". Severity: medium. Proposal: (1) Studio: add `appstore.click` to config/events.json taxonomy {label 'Tapped the App Store link', stage acquisition, apps [pickleague], props {from: 'login'}} with a doc note modelled on _pairingDoc: it is intent leaving the web app, never an install; denominator is page.view route=Login on platform web (30d: 15 Login views, 14 web sessions). It shows as neverFired until the owner's own QA tap proves the call site (coverage counts excluded traffic). Do not list `from: landing` - landing.html has no analytics emitter (its only script, :379, sets localStorage). (2) Emitter: analytics.ts:141 `code: kept.get('code') ?? kept.get('utm_campaign')` so the /landing web QR (utm_campaign=footer) survives to account.created like the printed `?code=wom|hub|court` already does. Scope correctly: printed flyers (marketing-studio campaigns.yaml:61-78) already carry `code` and are NOT affected. (3) Studio only: reports/campaigns should state inline that the App Store QR on /landing and App Store installs generally are outside this studio (App Store Connect only); the redirect-through-site idea is a marketing decision for the owner, not a tracking gap. (4) Evidence wording: 'no fire in the 30d fetch (since 2026-09-11)', not 'never fired since ship'.

### 3. [HIGH] `pickleague_checkin_blind` — Court check-in emits nothing about the attempt, the refusal or the dismissal; only successes land (in a table no lane reads)
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Of players who tapped Find my court, how many were refused (no location, no court within 150 m), how many were offered a court and said Not here, and how many geofence arrival notices turned into a check-in? Who's-here adoption per court cannot be read anywhere today.

**Evidence:** CourtCheckinCard.tsx:70-85 findCourt: getCurrentCoords null -> setMessage('Turn on location...'); resolveVenueAt null -> 'No court found within 150 m' - no track. :87-100 checkIn -> checkInAt (courtCheckin.ts:131-155) -> runOrQueue; queued vs sent only sets a message. :165-166 'Not here' -> setNearby(null), nothing recorded. courtGeofence.ts:38-52 onEnter posts the local notice with no record; :72-82 court_checkin handler check-in has no emitter; SettingsScreen.tsx:78-95 toggleCourtAlerts treats n===0 as 'needs Always location' with no event. Successful check-ins are the only observable: supabase/migration_venue_checkins.sql:19-28 (source manual|geofence|session). No studio script reads venue_checkins (grep scripts/*.mjs). Taxonomy names checkin.create/checkin.out/checkin.failed and location.permission_requested/location.permission_result exist with apps [doggle] only (config/events.json).

**Proposal:** Extend the existing doggle names to pickleague (add 'pickleague' to apps; do not mint new ones): checkin.create {source: manual|geofence|session, queued: 0|1, venue_id}, checkin.out {source, via: card|island|lock_screen|geofence_exit}, checkin.failed {source, reason: no_location|no_venue_nearby|timeout|server}. Impression/denominator for the offer: prompt.shown {prompt: 'checkin_offer', surface: 'home_card'} when `nearby` is set, prompt.answered {prompt: 'checkin_offer', response: accepted|declined} on Check in / Not here (michi vocabulary, feeds the existing asks rollup). Geofence funnel: prompt.shown {prompt: 'court_arrival', surface: 'notice'} in onEnter, outcome = checkin.create {source: 'geofence'}. location.permission_requested/result {scope: foreground|always, granted: 0|1} around setCourtAlertsEnabled(true) and getCurrentCoords. Studio lane: add venue_checkins (user_id, checked_in_at, source) to config/apps.json pickleague activitySources and a who's-here series in usage.mjs; it is the durable count regardless of the emitter.

**Ground truth already available:** public.venue_checkins (successful check-ins only, with source and expiry) - not read by any lane yet.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two narrowings. (1) Drop checkin.out {via: card|island|lock_screen|geofence_exit}: pickleague has no check-out surface. courtCheckin.ts exports checkInAt/resolveVenueAt only (no checkOut), courtGeofence.ts handles onEnter only (:38-52) with no exit handler, and the card renders no leave/check-out control; presence ends by expires_at auto-expiry (courtCheckin.ts checkInAt sets expires_at; migration_venue_checkins.sql:19-28). Expiry is readable from venue_checkins in the studio lane, so no app event is needed. (2) The geofence notice tap is already inside the open gap pickleague_push_tap_blind (config/events.json, status open), which proposes notification.opened on tap - do not duplicate tap tracking here. Keep for this gap only the impression prompt.shown {prompt:'court_arrival', surface:'notice'} in courtGeofence.ts onEnter (:38-52) and the outcome checkin.create {source:'geofence'} in the court_checkin action handler (:70-85). Everything else in the proposal stands: checkin.create {source, queued, venue_id} + checkin.failed {reason: no_location|no_venue_nearby|timeout|server} in CourtCheckinCard.tsx findCourt/checkIn (:70-100), prompt.shown/answered {prompt:'checkin_offer'} around setNearby/Check in/Not here (:84, :95, :165), location.permission_requested/result {scope: foreground|always} around getCurrentCoords and setCourtAlertsEnabled(true) (SettingsScreen.tsx:78-95); add 'pickleague' to the apps arrays of the five doggle names and the two michi prompt names rather than minting new ones. Studio-lane half: add venue_checkins (user_id, checked_in_at, source) to config/apps.json pickleague activitySources and a who's-here-per-court series in usage.mjs - that is a separate studio gap, not app instrumentation.
- *ground-truth:* Minor sharpening only: the geofence-arrival notice is a device-local notification (courtGeofence.ts:46-49 postLocalNotice), not a push_outbox row, so the proposed prompt.shown {prompt:'court_arrival', surface:'notice'} is the ONLY possible denominator - note that the AsyncStorage RENOTIFY dedupe (:40-43) means one shown-event per venue per RENOTIFY_MS, which the funnel should state. Studio lane half is correct and separable: add venue_checkins (user_id, checked_in_at, source) to config/apps.json pickleague activitySources (apps.json:93) and a who's-here series in usage.mjs mirroring dog_place_checkins (usage.mjs:141,274).
- *worth-it:* Keep key pickleague_checkin_blind but register it as the pickleague half of gaps.doggle_product_blind (whose 'landed' status is doggle-only; cite fix text 'Landed 2026-09-04 in doggle/app'). Proposal fixes: (a) Enums must match code, not the DB check constraint: checkin.create {source: manual|geofence, queued: 0|1, venue_id} - 'session' is allowed by migration_venue_checkins.sql:26 but no caller passes it (grep checkInAt: only CourtCheckinCard.tsx:88 manual and courtGeofence.ts:73 geofence); checkin.out {source, via: card|geofence_exit} - no island or lock_screen checkout exists (checkOut callers: CourtCheckinCard.tsx:111, courtGeofence.ts:56 only). (b) checkin.failed {source:'manual', reason: no_location|no_venue_nearby|error} emitted in findCourt's two early returns and in run()'s catch when kind==='find'|'checkin'; keep doggle's 'error' class (doggle/app/src/lib/analytics.ts:742 reason: 'cooldown'|'error') rather than minting timeout|server unless withTimeout throws a typed error you can test for. Denominator for the Find tap needs no new name: every tap ends in exactly one of prompt.shown{prompt:'checkin_offer',surface:'home_card'} (court found, CourtCheckinCard.tsx:77 setNearby(v) with v non-null) or checkin.failed, so Find taps = shown + failed. prompt.answered {prompt:'checkin_offer', response: accepted|declined} on Check in (:85) / Not here (:165); add response:'abandoned' from the existing AppState background hook (analytics.ts:517) if nearby is still set, else a shown with no answer reads as 'left with it open' per _promptsDoc. (e) Studio lane (the only part that is not an app gap): add {table:'venue_checkins', userCol:'user_id', tsCol:'checked_in_at'} to pickleague activitySources in config/apps.json, add a Pickleague 'court check-ins' series by venue_id and source in scripts/usage.mjs beside the doggle one at :274, and extend digest.mjs:301 (currently doggle-only) with the pickleague count. ### 4. [HIGH] `pickleague_match_confirm_via_blind` — Match confirmation from the push button and from the app write the same row; neither emits, and the push-action path cannot even reach the emitter
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Does the one-tap Confirm button (11e8dac) get used, and does it cut time-to-confirm against the 1-hour deadline, versus players who open the app or let it lapse?

**Evidence:** MatchHistoryScreen.tsx:324-331 confirmMatch -> supabase.rpc('confirm_match',{p_match_id}) then noteGoodMoment(); notificationActions.ts:126-134 match_confirm handler -> rpc('confirm_match',{p_match_id}). migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:237-280 confirm_match(p_match_id uuid) takes no source; matches.team1_confirmed_by/team2_confirmed_by (:23-24) record who, not how. Lapses are server-side (:285-292). Sends are ground truth in push_outbox (category, kind, entity_id; migration_push_outbox.sql:28-52). Emitter constraint: analytics.ts:556-566 buffers until cachedUser is set, which happens only in AppNavigator.tsx:231 (onAuthStateChange); notificationActions.ts header states handlers 'run on a background launch where nothing is mounted: no navigator', so a track() from a lock-screen button would sit in `pending` and die with the process unless the emitter settles identity itself (currentUserId(supabase) as courtCheckin.ts:113 does).

**Proposal:** match.confirm {match_id, via: in_app|push_action, pending_secs} after a successful RPC in both paths; match.confirm_failed {via, reason: lapsed|already_settled|server} from mapError's branches (notificationActions.ts:87-96). Denominators: push_outbox rows with category 'match_confirm' for the push path (ground truth, join entity_id = match_id); page.view route MatchHistory for in-app. Prerequisite in analytics.ts: a headless path that sets cachedUser from currentUserId(supabase) when resetSessionUser has not run (or flush on next foreground with the buffered `at`). The same prerequisite covers every lock-screen handler (event_accept/decline, drill_*, tournament_accept, checkin_extend/out, court_checkin, play_start).

**Ground truth already available:** public.matches (team1_confirmed_by, team2_confirmed_by, confirm_deadline, status) gives who confirmed and when it completed; public.push_outbox gives which confirm requests were sent. Neither says in-app vs push button.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the groundTruth claim: public.matches does NOT give the lapsed denominator. expire_pending_matches() DELETES lapsed pending rows every minute (migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:285-292; migration_match_confirm_notify_category.sql:9 restates this), so 'let it lapse' outcomes vanish from matches and pending_secs is only computable for rows that completed. push_outbox (category='match_confirm', entity_id=match_id; migration_push_outbox.sql:28-52, service-role only) is the only durable record of a confirm request that was sent, so the lapse rate must be derived as push_outbox rows whose entity_id has no surviving matches row, or a match.confirm_failed{reason:lapsed} event, or (studio-lane alternative) a soft-expire that keeps the row. Also note confirm_match returns 'completed' | 'one_team_confirmed' (migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:272-277): the proposed match.confirm should carry result: completed|one_team_confirmed from the RPC return rather than only via. Everything else in the gap stands.
- *ground-truth:* Tighten groundTruth: public.matches has no confirmed_at (only confirmed_by uuids, migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:23-24), so it gives who confirmed but NOT when; and lapsed matches are deleted by expire_pending_matches (:285-292), so the lapse denominator is only recoverable as push_outbox rows with category 'match_confirm' whose entity_id has no surviving completed match. pending_secs therefore must come from the event (or from the push_outbox.created_at of the matching row), not from matches.
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap, file it under the pickleague half of doggle_product_blind (that gap's fix landed only in doggle/app). Rewrite the proposal: EVENTS: match.confirm { match_id, via: 'in_app' | 'push_action' } after the RPC resolves ('one_team_confirmed' | 'completed'; add result: 'one_team' | 'completed' from the RPC return so the completing confirm is distinguishable); match.confirm_failed { match_id, via, reason: 'not_pending' | 'expired' | 'not_found' | 'not_on_match' | 'timeout' | 'other' } - reason derived in-process from the confirm_match raise text (migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:248-258) or TimeoutError, never the message itself. Drop pending_secs: compute time-to-confirm in the studio as event ts minus matches.created_at (survives for confirmed matches) or push_outbox.created_at. DENOMINATORS: push path = public.push_outbox where kind='match' and entity_type='match' and status='sent' (push_outbox.category is the pref gate, not the action category - send-push/index.ts:123 resolves CATEGORY.match_confirm per send and does not persist it; a caveat line in the report must say the button-offered condition is inferred, not recorded, or send-push gains a persisted `action_category` column on push_outbox - a server-lane fix, not an app one). Asked-to-confirm for both paths = public.notifications where type='match' (written by _notify_pending_match :219-221 for every other player; survives match deletion since entity_id has no FK). Lapsed = those notifications whose entity_id has no matches row and no match.confirm event. In-app impression: add match.confirm_shown { match_id } where MatchHistoryScreen renders a Confirm control for a pending match the viewer has not yet confirmed; page.view route=MatchHistory is not an adequate denominator. PREREQUISITE (unchanged, now the headline): analytics.ts needs a headless identity path - when cachedUser is null, resolve currentUserId(supabase) (expo-foundation src/supabase/query.ts:81) and emit directly instead of buffering, or persist `pending` to AsyncStorage and drain in resetSessionUser; without it every one of the 11 lock-screen handlers emits nothing. Register match.confirm, match.confirm_failed, match.confirm_shown in config/events.json taxonomy with apps:['pickleague'], planned:true, failure:true on the _failed one. Add push.action { action_id, outcome: 'ok' | 'failed' | 'timeout' } once in handleNotificationAction (not per handler) so the other 10 buttons get a denominator-paired outcome from the same prerequisite; name sits beside doggle's push.opened.

- **already-emitted** — real (high): No emitter covers either confirm path. pickleague/mobile has exactly five track() call sites (analytics.ts:587 page.view; GuestJoinScreen.tsx:114 and LoginScreen.tsx:48 auth.login; RegisterScreen.tsx:93 account.created; GetTheAppButton.tsx:63 appstore.click) and none is on a match surface. MatchHistoryScreen.tsx:324-332 confirmMatch calls supabase.rpc('confirm_match') then status.success/noteGoodMoment/loadMatches with no track(). notificationActions.ts:124-134 match_confirm handler calls requireUser() then rpc('confirm_match') with no track(); the module imports nothing from ./analytics (grep of notificationActions.ts, push.ts for 'track|analytics' returns nothing; courtCheckin.ts:18 only…
- **ground-truth** — real (high): No product table records the confirm source or confirm time. migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:23-24 adds only team1_confirmed_by/team2_confirmed_by (uuid) and :25 confirm_deadline; there is no confirmed_at column (grep for confirmed_at/updated_at in migration_match_confirm_flow.sql and migration_godmode_invite_overrides.sql returns nothing). confirm_match(p_match_id uuid) takes no source arg in every redefinition: migration_match_confirm_flow.sql:237, migration_godmode_invite_overrides.sql:21, migration_prod_reconcile_2026q3.sql:1313. A repo-wide grep finds exactly four product track() call sites in pickleague (GetTheAppButton.tsx:63, GuestJoinScreen.tsx:114, LoginScreen.tsx:48, RegisterScreen.tsx:93). ### 5. [HIGH] `pickleague_play_session_source_blind` — Play sessions record start/end but nothing says what started them or what the island was used for
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Does the floating island get used (Enter score / extend / End) or do sessions just expire after 6 hours? Do the 'starting now' notices for scheduled drills and matches produce sessions, versus the home card?

**Evidence:** playSession.ts:63-75 startPlay(meta) -> start_play_session(p_id,p_kind,p_source,p_venue,p_at) - migration_play_sessions.sql:39-70 has no via/source-of-start parameter; three entry points write identical rows: CourtCheckinCard.tsx:143-149 (checked in, 'Start playing'), :175-181 ('Start a session without checking in'), playSession.ts:173-183 play_start lock-screen handler. PlayIsland.tsx:43-46 'Enter score' navigates MatchEntry with {fromHome:true}, the same params as the Home tile HomeScreen.tsx:579, so a score entered from the island is indistinguishable from one entered from Home. :47-61 'Stay 1 more hour' and :62-70 'End' emit nothing. playSession.ts syncPlayStartReminders :133-152 schedules the 'starting now' notices with no record of how many were scheduled. No studio script reads play_sessions (grep). No play.* name exists in config/events.json taxonomy.

**Proposal:** play.start {kind: open_play|drill|match|event, via: home_card_checked_in|home_card_no_checkin|notice_play_start, venue: 0|1, queued: 0|1}; play.end {via: island|auto_expire_not_applicable, secs}; island.action {action: enter_score|extend|end}. Denominator for island.action is play.start (the island is visible for every live session). Durations and expiry are ground truth: play_sessions.ended_at = started_at + 6h marks the cron-closed ones (migration_play_sessions.sql:117-122). Studio: add play_sessions to activitySources and a kind x via series; read ended-by-cron as 'abandoned'.

**Ground truth already available:** public.play_sessions (kind, source_id, venue_id, started_at, ended_at) answers how many/how long; it cannot answer via or island usage.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Two citation fixes and one narrowing. (1) The cron-close marker is migration_play_sessions.sql:92-106 (expire_play_sessions + cron.schedule '25 * * * *'), not :117-122; the file ends at line 106. (2) Entry points are CourtCheckinCard.tsx:146-156 and :180-188 (the cited :143-149/:175-181 are off by a few lines). (3) Add to the proposal: PlayIsland 'Stay 1 more hour' extends the court CHECK-IN (extendCheckinByHour, PlayIsland.tsx:52), not the play session, so island.action{action:extend} should be read against checkin extension, and the same extend/check-out buttons on CourtCheckinCard.tsx:129-144 are equally unmeasured - pickleague has no checkin.* events at all (taxonomy checkin.create/out/failed are doggle-only). Separately flag for the verifier: gap doggle_product_blind is marked landed but its pickleague half never landed - pickleague/mobile still emits only session.start, page.view, auth.login, account.created, appstore.click.
- *ground-truth:* Two refinements, not refutations. (1) play.end {via} is largely derivable from the table and the event should shrink: endPlay() has exactly one call site (PlayIsland.tsx:68), so in play_sessions an ended_at that is neither started_at + 6h (cron, migration_play_sessions.sql:95) nor equal to the user's next session's started_at (auto-close on a new start, :59-60 - a third end cause the gap omitted) IS an island End. Studio lane should classify ended_at into island_end | cron_expired | superseded_by_start rather than the app emitting play.end. (2) Keep play.start {via} and island.action {action: enter_score|extend} as app events - the table cannot carry via (RPC :33-39 has no parameter), and extend overwrites expires_at in place. Denominator for island.action stays play.start; denominator for via=notice_play_start needs a count of notices scheduled (syncPlayStartReminders :137-148 schedules N with no record), so pair it with notice.scheduled {kind} or an impression count, else the notice->session rate has no denominator.
- *worth-it:* Severity medium (not high). Split in two. STUDIO LANE (no app change): add public.play_sessions to pickleague activitySources (userCol user_id, tsCol started_at) and a kind x via series where via is derived: kind in ('drill','match') => notice_play_start; venue_id is not null => home_card_checked_in; else home_card_no_checkin. End mode derived: ended_at = started_at + interval '6 hours' and no later start => cron_expired; ended_at = next session's started_at => superseded; else user_ended. APP (pickleague, 3 events, area 'play' + existing area 'push'): play.start {kind: open_play|drill|match, via: home_card_checked_in|home_card_no_checkin|notice_play_start, venue: 0|1, queued: 0|1} at playSession.ts startPlay (callers pass via); play.end {via: island, secs} at playSession.ts endPlay; play.island_action {action: enter_score|extend} at PlayIsland.tsx:45 and :52 (End is play.end, not duplicated; denominator = play.start since the island is visible for every live session, PlayIsland.tsx:39). Denominator for the notice: push.local_scheduled {category: play_start, n} at the end of syncPlayStartReminders (n = ids.length), and wire expo-foundation router onOpened in pickleague to emit push.opened {category: play_start, action: play_start|tap} so notice fired->opened->play.start reads as a funnel. Drop kind 'event' until a writer exists. Separately register appstore.click in config/events.json taxonomy (already emitted at GetTheAppButton.tsx:63, currently unrecognised). Also pass {from:'island'} instead of {fromHome:true} at PlayIsland.tsx:45 so MatchEntry can attribute a saved match (matches table) to the island.

### 6. [MEDIUM] `pickleague_build_distribution_unread` — app_build and installed_at now ride on every native session row and no studio lane reads them; the update banner and hard gate emit nothing
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Who is still on an old build, how fast do players move after a release, how many sessions would the min-version gate block before the floor is raised, and does anyone tap Restart on the OTA banner or always Later?

**Evidence:** analytics.ts:308-309 sends app_build and installed_at (45df19a, 2026-10-09; supabase/migration_analytics_build.sql adds the columns and index analytics_sessions_app_build_idx). scripts/events.mjs:76 selects s.app_version only; grep for app_build/installed_at across scripts/*.mjs and scripts/lib/*.mjs returns nothing. Registered gap doggle_product_blind._whatIsStillNotSeen already notes 'which build a stranded user is on ... is a report nobody has written'. Gate: AppNavigator.tsx:180-185 isUpdateRequired() flips updateRequired after the navigator has rendered, so the session row (with app_build) exists before the block screen; app_config.min_supported_version per platform (lib/minVersion.ts:49-60). Banner: AppNavigator.tsx:400 <UpdateReadyBanner/>; foundation ui/UpdateReadyBanner.tsx:21-62 has no onShown/onDismiss/onRestart props, Later is local state (:55).

**Proposal:** Studio-lane gap first, no emitter needed: extend scripts/events.mjs session select with app_build, installed_at and add a per-app panel - native sessions by platform x app_build per window, days since installed_at, and share of sessions below app_config.min_supported_version (read that row from pickleague's project; same shape for doggle's 0400 migration). This is the readout 45df19a unlocks. The hard gate needs no event: a session whose app_build is below the floor at the time is 'would be blocked'. The OTA banner needs foundation hooks (onShown/onLater/onRestart on UpdateReadyBanner) then update.banner {action: shown|later|restart} - shown is the denominator; mark as foundation-dependent.

**Ground truth already available:** analytics_sessions.app_build / installed_at / platform / app_version (all apps that upgraded the emitter) and app_config.min_supported_version.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow the doggle half of the proposal: doggle has NO app_build/installed_at emitter and no 0400 analytics_build migration (doggle/db/migrations contains 0397, 0398, 0402-0404; grep app_build across doggle/db and doggle/app/src returns nothing; no min_supported_version in doggle app code). Doggle does mount the same foundation UpdateReadyBanner (app/src/lib/providers/UpdateNudgeHost.tsx:6-9), so the banner-hook part applies to both apps, but the build-distribution/min-version-gate panel is pickleague-only until doggle ships the columns and emitter. Also note app_version is already read (events.mjs:76) but never surfaced in any rendered report, so the panel gap exists even for the pre-existing column.
- *ground-truth:* Three precision fixes to the proposal, none changing the verdict. (1) The "would be blocked" computation must compare app_version (marketing string) to app_config.min_supported_version[platform] with compareVersions semantics, NOT app_build: pickleague/mobile/src/lib/minVersion.ts:49-60 reads getNativeBuild().version and compareVersions(current, min) < 0; app_build is an integer CFBundleVersion/versionCode and is never compared to the floor. Use app_build for the "who is on an old build / adoption speed after a release" panels and app_version for the gate simulation. (2) Banner file path is expo-foundation/src/ui/UpdateReadyBanner.tsx (not ui/); the gate state is AppNavigator.tsx:177-186, and pickleague's 1.0.3-and-older builds send neither key (migration_analytics_build.sql:10-11), so the panel must show a "build unknown" bucket rather than drop those sessions. (3) Doggle's 0400 lives on branch next-build (commit 91cc299, "applied"), not main - the studio lane should read app_build via to_jsonb(s)->>'app_build' like device_id (events.mjs:78-79) so a project whose migration is not on the branch the lane reads returns null instead of erroring.
- *worth-it:* Three fixes to the proposal. (a) WRONG COMPARATOR: the gate compares the marketing version string, not app_build - lib/minVersion.ts:52 uses getNativeBuild().version ?? expoConfig.version against min_supported_version[Platform.OS] via compareVersions (:60). So 'share of sessions that would be blocked' must be computed from s.app_version vs app_config.min_supported_version per platform with semver compare; app_build is for 'who is on an old build / update velocity after a release', not for the gate share. (b) DOGGLE IS NOT ON MAIN: doggle's 0400_analytics_build.sql and the matching analytics.ts:301-302 emitter exist only on branch next-build (commit 91cc299, 2026-09-04); HEAD/main has no app_build anywhere (db/migrations jumps 0398 -> 0402). The studio select must read app_build/installed_at through to_jsonb(s)->>'...' exactly like device_id/landing_route at events.mjs:77-78 so an unlanded project returns null instead of failing the lane, and the panel must print 'column not landed' for doggle rather than a zero. (c) NAME CONVENTION: the taxonomy never multiplexes outcomes under one name with an {action} prop - it uses a shown event plus an answered/declined event (prompt.shown/prompt.answered, cap.gate_shown/cap.gate_dismissed, pro.offer_shown/pro.offer_declined). Rewrite as update.banner_shown {} (denominator) and update.banner_answered { choice: 'later' | 'restart' }, emitted from the apps via new onShown/onLater/onRestart props on expo-foundation UpdateReadyBanner (foundation-dependent, both doggle and pickleague since both mount it). Report installed_at only as whole days-since-install, never the raw timestamp, on the panel.

<details><summary>Lens verdicts</summary>

- **already-emitted** — real (high): Not refuted. (1) Emitter exists as claimed: pickleague/mobile/src/lib/analytics.ts:308-309 inserts app_build and installed_at on the session row; pickleague/supabase/migration_analytics_build.sql:14-20 adds the columns + analytics_sessions_app_build_idx (commit 45df19a, 2026-10-09). (3) Gate and banner emit nothing: pickleague AppNavigator.ts…
- **ground-truth** — real (high): Gap already names the ground truth correctly and proposes a studio lane first, so it stands. Columns exist: pickleague/supabase/migration_analytics_build.sql:14-20 adds analytics_sessions.app_build int + installed_at timestamptz and index analytics_sessions_app_build_idx (platform, app_build); commit 45df19a "analytics: app_build + installed_at on session rows (applied to prod)". Emitter: pickleague/mobile/src/lib/analytics.ts:308-309 inserts app_build and installed_at on every new native session row (commit 45df19a, 2026-10-09, 'applied to prod'); pickleague/supabase/migration_analytics_build.sql adds both columns plus analytics_sessions_app_build_idx. No registered gap covers it: config/events.json gaps list (share_attribution ... puzzle_blind) has no pickleague build/version…

### 7. [MEDIUM] `pickleague_event_vote_via_blind` — Event RSVP/votes from the push buttons and from the screen are the same insert; failures and the in-app impression are not recorded
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Do the Can't-make-it / I'm-in buttons on the event push (8c574ee) get used, and how often does a vote fail to save in the app?

**Evidence:** EventDetailScreen.tsx:214-227 inserts/deletes event_slot_votes and :248-262 event_declines, with status.error('Could not save your vote...') on failure and no track. notificationActions.ts:106-118 event_accept and :119-125 event_decline insert the same rows (insertIgnoringDuplicate). Neither table has a source column (schema.sql:102 event_slot_votes; migration_event_declines_and_min_players.sql:26). page.view {route:'EventDetail'} carries no event id or voting state (analytics.ts:587). usage.mjs:224 already counts event_slot_votes as 'votes'.

**Proposal:** event.vote {event_id, slot: 1|0 (slot vote vs decline), on: 1|0 (added vs removed), via: in_app|push_action}; event.vote_failed {event_id, via, reason: server|timeout}. Denominators: push_outbox rows with category event_vote / event_confirmed for the push path; for in-app, page.view EventDetail per session (no id - acceptable, or add {id} to page.view props for parameterised routes, which the emitter does not do today). Same headless-emitter prerequisite as pickleague_match_confirm_via_blind.

**Ground truth already available:** public.event_slot_votes and public.event_declines hold every saved answer (usage lane reads votes); push_outbox holds the sends.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Keep the gap, fix the denominator. push_outbox.category is notifications.category, the preference gate (migration_push_outbox.sql:34), NOT the button category: event_vote / event_confirmed are resolved at send time in supabase/functions/send-push/index.ts:100-106 (league_events.status === 'voting' -> event_vote; confirmed_slot_id -> event_confirmed) and placed only on the Expo message as categoryId (_kit/outboxDispatch.ts:155-162); neither push_outbox nor push_deliveries persists it. Push-path denominator is therefore push_outbox rows with entity_type='event' and status='sent' (approximate: the voting/confirmed split must be re-derived from league_events at read time), or the outbox needs a persisted button_category column - that half is an outbox/studio-lane gap, not an app-emitter gap. Also narrow the title: during voting the push carries only "Can't make it" (notificationCatalog.data.ts:16-20); "I'm in" exists only on event_confirmed (:22-27), so event.vote {slot:1, via:push_action} can only occur for an already-confirmed event. Title becomes: "Event votes/declines from the push buttons and from EventDetail are indistinguishable (no source column, no emitter); vote removals and save failures leave no record anywhere."
- *ground-truth:* Keep the proposal (event.vote {event_id, slot, on, via}; event.vote_failed {event_id, via, reason}). Fix the push denominator: push_outbox has no action-category column - `category` there is the notification preference category (migration_push_outbox.sql:33), and event_vote/event_confirmed are computed in send-push/index.ts:100-107 and stamped only on the Expo message. Denominator for the push path = push_outbox rows where entity_type='event' and status='sent' (recipient_id, entity_id, sent_at), optionally split by kind ('event_start'/'event_start_soon' from migration_event_reminders_v2.sql:35-36 are post-finalisation so would have carried event_confirmed). Alternative DB-side fix worth noting for the verifier: add a nullable `via text` column to event_slot_votes and event_declines written by notificationActions.ts - that answers the 'which button' half from ground truth, but still cannot record failures or retractions (DELETE at EventDetailScreen.tsx:215-219), so the event is still needed for those.
- *worth-it:* Keep the gap; fix the denominator, the impression, the reason enum and one name reuse.

1. The push-path denominator as written does not exist. push_outbox.category is notifications.category, a PREFERENCE key ('notifyEventReminders', 'notifyLeagueUpdates'...), per supabase/migration_push_outbox.sql:34 and :153-155; send-push resolves the BUTTON category (event_vote / event_confirmed) at send time from league_events.status (supabase/functions/send-push/index.ts:100-106) and persists it nowhere (push_deliveries has outbox_id, token, ticket_id, status, error only - migration_push_outbox.sql:62-71). Correct denominator: push_outbox where entity_type='event' and status='sent' (join push_deliveries status='ok' for devices reached), with the button set UNKNOWN per row. If the owner wants button-level sends, that is a send-push change (stamp the resolved category onto push_outbox or push_deliveries), not an app event.

2. Push-path attempt event: do not invent one - add 'pickleague' to the existing push.opened (taxonomy line 377, doggle-only today) with props {action: tap|event_accept|event_decline|match_confirm|...}. pickleague emits nothing on a push response today (mobile/src/lib/push.ts:198-200 wires onAction to handleNotificationAction with no track; the only track() calls in the app are auth.login, account.created, appstore.click). push.opened{action} is then the attempt, event.vote{via:push_action} the outcome, and a zero is readable.

3. In-app impression: page.view {route:'EventDetail'} (analytics.ts:583-587) carries no id or voting state, so 'saw an open vote and did not answer' is not computable. Emit event.view {event_id, status: voting|confirmed|closed|cancelled, mine: none|voted|declined} once from EventDetailScreen load() rather than changing trackScreen for every parameterised route.

4. Outcome events (names are fine - 'event' is a new area because pickleague has no product areas yet, and X.Y_failed matches trial.start_failed / puzzle.guess_failed): event.vote {event_id, slot: 1|0, on: 1|0, via: in_app|push_action}; event.vote_failed {event_id, slot, via, reason}. Widen reason to the refusal classes the code already separates: in_app -> server|timeout (sbCall path, EventDetailScreen.tsx:229, :262); push_action -> no_session (requireUser, notificationActions.ts:73-77), needs_app (needs(), :80-83, missing confirmed_slot_id/entity_id), stale ('no longer pending|expired|not found', :89-91), timeout (runNotificationAction 10 s), server. Never the message text. Note push duplicates (23505) are treated as success (:109-113) and must count as on:1, not failed.

5. Headless caveat to state in the spec: track() goes through ensureSession() (analytics.ts:255-345), so a lock-screen button press would mint an analytics_sessions row and a session.start. Either stamp those sessions (props is_background:1 or a session column) or exclude via=push_action sessions from DAU/session counts, or the buttons inflate pickleague sessions the day they ship.

Ground truth stands as stated: event_slot_votes (voted_at) and event_declines hold saved answers but have no source column (schema.sql:102-108; migration_event_declines_and_min_players.sql:26-33), and a failed insert leaves no row, so neither question is answerable from tables alone.

### 8. [MEDIUM] `pickleague_guest_upgrade_untracked` — A guest-pass holder becoming a full account emits nothing; account.created only knows email signups
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Of guests who redeemed an event invite, how many saw the upgrade banner and how many finished creating an account?

**Evidence:** UpgradeAccountScreen.tsx:96-128: supabase.auth.updateUser({email,password,data}) then rpc('complete_guest_upgrade',{p_full_name,p_gender,p_phone}); grep shows no `track` import or call in the file. The guest entry is recorded (GuestJoinScreen.tsx:114 auth.login{via:'guest_invite'}) so the funnel has a top and no bottom. RegisterScreen.tsx:93 is the only account.created call site (via 'email'). HomeScreen.tsx:415 renders <GuestUpgradeBanner/> with no impression event (components/GuestUpgradeBanner.tsx not instrumented; no track import). Registered gap upgrade_unconfirmed covers michi's submitted-vs-completed upgrades, not pickleague's missing emitter.

**Proposal:** track('account.created', {via: 'guest_upgrade'}) after the complete_guest_upgrade RPC succeeds (after UpgradeAccountScreen.tsx:118, not after updateUser, so it counts completed upgrades - the michi lesson). Denominator: prompt.shown {prompt: 'guest_upgrade', surface: 'home_banner'} when GuestUpgradeBanner renders for a guest, prompt.answered {prompt: 'guest_upgrade', response: accepted} on its tap. Failure: account.create_failed is not a taxonomy name; reuse the registered no_error_events pattern only if a pickleague error family is specced; otherwise log the RPC error branch as prompt.answered response 'abandoned' is wrong - leave failures to a later gap.

**Ground truth already available:** profiles.is_guest / guest_expires_at flip on complete_guest_upgrade; guest_invites records redemptions. Check whether complete_guest_upgrade stamps an upgraded_at - the screen passes none, so time-to-upgrade is likely unrecoverable from tables.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Three narrowings. (1) The denominator cannot come from analytics_sessions.is_guest on pickleague: analytics.ts:301 stamps is_guest as `!user.id` (signed-out visitor), and a guest-invite redeemer is an anon AUTH user with a uid, so their sessions read is_guest=false. The studio's "Guest to account" funnel (config/events.json ~882-939, match 'guest') therefore does not see pickleague invite guests at all; the proposal's auth.login{via:'guest_invite'} count is the only stream-side top of funnel, and the session-level guest flag is a separate studio-lane mismatch worth naming. (2) prompt.shown / prompt.answered are declared for apps ["doggle","michi"] only in config/events.json taxonomy; adding them to pickleague requires appending "pickleague" to both apps lists in the same change or coverage will treat them as undeclared. (3) groundTruth correction: guest_invites records invites, not redemptions (no redeemed_at/redeemed_by column; redeem_guest_invite logs nothing), so "guest_invites records redemptions" is wrong - redemptions are recoverable only as profiles with is_guest=true (plus league_members.expires_at not null), and completed upgrades only as profiles that flipped to is_guest=false with no timestamp. Proposal otherwise stands: account.created{via:'guest_upgrade'} after the RPC succeeds (after UpgradeAccountScreen.tsx:118), prompt.shown{prompt:'guest_upgrade',surface:'home_banner'} when GuestUpgradeBanner.tsx renders with isGuest true (after line 49), prompt.answered{prompt:'guest_upgrade',response:'accepted'} in its onPress (line 55).
- *ground-truth:* Two corrections to the gap text, verdict otherwise stands. (a) groundTruth is wrong that "guest_invites records redemptions": guest_invites is one row per invite token with no redeemed_by/redeemed_at (migration_guest_event_invites.sql:72-82), and redeem_guest_invite inserts no redemption row (migration_guest_upgrade.sql:107-116). Neither the number of redemptions nor the number of upgrades is recoverable from tables - not just time-to-upgrade. (b) Add to the proposal a server-side column so the count is recoverable going forward: have complete_guest_upgrade stamp profiles.upgraded_at timestamptz (missing column; it currently only clears is_guest/guest_expires_at at migration_guest_upgrade.sql:180-186) - or a guest_invite_redemptions ledger (invite_id, user_id, redeemed_at) written by redeem_guest_invite. The event proposal (account.created {via:'guest_upgrade'} after the RPC, prompt.shown/prompt.answered {prompt:'guest_upgrade', surface:'home_banner'}) is correct and needs 'pickleague' added to the apps list of prompt.shown / prompt.answered in analytics-studio/config/events.json:271-287, which today lists only doggle and michi. Also note for the verifier: analytics_sessions.upgraded_at measures guest ENTRY for pickleague guest-pass holders (claim fires at signInAnonymously, analytics.ts:649-654 + GuestJoinScreen.tsx:109), so the pickleague-visitor-signup funnel's 'claimed' stage (events.json:966-998, match 'converted') is not an upgrade signal.
- *worth-it:* Title: Pickleague guest-pass upgrades emit no account.created; the Home upgrade banner has no impression or click event.

Events (all pickleague, plain track() from mobile/src/lib/analytics.ts; add 'pickleague' to the apps list of account.created is already done - add these new names to the taxonomy):
1. account.upgrade_shown { surface: 'home_banner' } - in GuestUpgradeBanner.tsx, fire once per mount when isGuest becomes true (the render at `if (!isGuest) return null` flipping to the banner), NOT prompt.shown: a passive banner never resolves, and the prompt family's orphan semantics ('left with it open') and doggle's abandon-on-background helper would misread every non-tapper.
2. account.upgrade_click { surface: 'home_banner' } - in the banner onPress before navigationRef.navigate('UpgradeAccount'). The UpgradeAccount screen view itself is already recorded as page.view {route:'UpgradeAccount'} (analytics.ts:587) and is the attempt denominator for 3/4.
3. account.created { via: 'guest_upgrade' } - UpgradeAccountScreen.tsx after `if (rpcErr) {...}` at :119, before refreshSession at :124. This is product completion (profiles.is_guest -> false, league_members permanent). It is NOT email confirmation; confirmation stays a ground-truth read (auth.users.is_anonymous = false / email_confirmed_at joined to profiles), same caveat shape as michi's upgrade_unconfirmed. Mark the pickleague-visitor-signup funnel 'signed' stage with a caveat key until this lands (doggle's twin already carries one).
4. account.upgrade_failed { reason: 'auth' | 'rpc' | 'thrown' } (failure: true) - at :103-106 (authErr), :116-119 (rpcErr), :128-130 (catch). reason is the fixed enum of which branch fired; never authErr.message / rpcErr.message. Follows the landed no_error_events pattern and the trial.start_failed/feedback.failed { reason } shape.

Remove from proposal: prompt.shown / prompt.answered for this surface. Keep: ground-truth note (no upgraded_at anywhere on profiles/guest_invites; analytics_sessions.upgraded_at is the redemption claim, not the upgrade), so time-to-upgrade is only recoverable from the stream once (3) lands.

### 9. [MEDIUM] `pickleague_rating_prompt_blind` — The store-rating ask discards the only signal the OS gives back
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** How many players have been asked to rate, after which good moment, and how many good moments accrue before the first ask? The App Store shows ratings, not asks, so the ask rate is unmeasurable today.

**Evidence:** ratingPrompt.ts:12-14 `void note(POLICY).catch(() => {})` - the boolean from expo-foundation/src/platform/storeReview.ts:72-110 (true only when requestReview() was called, :99-106) is dropped. Call sites: MatchHistoryScreen.tsx:331 after a confirmed match, CourtCheckinCard.tsx:96 after a non-queued check-in. Policy minMoments 2, cooldown 120 days, 3/year (ratingPrompt.ts:10). No prompt.shown with prompt 'store_review' or any review.* name in config/events.json; prompt.shown/prompt.answered exist for doggle+michi.

**Proposal:** Make the wrapper await the boolean and emit prompt.shown {prompt: 'store_review', surface: match_confirm|checkin} when it is true (add 'pickleague' to prompt.shown apps). Denominator: review.moment {surface} on every noteGoodMoment call (new name; moments are the attempt). State in the report that prompt.answered can never fire for store_review - Apple does not report whether the sheet appeared or was answered - so the asks rollup must not read 0 answers as 100% declined; a 'shown with no answer' here is the OS, not the person (same shape as the _promptsDoc 'left with it open' rule).

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrowings: (a) `requestReview()` resolving means the OS was asked, not that the sheet appeared (storeReview.ts:2-4 header; iOS caps at 3/year and suppresses first-session asks) - name the event or doc the impression as 'asked', not 'shown'. (b) The denominator event should fire from inside the wrapper (ratingPrompt.ts:12) rather than per call site, so both surfaces are covered by one change; pass surface as an enum arg (match_confirm | checkin). (c) MatchHistoryScreen.tsx:331 and CourtCheckinCard.tsx:94 also have no success events of their own (match.confirmed, checkin.create) - pickleague still emits only the four plumbing names doggle_product_blind described, so this gap is a slice of a broader pickleague product-blind gap that the registered entry's `fix` text (events.json:1230) only resolves for doggle.
- *ground-truth:* Evidence line fix: the CourtCheckinCard call site is CourtCheckinCard.tsx:94 (`else noteGoodMoment();`), not :96. Partial ground truth for the DENOMINATOR only: confirmed matches and non-queued court check-ins already exist as product rows (matches / court check-in tables), so the studio can state "good moments accrued" from ground truth; the ask itself (numerator) still needs prompt.shown {prompt:'store_review', surface} from the wrapper awaiting the boolean. Keep prompt.answered explicitly unemittable for store_review.
- *worth-it:* Severity: low. App: pickleague only (sole consumer of expo-foundation noteGoodMoment). Events (new area, pickleague only; do NOT add pickleague to prompt.shown): `review.moment {surface}` emitted synchronously in ratingPrompt.ts noteGoodMoment() before calling note(); `review.requested {surface, moments}` emitted in `.then(asked => asked && track(...))` - keep it fire-and-forget as the file header requires ('never awaited by a button'). surface enum: match_confirm (MatchHistoryScreen.tsx:331) | checkin (CourtCheckinCard.tsx:94). moments = the count that tripped the ask (needs noteGoodMoment in expo-foundation to return {asked, moments} or the wrapper to read it; if the foundation API stays boolean, drop the moments prop rather than guess). Denominator: review.moment; outcome: review.requested; no answered half exists and the report must say so inline: 'requested' = requestReview() resolved, which Apple may silently suppress (3 per 365d OS cap), so requested/moment is an eligibility rate, not a shown rate, and ratings can only be read off App Store Connect. Title: 'The store-rating ask is counted on-device and nowhere else'. groundTruth: none - state is AsyncStorage under key pickleague_review (storeReview.ts load/save), so this is an app-instrumentation gap, not a studio-lane gap.

### 10. [LOW] `pickleague_signup_claim_stage_structural` — The visitor funnel's 'Signed in on that visit' stage cannot be reached by an email signup
**Status:** **SURVIVED** (3/3)  
**Lane:** pickleague-product · **App:** pickleague

**Question it answers:** Is the stage-4 zero 'nobody converts on the visit' or 'the product cannot do that'? Today it reads as the former.

**Evidence:** config/events.json funnels pickleague-visitor-signup stage 4 'claimed' match 'converted' (session claimed on the same visit). RegisterScreen.tsx:93-95 fires account.created on signUp success and shows 'Please check your email to confirm, then sign in', then navigates to Login after 3 s; the claim happens only when onAuthStateChange delivers a user (AppNavigator.tsx:231 -> analytics.ts:651-657), which cannot occur before the confirmation email is clicked, usually in another tab or on another day with a fresh session. data/events.json 30d: signed 0, claimed 0 - so no row yet contradicts it, but the stage is structurally near zero regardless of product performance. The only same-visit claim path is GuestJoinScreen (signInAnonymously, :104-114), which emits auth.login not account.created and so never enters stage 3.

**Proposal:** Studio-side: either relabel stage 4 to 'Confirmed email and signed in (any later visit)' matched on auth.login by a user whose account.created exists in the window, or carry an inline caveat next to the stage pointing at this gap. Ground truth for the real question is auth.users.email_confirmed_at vs created_at on pickleague's project (confirmation lag and never-confirmed share); no emitter change. Keep account.created at signUp success - it is the attempt - and read confirmations from the table.

**Ground truth already available:** auth.users.email_confirmed_at and created_at (pickleague project); profiles.created_at.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Narrow 'cannot be reached' to 'cannot be reached on web via the normal confirm-in-a-new-tab path'. Two same-visit paths DO exist and must be acknowledged in any caveat: (a) native: a signed-out visitor gets a session row (analytics.ts:256 - cachedUser is {id:null}, truthy), and the app-review flow 'tap link -> browser confirms -> back to the app -> sign in' (docs/app-review-notes.md:316-318) fires SIGNED_IN -> AppNavigator.tsx:218-231 resetSessionUser -> analytics.ts:649-654 claimSession on the still-live in-memory sessionId, so stage 4 is reachable on native whenever the app process survives the trip to Mail; (b) web: if the original tab stays open while the confirm link lands in a sibling tab of the same browser, GoTrue's cross-tab session broadcast (persistSession true, createSupabase.ts:103) delivers SIGNED_IN to the original tab and claims the live row; returning to the original tab and signing in via LoginScreen.tsx:43-48 does the same. So the stage is 'structurally rare on web, viable on native', not 'structurally zero'. Proposal fix: the suggested studio-side relabel 'auth.login by a user whose account.created exists in the window' cannot be joined by uid (account.created rows for email signups have user_id null, analytics.ts:296-298); join on device_id (analytics_sessions.device_id, fixed gap guest_device_churn) or read auth.users.email_confirmed_at/created_at as proposed. Also note doggle's doggle-visitor-signup stage 4 (config/events.json:959-962) shares the pattern - doggle/app/src/data/auth.ts:29-37 fires account.created on signUp() the same way - so the caveat likely belongs on both visitor funnels (doggle not verified here).
- *ground-truth:* Title: "The visitor funnel's 'Signed in on that visit' stage is reachable by an email signup only if the visitor confirms and signs in within the 30-minute idle window on the same device (analytics.ts:51,270-275,651-657), so it reads 'confirmed later' as 'never converted'". Keep proposal as a studio lane: caveat or relabel stage 4, and read confirmation lag / never-confirmed share from auth.users.created_at vs email_confirmed_at (profiles.created_at as the attempt timestamp). Note config.toml:226 enable_confirmations=false is local-only; hosted enforcement is documented at docs/app-review-notes.md:321.
- *worth-it:* Title: "Pickleague's 'Signed in on that visit' stage only counts signups whose email confirmation and sign-in land inside the same live session; confirm-later is invisible and the stage carries no caveat". Severity: low. Proposal (studio only, no emitter change): (1) drop option A - it cannot be built from the stream because account.created fired signed-out has user_id null (analytics.ts:19-21, RegisterScreen.tsx:93) and only a claim joins it; (2) add a `caveat` on config/events.json pickleague-visitor-signup stage `claimed` (:995-997) pointing at a new gap key `pickleague_confirm_lag_unseen` (status open, apps [pickleague]) whose detail states the two same-visit paths (native app kept alive: analytics.ts:651-657; web same browser within 30 min: analytics.ts:51, :262-270) and that everything else is lost; (3) ground truth lane for the real question: on pickleague's project, auth.users.email_confirmed_at - created_at for is_anonymous=false users (median lag, share never confirmed, share confirmed >30 min later), counts only, reported in the pickleague section with the standard exclusions and the usual excluded-count line; (4) relabel stage 3 'Created an account (unconfirmed)' and keep account.created {via:'email'} at signUp success as the attempt. Cross-reference upgrade_unconfirmed (:1159) as the michi/tcgscan sibling; do not merge, the mechanism (signUp vs updateUser) and the session model (anon-role identity-less vs in-place uid) differ.

<details><summary>Lens verdicts</summary>

## 4. tcgscan-app, and its symmetry with michi-maker

**Mostly UNVERIFIED.** All but one of this lane's verifier agents died in the transient failure burst. The finder's evidence stands on its own; the three-lens check has not happened. Treat each item as a lead with file:line, not a confirmed gap.

### 1. [BLOCKING] `tcgscan_binder_gate_blind` — The 3-free-binder-pages wall emits nothing: no cap.gate_shown, no pro.offer_shown, no dismissal
**Status:** **PARTIAL** (2/2 lenses that answered said real; the rest failed on a transient error)  
**Lane:** tcgscan-product · **App:** tcgscan

**Question it answers:** How many Free accounts hit the binder-page wall, how many were offered the trial there, and what share started it or walked away?

**Evidence:** gate-prompt.ts:54 `if (opts.cap) trackCapGate(...)` and :87 `if (opts.cap) trackCapGateDismissed(...)` - both skipped when cap is omitted. Both binder-wall call sites omit it: scan.tsx:690-706 showGatePrompt({ tier, icon:'camera', title:`Your ${FREE_BINDER_PAGES} free binder pages are used`, forceUpgrade:true, trial: offerTrial }) and use-submit-picks.ts:433-440 showGatePrompt({ tier:'free', title:'Your 3 free binder pages are used', forceUpgrade:true, trial: trialOffer }). gate-prompt.ts:37-40 says cap is omitted 'for boolean feature locks' - but this wall is a counted cap (binder-taste.ts:21 FREE_BINDER_PAGES = 3, :35 binderPagesUsed(uid)). The gate's 'Start free trial' button (gate-prompt.ts:71,75 opts.trial()) calls startTrial('binder_gate') (scan.tsx:437, use-submit-picks.ts:102) with no pro.offer_shown - the only impression emitter is TrialCta.tsx:59 and TrialCta is not rendered here - and no trial.start_click (zero call sites in tcgscan src). The option landed in 51a02a6 (2026-09-11), after config/events.json's cap-wall-trial `_scope` note ('a tcgscan user who hits a scan cap is shown no trial at all'), which is now stale. Only a press that succeeds leaves anything: server-side trial.start {surface:'binder_gate'} (trial.ts:44).

**Proposal:** Pass `cap` at both call sites: { limit: 'binderPages', surface: 'scan', used: binderPagesUsed(uid) } (add a tier_caps row app='tcgscan', limit_key='binderPages', tier='free', value=3 so the 'limit_key verbatim' rule holds, or document it as the one client-side key). In showGatePrompt, when opts.trial is present emit pro.offer_shown { surface: 'binder_gate' } once per prompt and trial.start_click { surface: 'binder_gate' } on press; keep trial.start server-side. cap.gate_dismissed then fires for 'Not now' via the existing guard. Pairing: cap.gate_shown (impression) -> pro.offer_shown -> trial.start_click -> tcgscan_pro_trials (truth). Then drop the MICHI ONLY scope on the cap-wall-trial funnel for tcgscan.

**Ground truth already available:** tcgscan_pro_trials (surface = 'binder_gate') gives starts only - a numerator with no denominator.

**Verifier corrections (part of the finding):**
- *already-emitted:* Three corrections to the gap text, none of which refute it. (1) Dating: the `trial` option on showGatePrompt landed in 51a02a6 (2026-09-11), but the 3-free-pages Free wall itself landed in 977698b (2026-09-20, 'A Free account files three binder pages before the wall'); both postdate the cap-wall-trial `_scope` note. (2) Scope: a third uninstrumented variant sits at the same chokepoint, the GUEST binder gate at scan.tsx:690-696 ('Binder pages need a free account', no forceUpgrade -> 'Create an account' at gate-prompt.ts:71-74); it is a sign-in gate, not a counted cap, so register it as offer='signin' rather than a cap row. (3) Proposal: the missing trial.start_click for tcgscan is ALREADY registered under gaps.tcgscan_cap_gate_parity (specced) - narrow this gap to the impression pair (cap.gate_shown at the binder wall + pro.offer_shown { surface: 'binder_gate' } when opts.trial is present) and cite parity for the click. Also note that gate-prompt.ts:99-103 capFor() reads activeLimits(), which has no 'binderPages' key (tiers.ts:74 exposes only the boolean fullPageBinderScan), so without either a tier_caps row (app='tcgscan', limit_key='binderPages', tier='free', value=3) or an explicit cap passed by the call site, `cap` would write the -1 'unlimited' sentinel; the proposal must pick one and say which. Ground truth stands as stated: tcgscan_pro_trials(surface='binder_gate') is starts only, and the denominator never reaches a server (binder-taste.ts:9-14, AsyncStorage per uid).
- *ground-truth:* Keep the gap; amend the proposal in four places. (a) Use ONE surface string, 'binder_gate', for cap.gate_shown, pro.offer_shown, trial.start_click and the existing RPC p_surface (not 'scan' for the cap and 'binder_gate' for the offer): analytics.ts:561-562 requires the same string so gate->offer->trial is a join, and tcgscan_pro_trials already carries 'binder_gate'. (b) Do not add a tier_caps row: binder-taste.ts:11-14 is an owner-documented decision to keep this count off the server. Instead pass cap explicitly: gate-prompt.ts:99-103 capFor() reads activeLimits(), where 'binderPages' is absent and would normalize to the -1 'unlimited' sentinel; either extend `cap` with an optional `cap?: number` (FREE_BINDER_PAGES) or add binderPages to the TIER_LIMITS mirror, and register 'binderPages' in config/events.json as the one documented client-side limit_key. (c) Cross-link gaps.tcgscan_cap_gate_parity (events.json:1205): landing trial.start_click here also closes part of it; the parity gap's useCapGate fix must preserve this. (d) Scope note: the trial offer at this wall is Android/web only (use-trial.ts:22,72 APPLE_TRIAL_ONLY -> 'ineligible' on iOS with RC key) and michi's trial was closed 2026-10-11 (migration 20261011120000_close_in_app_trial.sql rewrites start_pro_trial only; start_tcgscan_pro_trial is untouched). cap.gate_shown + cap.gate_dismissed at 'binder_gate' is the durable denominator; pro.offer_shown/trial.start_click there may be short-lived if tcgscan's trial follows michi into Checkout.
