### 2. [HIGH] `tcgscan_game_blind` — Nothing emits which game is active or was scanned, and no server table can recover it
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** tcgscan-product · **App:** tcgscan

**Question it answers:** What is the game split of sessions, scans and adds since One Piece and Lorcana shipped, and does scan success differ by game?

**Evidence:** switchGame (game.ts:160-178) and the selector press (game-select.tsx:96-101: setPending(g); switchGame(g)) emit nothing; game.ts imports only react-native. No event carries game: scan.capture scan.tsx:877 { mode:'live', cards }, :1066 { mode:'still' }, :1078 { mode:'library' }, scan.web.tsx:1654 { mode:'web', cards }; card.add auto-add.ts:364,506 { source:'scan' }; collection.card_add add-to-collection-modal.tsx:547 { size }. `game` is already in scope at every one of those sites (scan.tsx:377,384 useGame(); scan.web.tsx:177,183,456; add-to-collection-modal.tsx:179). Server side: card ids are TCGplayer productIds, 'one numeric namespace across every game' (card-sources.ts:41-44); the id->game map is client memory only (other-game.ts:229-247 gameOfId); scan_events has card_id/confidence/auto_added/mode and no game (20260816120000_tcgscan_lifetime_scans_and_metadata.sql:27,58-62); the only migrations mentioning 'game' are the michi daily-puzzle ones. The choice persists in localStorage 'tcgscan.game' / game.txt (game.ts:25,46), nowhere on the server.

**Proposal:** game.switch { from: 'pokemon'|'onepiece'|'lorcana', to: <same enum>, surface: 'home'|'scan'|'browse' } from GameSelect's press (not from switchGame, so ?game= deep links are not counted as a press); and a `game` prop (same enum) on scan.capture, scan.failed, card.add { source:'scan' }, collection.card_add and collection.sealed_add. Denominator for scan success per game = scan.capture { game }; outcome = card.add { source:'scan', game }. Studio: read game as a dimension on the activation funnel; never infer it from card_id.

### 3. [HIGH] `tcgscan_native_scan_failure_blind` — Native scanning emits no scan.failed and still/library captures never record how many cards were found
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** tcgscan-product · **App:** tcgscan

**Question it answers:** On the iPhone build, what share of scans fail or find nothing, by mode and game, versus the web?

**Evidence:** scan.failed has four web call sites (scan.web.tsx:2287 no_camera, :2312 camera_error, :2761 stalled, :2791 gpu_lost) and ZERO in scan.tsx. Native recognize failure lands at scan.tsx:741 `if (gen === shotGen.current) setRecoErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e))` inside a catch with no track. Still and library captures omit `cards` by design (scan.tsx:1064-1066 'The card count isn't known until recognize() runs, so it's omitted'), so a photo that resolved to nothing and a photo that resolved to five cards are the same row. Studio coverage: scan.failed is in neverFired (data/events.json apps.tcgscan.coverage) - consistent with no native emitter, not with no failures; 23 of 72 sessions in the 30d window are ios.

**Proposal:** Native scan.failed { reason: 'recognize_error' | 'camera_denied' | 'no_match', mode: 'still' | 'library' | 'live', game } at scan.tsx:741 and the permission-denied branch near :1083. Move the still/library scan.capture emission to after recognize() resolves so `cards` is stamped (same shape as live/web), or emit scan.recognized { mode, cards, game } there if the pre-recognize timing must stay. Pairing: scan.capture (attempt) vs card.add { source:'scan' } + scan.failed (outcomes), split by mode and game.

**Ground truth already available:** scan_events (record_scan_event, scan-quota.ts:347-351; mode single|riffle|binder, confidence, auto_added) counts COMMITTED adds only - successes without attempts, and without game.

### 4. [HIGH] `tcgscan_scan_consent_blind` — The Save-my-scans consent prompt and its two switches emit nothing, and the answer lives only on the device
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** tcgscan-product · **App:** tcgscan

**Question it answers:** Of the accounts asked to keep their scans, what share said yes, what share also allowed research, how many said Not now, and how many flip the switch later in Settings?

**Evidence:** scan-consent-prompt.tsx:313-319 answer() -> answerConsent(keep, keep && share) then onDone; :323 onRequestClose -> answer(false,false); :368 'Not now' -> answer(false,false); :374 primary -> answer(save, research). No analytics import in the file (the 15-file import list excludes it). answerConsent (scan-consent.ts:236-239) writes settings only; settings.ts:355 STORAGE_KEY 'tcgscan.settings.v8' in AsyncStorage, scanConsent per uid at :291 - no server row. Settings flips setSaveScans/setResearch (scan-consent.ts:246-259; settings.tsx:274,287) emit nothing. The second door (card-image-toggle.tsx:91 setAsking(true) when picking 'scan' unanswered) emits nothing. Ask timing is gated on CONSENT_MIN_ADDS=10 / CONSENT_MIN_SESSIONS=2 (scan-consent.ts:43-45,98-106) so 'asked' is far rarer than 'scanned' and needs its own impression. New evidence against config/events.json `_asymmetryDoc` ('the prompts genuinely do not exist in tcgscan (no uninvited dialogs)'): this IS an uninvited dialog, shipped 2026-09-22/24 (d57ab2a, 0706d32).

**Proposal:** Port michi's trackPromptShown/trackPromptAnswered verbatim (analytics.ts michi :676-697, plus openPrompts + abandonOpenPromptsBeacon :716-744 and the pagehide hook :457). Emit prompt.shown { prompt: 'scan-consent', surface: 'scan' | 'collection', held: <n> } when the Modal becomes visible, and prompt.answered { prompt: 'scan-consent', surface, response, save: <bool>, research: <bool>, held: <n> } with response = 'accepted' (primary with save on), 'declined' (primary 'Done' with save off), 'dismissed' (Not now / back / Escape), 'abandoned' (unload with it open). For Settings flips add consent.switch { switch: 'save_scans' | 'research', on: <bool> } (no 'consent.*' name exists in the taxonomy). Add 'scan-consent' to the prompts doc and 'tcgscan' to prompt.shown/prompt.answered apps.

**Ground truth already available:** Partial, yes-side only: portfolio_entries.scan_path IS NOT NULL per user (20260828120000_scan_images.sql:17-20; late fill 20260925120000_scan_path_keep.sql) = Save my scans on AND a scanned add; scan_feedback rows (scan-feedback.ts:151) = research on. 'Asked', 'declined', 'dismissed' and the asked count have no server record.

### 6. [MEDIUM] `tcgscan_scan_image_pipeline` — Scan photo upload and the held-until-consent drain emit nothing; the studio can read the outcome from scan_path vs the bucket instead
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** tcgscan-product · **App:** tcgscan

**Question it answers:** Of the scans an opted-in account chose to keep, how many actually landed in the bucket, how many pointers dangle, and how many held crops were drained vs deleted at the consent answer?

**Evidence:** scan-image.ts:71 `const status = { staged, uploaded, failed, queued, lastError }` is module memory; failures go to console.warn via note() (:92, :548 upload, :644/:715 upload-web). The hold engine's commit/discard/drain (scan-hold-engine.ts:527-556, scan-hold.ts:117 discard) emit nothing. No analytics import in scan-image.ts, scan-hold.ts, scan-hold.web.ts or scan-hold-engine.ts. Ground truth exists: portfolio_entries.scan_path is minted at entry creation while bytes upload async and 'the object may lag or never arrive' (20260828120000_scan_images.sql:17-20); held crops get scan_path written late once the object exists (20260925120000_scan_path_keep.sql header).

**Proposal:** Studio-lane first: a scan-images reconciliation in the events or usage lane (pointers vs objects per user, exclusions applied, all-time plus windows). App side only if the dangle rate is material: scan.image_failed { reason: 'storage_4xx' | 'storage_5xx' | 'read' | 'paused', held: <bool> } at scan-image.ts:548 and :715, denominator = portfolio_entries rows with scan_path.

**Ground truth already available:** portfolio_entries.scan_path (pointer) joined to storage.objects where bucket_id = 'scan-images' (object) on the shared project: pointer with no object = never uploaded/failed; late-filled scan_path (updated_at after added_at) = drained held crop. Deleted-on-No crops leave no server trace by design.

### 7. [MEDIUM] `tcgscan_sync_blind` — Sync emits nothing: a failed pull, a refused wipe or a hit page ceiling is a local status and a console line
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** tcgscan-product · **App:** tcgscan

**Question it answers:** How often does sync fail for a real account, in which phase and why, and do the large (12k-card) accounts hit the paging ceiling or the wipe guard?

**Evidence:** sync.ts is not among the 15 files importing analytics. Failure paths: sync.ts:963 and :1016 `setStatus('error', e.message)` (the only record is the account line 'Sync error - tap to retry', :947); :560 `console.error('[sync] REFUSED a full-table delete', ...)` from the sync-gate (sync-gate.ts:110 licenseDeletes, WIPE_FLOOR 20 at :41); :811 `console.warn('[sync] mirror not persisted (sync continues)')`; sync-paging.ts readAllPages throws after MAX_PAGES (=500, :78) and the caller must not merge a partial table (:102). Pull reads 4 tables in 1000-row pages (sync.ts:420-445). No server table records a sync cycle; collections/portfolio_entries carry updated_at (state), not attempts.

**Proposal:** sync.reconciled { rows: <n>, pages: <n>, ms: <n>, tables: 4 } once per completed reconcile (denominator; fires on sign-in and foreground) and sync.failed { phase: 'pull' | 'push' | 'reconcile', reason: 'wipe_refused' | 'page_ceiling' | 'rls' | 'network' | 'mirror' | 'other' } at sync.ts:963, :1016, :560 and :811 with the reason chosen by a fixed classifier, never e.message. Props are counts and enums only; no table names or row ids.

### 8. [LOW] `tcgscan_ios_build_blind` — iOS sessions cannot be told apart by build, and no in-app install path exists to attribute an App Store install
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** tcgscan-product · **App:** tcgscan

**Question it answers:** Which iOS build did a session run, and once the listing is public, which web page, QR or banner sent the install?

**Evidence:** analytics.ts:268 `const appVersion = Constants.expoConfig?.version` -> analytics_sessions.app_version = '1.0.0' (app.json:5) for build 13 and build 14 alike; nativeBuildVersion is not read. Install path: +html.tsx:30-31 `const IOS_APP_ID = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_IOS_APP_ID ?? ''` ('Unset until the app ships'), smart banner conditional at :116; landing.html:27 forbids the App Store URL on the page; share-k7q3m.html carries no analytics tags; no QR to the store exists. platform is already on the session row (ios 23 of 72 sessions in the 30d window, data/events.json).

**Proposal:** Add build: <int> (Constants.nativeBuildVersion parsed) to session.start props in BOTH emitters (michi has no native build, so it emits nothing new - symmetric by construction). When the store link ships, mint it with Apple campaign tokens per surface (landing, smart banner, QR) and read App Store Connect in a studio lane; do not add an in-app event for a hop the app cannot see.

**Ground truth already available:** App Store Connect (installs, and campaign tokens ct=/pt= on the store URL) is the only source for install attribution; analytics_sessions.platform = 'ios' with first session.start per device_id is the in-app proxy for first open.

## 5. The studio's own contract

**UNVERIFIED** — same cause. These are studio-side changes (lanes, exclusions, funnels), not app changes.

### 1. [BLOCKING] `studio_activation_funnel_gated_by_demo` — The activation funnel makes 'Made something real' conditional on 'Tried a demo', so it emits a near-zero that the make events contradict
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** studio-contract · **App:** studio

**Question it answers:** Of the people who open michi or tcgscan, how many make something real - and does trying a demo first change that rate?

**Evidence:** config/events.json:830-878: funnel 'activation' (apps michi, tcgscan) orders arrived -> looked -> tried [demo.tricolor_search, demo.csv_import, demo.curation, demo.print] -> created [binder.add, csv.import, card.add, scan.capture, collection.create]; scripts/events.mjs:481-546 carries only users who cleared every earlier stage ('carried = next'). data/events.json 30d (2026-10-11): michi activation = arrived 592 / looked 590 / tried 21 / created 19, while eventsByName has binder.add 238 events / 175 users and card.add 6093 / 120 users; tcgscan activation = 43 / 42 / tried 0 / created 0 while scan.capture 7 events / 2 users, collection.create 2 / 2, card.add 5 / 2 - tcgscan has no demo.* call site at all (grep of tcgscan-app/src), so its 'tried' stage is a structural zero that zeroes 'created'. The 'tried' list also omits demo.theme_search (michi-maker/src/data/themeDemo.ts:21), the busiest demo: 603 events / 104 users in 30d vs 7 users for demo.tricolor_search; guestDepth.makeEvents (config:1275-1285) and signup.engaged (config:899-907) omit it too.

**Proposal:** Studio config change, no app change. Split the funnel: 'activation' becomes arrived -> looked -> created (names unchanged) so the make rate is measured on everyone; add a second funnel 'demo-to-make' (michi only) arrived -> looked -> tried -> created to answer whether demos help; add demo.theme_search to tried, to guestDepth.makeEvents and to signup.engaged. For tcgscan add its own activation funnel: session.start -> page.view -> card.open|card.search -> scan.capture|collection.create|card.add, and set the shared funnel's apps to michi only. Ground-truth cross-check for the final stage already exists in the DAU activity tables (apps.json activitySources: binders/binder_slots for michi, scan_events/collections for tcgscan).

**Ground truth already available:** public.binders, public.binder_slots (michi); public.scan_events, public.collections (tcgscan) - the activitySources in config/apps.json

### 2. [HIGH] `studio_coverage_cannot_tell_dead_from_untriggered` — Coverage emits 'never fired' with no view of whether a call site exists or whether the build carrying it ever ran, and all 24 never-fired names today are live code
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** studio-contract · **App:** studio

**Question it answers:** Which never-fired names are a missing emitter, which are a build that has not reached users, and which are simply behaviour that has not happened?

**Evidence:** scripts/events.mjs:1173 neverFired = declared.filter(n => !seenAny.has(n)) - rows only; scripts/events-report.mjs:457 prints 'Unverified - the call site may be unreachable' for every one. Checked against code 2026-10-11: michi compose.pages_kept michi-maker/src/store/binders.tsx:1146; trial.start_failed, trial.start_click components/monetization/TrialCta.tsx; search.no_results, feedback.failed, offer.checkout_failed, puzzle.guess_failed each 1 site (grep). tcgscan card.search src/app/(tabs)/browse/index.tsx:226 (findSimilar wrapper), cap.gate_dismissed lib/gate-prompt.ts:87 -> lib/analytics.ts:582, scan.failed 4 sites, scan.sealed_hint scan.tsx:3288 + scan.web.tsx:1741. doggle: push.permission_result unavailable/unsupported_platform at app/src/lib/PushNotificationContext.tsx:273 is reached on WEB by enablePushAlerts (:502-503) from screens/OnboardingScreen.tsx:323 - any web onboarding since the 2026-09-04 build (doggle 6d6f6b9) should have written a row, yet push.permission_result has zero rows all-time including ours (data/events.json doggle coverage), while prompt.shown/walk.start/checkin.create fired from excluded accounts only (neverFiredReal). The studio cannot tell whether the web deploy carries that build: all 81 doggle sessions in data/journeys.json read app_version 1.0.0 (app/app.json:5 is still 1.0.0, runtimeVersion policy fingerprint at :100). Pickleague account.created RegisterScreen.tsx:93 and doggle account.created data/auth.ts:37,51 are live sites never triggered. Zero dead declarations found; the panel text is wrong for all 24.

**Proposal:** Two studio changes. (1) events.mjs coverage gains firstSeenAt and lastSeenAt per name and a 'builds' table per app: sessions grouped by (platform, app_version, app_build) with first/last seen, so 'never fired' can be read next to 'the newest build seen is X'. (2) A call-site manifest: scripts/callsites.mjs greps each sibling repo's src for track('name' / the typed helper names exactly as scripts/campaigns.mjs already reads ../marketing-studio's registry, writes data/callsites.json (name -> count of sites, committed, no identity) and the coverage panel prints 'declared, N call sites, never fired' vs 'declared, 0 call sites' instead of one sentence for both. Deprecated names expect 0 sites and must fail the check if they gain one (config/events.json:634).

**Ground truth already available:** analytics_sessions.app_version / app_build (build identity); the app repos' source for call sites

### 3. [HIGH] `studio_no_build_identity_read` — The studio reads app_version only, while pickleague session rows now carry app_build and installed_at and doggle carries neither
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** studio-contract · **App:** studio

**Question it answers:** Who is still on an old build, how fast do people update after a release, and did the build carrying a new emitter reach anyone outside our own devices?

**Evidence:** scripts/events.mjs:76-80 selects s.app_version, to_jsonb(s)->>'device_id', to_jsonb(s)->>'landing_route' - no app_build, no installed_at; journeys carry appVersion only (events.mjs:1222). pickleague/supabase/migration_analytics_build.sql:14-16 adds app_build int + installed_at timestamptz ('app_version is the marketing version, which never moves between builds'), written by pickleague/mobile/src/lib/analytics.ts:305-309 via getNativeBuild/getInstallTime; commit 45df19a 'applied to prod'. Doggle has no app_build column (grep app_build|installed_at across doggle repo: no matches, despite the pickleague comment 'mirrors doggle 0400' - 0400 does not exist, migrations jump 0402-0404) and every doggle session in data/journeys.json is 1.0.0 (web 75, ios 6). data/events.json pickleague 30d: platforms web 14 / ios 1; journeys show versions 1.0.3 (11), 1.0.0 (3), 1.0.2 (1) - the minimum-version gate (pickleague commit ad156a6 'gate reads the binary version') has no studio readout.

**Proposal:** events.mjs: add to_jsonb(s)->>'app_build' and to_jsonb(s)->>'installed_at' to sessionsSqlFor (null-safe on doggle/michi where the column is absent), carry appBuild and installedAt on journeys, and add a 'builds' block per app per window: sessions and distinct visitors by (platform, app_version, app_build), first/last seen, days since install bucketed (0-1, 2-7, 8-30, 31+). Render in events-report under coverage with the note that web rows have null app_build by design. State that doggle has no build column so its rows cannot be split - a studio note, and a one-line work-order item for doggle to mirror the pickleague migration.

**Ground truth already available:** public.analytics_sessions (app_version, app_build, installed_at, platform) on each project

**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)

Pickleague sends live in public.push_outbox + public.push_deliveries (pickleague/supabase/migration_push_outbox.sql:28-66: kind, category, status pending|sending|sent|failed|expired, expires_at, ticket_id, receipt_checked_at) and tokens in public.push_tokens (migration_push_notifications.sql:24). Exclusions via recipient_id against exclusionCte, size stated inline. Pair every send count with the stream's push.opened users for the same app and day as numerator over sent as denominator; where push.opened is zero but sends are non-zero, print 'sent, no tap recorded' not 0%. No new event names.

### 5. [HIGH] `studio_unrecognised_is_per_taxonomy_not_per_app` — A name declared for one app is silently absorbed when another app emits it, and pickleague already emits a name the taxonomy has never heard of
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** studio-contract · **App:** studio

**Question it answers:** Is every name an app actually emits accounted for in that app's coverage, including names borrowed from a sibling app?

**Evidence:** scripts/events.mjs:1178 unrecognised = [...seenAny].filter(n => !(n in TAX)) and :551 known: e.name in TAX - both test the whole taxonomy, while declared (:1168-1169) is filtered by apps.includes(key). So a tcgscan row named prompt.shown or a pickleague row named push.opened is neither 'declared' nor 'unrecognised' for that app and drops out of coverage with known:true. pickleague/mobile/src/components/GetTheAppButton.tsx:63 emits track('appstore.click', { from }) (rendered at screens/LoginScreen.tsx:103, commit 9b148a4) - absent from config/events.json taxonomy; data/events.json pickleague coverage declared 4, unrecognised [] because no row has arrived yet. When the first row lands it will show as unrecognised (correct), but the push.*/prompt.* names pickleague will gain from the foundation port (doggle 34404bc work order) will not.

**Proposal:** events.mjs: compute unrecognised per app as seenAny minus the names whose apps include this key (planned included), and set known per (app, name). Register appstore.click in the taxonomy: label 'Followed the App Store link', stage acquisition, apps [pickleague], props { from } as a closed set (login today), and note it is web-only and leaves the page so the silence after it is not a drop-off (same reading as external_marketplace_search_clicked, config:635). Add pickleague to push.permission_requested / push.permission_result / push.token_registered / push.opened and prompt.shown / prompt.answered with a per-app planned list (a new plannedApps array, since planned:true today is per-name and would hide doggle's live sites from neverFired).

### 7. [MEDIUM] `studio_exclusions_vs_michi_is_test_account` — Michi now has its own test-account predicate and it disagrees with the studio's exclusion CTE in both directions
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** studio-contract · **App:** studio

**Question it answers:** Is a test account the same set of people in michi's Studio and in analytics-studio, and would a renamed rig domain be caught here?

**Evidence:** michi-maker commit 16207d1 (supabase/migrations/20261004120000_test_accounts_out_of_stats.sql:20-31): is_test_account(uid) = auth.users.email like '%@example.com' OR exists analytics_events where props->>'surface' = 'qa-rig'; used by admin_event_funnel (:45), admin_recent_users (:64), refresh_community_stats (:85). The rig stamps p_surface 'qa-rig' (michi-maker/qa/lib/accounts.mjs:95) on accounts named qa-rig-<tag>-<n>@example.com (accounts.mjs:38,43). Studio exclusionCte (scripts/lib/studio.mjs:128-160) keys only on email patterns/exact, userIds, auth.sessions.user_agent and guest IPs; config/exclusions.json emailPatterns include %@example.com so today's 23 rig accounts are excluded by email on both sides - but (a) the studio has no event-props clause, so a rig run with a different DOMAIN leaks into every studio number while michi's own funnel filters it, and (b) is_test_account does not flag the owner accounts (emailExact in exclusions.json) that the studio removes, so michi's admin funnel and the studio report different trial counts for the same question (16207d1 message: 'trial.start users 35 to 26').

**Proposal:** Studio change only. Extend exclusionCte with an optional eventProps rule in config/exclusions.json byApp.michi-maker/tcgscan: { table: 'analytics_events', prop: 'surface', equals: 'qa-rig' } compiled to 'exists (select 1 from public.analytics_events e where e.user_id = u.id and e.props->>''surface'' = ''qa-rig'')'; or call public.is_test_account(u.id) as an extra clause on the shared project. Add a note in exclusions.json and in the events report's exclusion line that michi's own predicate excludes rig/example.com but NOT owner accounts, so its admin counts run higher than the studio's by the owner set.

**Ground truth already available:** public.is_test_account(uuid) on project piikwvntldytjejxmcla

## 6. The daily digest for michi-maker + tcgscan — consolidation and additions

**UNVERIFIED** — same cause. The finder read digest.mjs, the last real email and both apps' recent commits; its proposals carry line numbers. This is the section the owner asked about directly, and it is the one the failure burst hit hardest.

Same shape at digest.mjs:210-211 `if (!m) continue`: an app absent from metrics.apps gets no glance row (433-453) and no 'active' block rather than a 'not collected' line.

Rendered line: 'TCGScan — entitlements not readable this run, so this is "not available" rather than zero.' (the sentence already written at digest.mjs:653, made reachable).

### 3. [HIGH] `digest_price_change_invisible` — 'What people bought' names product and interval but never the price, so the 2026-10-11 cut to $2.99/$24.99 is invisible

PERIOD_FLOOR_DAYS {month:20, year:300} and purchaseKind (events.mjs:1350-1359) classify by period length, so the price cut does NOT break trialing-vs-paid; a 3-day trial_end on a monthly sub still reads trialing. What breaks is wording: a `tier_pro month` row granted 2026-09-28 is $5.99 until its next renewal and $2.99 after, and the email cannot tell them apart.

**Proposal:** Product change (michi-maker payments-webhook + revenuecat-webhook :191-193): stamp `price_lookup_key text` and `unit_amount_minor int` on entitlements at every grant/renewal write. Studio: purchasesSql (events.mjs:274-275) selects the two columns; journeys purchase rows gain `amountMinor`, `lookupKey`; events.json purchases gains `mrrMinor` (sum over kind='paid' of amount normalised to monthly). Digest line (687): '@x — PRO monthly $2.99 via stripe, renewed 2026-11-01' and header 'What people bought — 1 paying, $2.99/mo recurring'. Until the column exists, print the seam honestly: 'granted before 2026-10-11: old price until next renewal' next to any row with at < 2026-10-11.

**Ground truth already available:** Stripe Price (lookup_key -> unit_amount) is the only authority; the webhook already resolves lookup_key at payments-webhook/index.ts:182 and writes entitlements at :127/:207/:259 without it.

**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)

Build: tcgscan-app/src/lib/analytics.ts:268-277 sends Constants.expoConfig.version only; every session reads app_version '1.0.0', so build 14 vs 13 is indistinguishable. Consent: tcgscan-app/src/components/scan-consent-prompt.tsx:109-117 answer(false,false)/answer(save,research) has no track(); tcgscan-app/src/lib/scan-consent.ts:22,162-167,236-238 persists answers only in device `settings.scanConsent`; no table or column holds consent (grep opt_in|consent|research over tcgscan-data and michi-maker migrations: none). The only server trace of a 'yes' is a crop object in the scan bucket (scan-image.ts:48,131,544,703), which cannot see a 'no'. Game: portfolio_entries has no game column (20260714120000_tcgscan_app_tables.sql portfolio_entries block: card_id, variant, condition, quantity, purchase_price, ...); scan.capture props are { mode, cards } (scan.tsx:877, 1066, 1078; scan.web.tsx:1654) with no game; game is derivable from card_id via cardGameOf (tcgscan-app/src/lib/other-game.ts:239). QR installs: marketing-studio campaigns.yaml:81-92 tcgscan codes wom/cardshow point at https://tcgscan.ai; 0 of 72 tcgscan sessions carried ?code (journeys landing); the App Store hop drops the query and tcgscan-app/src/store/auth.tsx:198 getInitialURL handles auth links only, so an install cannot be joined to a scan.

**Proposal:** Three parts. (a) Studio only: digest glance and 'who was active' gain a per-app platform note from windows['1'].totals.platforms — 'TCGScan — 3 active (2 iOS, 1 web)'; usage lane derives game via card_id prefix rules copied from other-game.ts:239 — 'cards added: 40 Pokemon, 12 One Piece, 3 Lorcana'. (b) tcgscan-app emitter (tcgscan-only surface, so no michi mirror needed): `scan.consent_shown { held }` once per sheet mount and `scan.consent_answered { save, research, held }` (two booleans + count) at scan-consent-prompt.tsx:109-117; digest line 'Save my scans: asked 6, yes 4 (2 also research), not now 2'. Register both in config/events.json taxonomy. (c) Installs: state in the email that QR->App Store installs are unmeasurable by design; if wanted, a web redirect page tcgscan.ai/ios?code=X records a page.view with the code in landing_route before bouncing to the App Store, which measures scans of the code, never installs — label it 'QR scans', not 'installs'.

**Ground truth already available:** analytics_sessions.platform (already fetched) answers the platform split; scan_captures.platform (20260809120000_scan_captures.sql) answers scans by platform; nothing answers consent or installs.

Keep the rule from CLAUDE.md: stated in text, never drawn.

### 8. [MEDIUM] `digest_glance_and_active_sections_duplicate` — Glance, New accounts and Who-was-active say the same people three times under two different windows
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** digest · **App:** digest

**Question it answers:** Who showed up yesterday, which of them were new, and what did each one do — in one list, without reconciling an email list against an @username list?

**Evidence:** digest.mjs:433-453 glance table (Active, New per app) -> 502-521 'New accounts' table repeats the New column as names -> 586-617 'Who was active' repeats the Active column as names (592 header `${appName} — N active account(s)`), then the 'did' table (597-608) switches population to the rolling-24h event stream (caveat only after, 611-616; and inline at 596). digest-latest.txt:5-9, 17-19, 27-33 show one person (the day's one signup) appearing in the glance count, the New accounts row, the Active list and the did table under two different names (email vs @username; acknowledged at digest.mjs:1017). The did table also lists people who are NOT in the active set (e.g. guests filtered by isReal at 97 but accounts active only in the stream) under a heading that says 'active yesterday'.

**Proposal:** One 'People' section per app: a single table keyed by person with columns Who (email, with @username in muted text when the roster resolves it — both already in journeys.sessions rows: user + email, events.mjs:1211-1212), Status (new | returning), Active (product tables, calendar day) and Did (24h stream). Build it by joining accounts.activeByDay and newAccounts (digest.mjs:115-135, 217) to journeys.sessions[].email -> user label, then attach didByPerson rows by label. Drop the separate 'New accounts since yesterday' table (502-521) and the per-app 'Active:' sentence (593). Keep the glance table as the only numbers-only view and keep the charts under it. Rendered row: '[an account email - redacted] (@username) · NEW · active · Created a binder, Added cards, Opened the daily puzzle (fresh)'. One footnote replaces 596 and 611-616.

**Ground truth already available:** metrics.json days[d].dau / accounts.activeByDay (calendar day, product tables) is the authoritative 'active'; the stream is the 'did'.

### 9. [MEDIUM] `digest_reach_surfaces_unread_and_unlabeled` — What's New, Discover and shared-binder reach are either unread by the digest or printed as raw paths
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** digest · **App:** digest

**Question it answers:** Did anyone read the What's New batch that announced the price cut and the winners; how many people browse Discover and go on to open a binder from it; how many visitors arrived via a shared binder yesterday?

**Evidence:** Shares: data/shares.json exists (shares.mjs:27,119-198; byWindow['1'] = views 20, visitorViews 5, visitors 4, firstTouch 1, reshares 0) and digest.mjs:53-58 never read()s it. Share emitters: share.link_created/copied/opened and binder.reshare are `planned` (config/events.json:321-355, coverage.planned, plannedLanded []) and ShareSheet.tsx:313-324 copy/Share.share handler has no track(); gap share_attribution is deferred (events.json:1053-1060) and that deferral still stands — the shares lane is the answer and the digest just does not print it. Discover: discover.tsx has 0 track() calls; binder opens from Discover carry `?back=` (discover.tsx:147 `router.push(/binder/${id}?back=...)`) but RouteTracker strips the query (RouteTracker.tsx:9-10, 25), so a Discover-sourced binder open is identical to a pasted link; 30d /discover = 234 views / 104 people (routes rollup). What's New: whats-new.tsx has 0 track(); the rail glow is date-based only (changelog.ts:2146-2159 freshPinned = pinned items dated within 7 days), no per-user seen state exists anywhere, so 'reach of a pin' = people who viewed /whats-new within FRESH_DAYS of the batch date; 30d /whats-new = 31 views / 23 people. Labels: config/events.json routes (684-733) lack /whats-new, /daily, /feedback, /contest-binders, /u/:id, /learn/*, so digest.mjs:604 Pages column prints raw paths.

**Proposal:** Studio: digest reads data/shares.json and prints one 'Reach (24h)' block: 'Shared binders: 4 visitors opened someone else\'s binder (1 arrived cold, 0 signed up) · Discover: 9 people · What\'s New: 3 people (batch 2026-10-11 pinned: price cut, contest winners)'; add route labels for /whats-new, /daily, /feedback, /contest-binders, /u/:id in config/events.json routes so the Pages column reads in English. App (michi, mirrored in tcgscan RouteTracker to keep the emitters identical): page.view gains an optional `from` prop drawn from an allowlisted query enum — `from` in {discover, card, nav, direct, share} read from ?back= (discover) or ?from= (the puzzle already uses ?from=, config/events.json:678) — ids only, never the raw query string. Then 'Discover -> binder opens' = page.view route /binder/:id with from=discover over people who viewed /discover (denominator).

**Ground truth already available:** binders.owner_id join for share arrivals (shares lane, already computed). None for Discover-sourced opens or What's New reads.

### 10. [LOW] `digest_theme_search_wall_and_demo_unqualified` — The busiest activation event and the busiest wall both print without saying which wall or which theme
**Status:** **UNVERIFIED** (all three verifier agents failed on a transient API error; finder evidence only)  
**Lane:** digest · **App:** digest

**Question it answers:** Which wall stopped people yesterday (theme search vs find-similar, editor vs browse), which themes do people try, and does anyone with PRO actually use theme search?

**Evidence:** demo.theme_search is the largest non-plumbing event (events.json 30d: 603 fires) and carries { surface, theme } (michi-maker/src/data/themeDemo.ts:21; themes are the closed 30-item set in src/data/demoThemes.ts per config/events.json:521); QUALIFIERS (events.mjs:825-844) has no entry for it, so the digest did-column prints 'Tried the theme search demo' with no theme (digest.mjs:272-273). cap.gate_shown is printed as the flat row 'Hit a plan limit x4' (digest.mjs:535-536; digest-latest.txt:23) although asks.gates already splits by limit|surface (events.mjs:762-788: 30d themeSearch@binder_editor 73 shown / 39 people, themeSearch@browse 49 / 27, findSimilar@binder_editor 73 / 39). Seam: michi 2d339f6 (2026-10-04) 'use-cap-gate stops recording an offer that was never on screen' — the `offer` prop on themeSearch@browse gates changes meaning on that date and no doc in config/events.json records it (the _themeButtonSeam at :521 covers only 2026-09-10). Real (non-demo) theme searches by PRO accounts emit nothing: CardBrowse.tsx:360-364 theme search is never locked and the browse kit search box has no emitter (binder_editor_blind, events.json:1235-1244, open).

**Proposal:** Studio: digest Money strip prints asks.gates rows 'Walls hit: theme search in the editor 2 (2 guests, offered sign-in) · find similar in the editor 2' instead of the flat eventsByName row; add 'demo.theme_search': 'theme' to QUALIFIERS so the did column reads 'Tried the theme search demo (snow, cave)'; add a `_themeWallSeam` note dated 2026-10-04 to config/events.json taxonomy. App (michi, and tcgscan when its kit gains the hook per tcgscan_search_blind): the paid theme-search path is covered by the existing open gap binder_editor_blind (`card.search` from the dock's own box with kind: 'theme'); no new name — do not re-register.

## Refuted on the way here — do not re-derive these

Each was proposed by a finder with evidence and then knocked down by at least two of three lenses. The reason is the useful part.

## Not covered, and why

1. **michi-maker product surface** — finder did not return. Everything shipped there since 2026-09-20 (first contest + winners' trophy, What's New, Discover redesign, canvas editor, undo/redo, Slice Studio, binder opening, shared binders, Theme Search, the browse box no longer handing out trials, the $2.99/$24.99 price change, test accounts excluded from aggregates) has no first-party gap analysis here. The digest lane's items 6.x touch several of them from the email's side.
2. **The completeness critic and the five work-order writers never ran** — they sit behind the verification barrier. This document is the consolidation the owner asked for in their place.
3. **michi/tcgscan symmetry** was examined from the tcgscan side only (section 4, unverified).

## What a 20-minute follow-up would close

- Re-run the three lenses on the 28 unverified/partial gaps in sections 4–6 (≈84 short agents). The finder evidence is already written; only the skeptics are missing.
- Run the michi-product lane alone with a hard tool-call budget, then verify its output the same way.
- Then register every survivor in `config/events.json` → `gaps` as `open`, and its proposed names in `taxonomy` as planned, so the studio recognises them the day an app ships them.
